dim c6, d6, e6, f6, g6, h6, i6, j6, k6, l6, m6
dim c7, d7, e7, f7, g7, h7, i7, j7, k7, l7, m7
dim c8, d8, e8, f8, g8, h8, i8, j8, k8, l8, m8, tarih1, tarih2, SF, XC8, XXC8, XXXC8
dim c9, d9, e9, f9, g9, h9, i9, j9, k9, l9, m9, toplamnm

dim total()
	dim S32E, jj, mpsno1, ab, input, output, tip, neden, dolum, netinput, netoutput, elekalti, kMe,kmb,ia

Const xlAutomatic = -4105

Sub Makro1()
	toplamnm = 0
	MlYilb = Doc.Customp_Gets("ML_YILB")
	MaliYilbasi = Right(MlYilb, 4) & "/" & Mid(MlYilb, 4,2) & "/" & Left(MlYilb, 2)

	Donem = Inputbox("Tarih Aralýðý Giriniz","Dinamo",MaliYilbasi & "-" & Doc.BuGun)
	tarih1 = mid(trim(Donem), 1, 10)
	tarih2 = mid(trim(Donem), 12, 10)

	XXC8=0
	XC8=0
	tem = 0
	tem1 = 0
	s=0
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb = Excel.Workbooks.Add	

	Set Sh1 = Wb.Sheets.Add
	Sh1.Name = "PRODUCTION SPLIT BY MACHINE"

	Sh1.Cells(1,3).Select
	Sh1.Cells(1,3).Font.Name="Arial"
	Sh1.Cells(1,3).Font.Size=12

	Sh1.Cells(1,3)= "PRODUCTION SPLIT BY MACHINE"
	excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	ReDim total(5, 13)

	Doc.CreateTableObject "ABCXYZ=SFDC32E","TMPCOPY"
	Set SF = Doc.GetTableObject("ABCXYZ") 

	
FOR i=1 TO listet.getreccount
	c5 = 0
	c6 = 0
	c7 = 0
	c8 = 0
	c9 = 0

	d5 = 0
	d6 = 0
	d7 = 0
	d8 = 0
	d9 = 0

	e5 = 0
	e6 = 0
	e7 = 0
	e8 = 0
	e9 = 0

	f5 = 0
	f6 = 0
	f7 = 0
	f8 = 0
	f9 = 0

	g5 = 0
	g6 = 0
	g7 = 0
	g8 = 0
	g9 = 0

	h5 = 0
	h6 = 0
	h7 = 0
	h8 = 0
	h9 = 0

	i5 = 0
	i6 = 0
	i7 = 0
	i8 = 0
	i9 = 0
	
	j5 = 0
	j6 = 0
	j7 = 0
	j8 = 0
	j9 = 0

	k5 = 0
	k6 = 0
	k7 = 0
	k8 = 0
	k9 = 0

	l5 = 0
	l6 = 0
	l7 = 0
	l8 = 0
	l9 = 0

	m5 = 0
	m6 = 0
	m7 = 0
	m8 = 0
	m9 = 0

'*******************
	mpsno1=""
	sf.empty
	call surehesapla(i)
	SF.sort("mpsno+notes")
for jj=1 to SF.getreccount
	Doc.StatusBarMessage "Atölye kayýtlarý okunuyor : " & LISTET.KOD(i) & " - " & jj & " / " & SF.getreccount
	ia = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"IADE",0)
	tip = SF.RECTYPE(jj)
	ab = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"LBC",0)
	neden = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"BGR",0)
	input = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"INPUT",0)
	netinput=input - Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"IADE",0)
	output = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"OUTPUT",0)	
	elekalti = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"EAA",0)
	NM = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"NM",0)
	kme = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"ME",0)	
	kmb = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"MB",0)	
	netoutput=output+elekalti
	tem = tem + Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"PP",0) + Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"PE",0)+Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"HDPE",0) + Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"KIRMA",0)
	tem1 = tem1 + Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"PP",0) + Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"PE",0)+Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"HDPE",0) 
	farkg=doc.DTDTDif1(SF.RECTARIH1(jj),SF.RECTIME1(jj), SF.RECTARIH2(jj),SF.RECTIME2(jj))
	sure = sf.SURE(jj)
	toplamnm = toplamnm + nm
	L5 = l5 + kme - kmb

		Dim Kriter
		Kriter=""
		Kriter=Kriter&"MPSNO='"&mpsno1 &"'"
		Kriter=Kriter&" & "
		Kriter=Kriter&"RECTARIH1<='"&tarih2 &"'"
		sab1 = 0 
		Set S322=Doc.Select1("___tABLESFDC","SFDC32E",Kriter,"",-1)

		QStr = "MPSNO='" & mpsno1 & "'&TARIH>='" & tarih1 & "'&TARIH<='" & tarih2 & "'"
		Set s20t=Doc.Select1("___tABLE10zzzzz","STOK20T", QStr,"",-1)

		for b=1 to S322.getreccount
			ab1 = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",S322.EVRAKNO(b),"LBC",0)
			if trim(ab1)="A" then
				sab1=1
			end if	
		next	
if trim(SF.RECTYPE(jj))<>"D" and trim(SF.RECTYPE(jj))<>"P" then
	
		if trim(SF.MPSNO(jj))<>trim(mpsno1) then

			mpsno1 = SF.MPSNO(jj)

		select case trim(ab)
			case "A" 
					F5 = f5 + input-ia
					H5 = h5 +netoutput
					C5 = C5+1
					sirano = sirano+1
					Sh1.Cells(sirano+95,2)= Mpsno1
					Sh1.Cells(sirano+95,3)= input-ia
					Sh1.Cells(sirano+95,4)= C5

	
			case "B"
				if sab1 = 0 and s20t.getreccount > 0 then
					C5 = C5+1
					F5 = f5 + input-ia
					H5 = h5 +netoutput
					sirano = sirano+1
					Sh1.Cells(sirano+95,2)= Mpsno1
					Sh1.Cells(sirano+95,3)= input-ia

						Sh1.Cells(sirano+95,4)= C5

				end if
			end select
		end if
		select case trim(ab)
			case "B"
				if trim(neden)="RTF" or trim(neden)="RFT" then
					C7 = C7 + 1
					F7 = F7 + netinput	
					H7 = H7 + netoutput	
					L7 = L7 + (kme - kmb)	
				else
					C9 = C9 + 1
					F9 = F9 + netinput	
					H9 = H9 + netoutput	
					L9 = L9 + (kme - kmb)	
				end if
			case "C" 
		
		end select
END if

	'****************************
		
		if trim(tip)="D" then
				C8 = C8 + 1
				F8 = F8 + netinput	
				H8 = H8 + netoutput	
				L8 = L8 + (kme - kmb)
		
		end if
		if trim(tip)="P" then
			XC8 = XC8 + 1
			XXC8 = XXC8 + netoutput
			XXXC8 = XXXC8 + (kme - kmb)

		end if

'******
	select case trim(ab)
		case "B" 
			if trim(neden)="RTF" then
			
				
			end if
		case "C" 
		
		end select
'End If
Next
'/////////////////////////////////
C6 = C7 + C8
C5= C5 + C6
D5 = 100
E5 = DOC.DIV(H5, C5)

G5 = 100
I5 = 100
J5 = F5 - H5
K5 = DOC.DIV(J5, F5)*100
M5 = DOC.DIV(H5, L5)
D6 = DOC.DIV(C6, C5)*100
F6 = F7 + F8
G6 = DOC.DIV(F6, F5)*100
H6 = H7 + H8
E6 = DOC.DIV(H6, C6)

I6 = DOC.DIV(H6, H5)*100
J6 = J7 + J8
K6 = DOC.DIV(J6, F6)*100
L6 = L7 + L8
M6 = DOC.DIV(H6, L6)
'******************
D7 = DOC.DIV(C7, C5)*100
E7 = DOC.DIV(H7, C7)
G7 = DOC.DIV(F7, F5)*100
I7 = DOC.DIV(H7, H5)*100
J7 = F7 - H7
K7 = DOC.DIV(J7, F7)*100
M7 = DOC.DIV(H7, L7)
'*****************
D8 = DOC.DIV(C8, C5)*100
E8 = DOC.DIV(H8, C8)
G8 = DOC.DIV(F8, F5)*100
I8 = DOC.DIV(H8, H5)*100
J8 = F8 - H8
K8 = DOC.DIV(J8, F8)*100
M8 = DOC.DIV(H8, L8)
'*********************
D9 = DOC.DIV(C9, C5)*100
E9 = DOC.DIV(H9, C9)
G9 = DOC.DIV(F9, F5)*100
I9 = DOC.DIV(H9, H5)*100
J9 = F9 - H9
K9 = DOC.DIV(J9, F9)*100
M9 = DOC.DIV(H9, L9)

'*********************
	call excele_yaz(i)


'*******************************
	total(0, 0) = "TOTAL "
	total(1, 0) = "PRODUCTION OF FINISHED GOODS "
	total(2, 0) = "Total Rework (Total B Grade but transformed to A)"
	total(3, 0) = "Rework With  Material"
	total(4, 0) = "Rework  scheduled"
	total(5, 0) = "Rework  Unscheduled "

	total(0, 1) = "NR OF LOTS"
	
	total(1, 1) = total(1,1) + c5
	total(3, 1) = total(3,1) + c7
	total(4, 1) = total(4,1) + c8
	total(5, 1) = total(5,1) + c9
	total(2, 1) = total(3,1) + total(4,1)

	total(0, 2) = "LOT %"
	total(1, 2) = "100%"
	total(2, 2) = doc.div(total(2,1), total(1,1))
	total(3, 2) = doc.div(total(3,1), total(1,1))
	total(4, 2) = doc.div(total(4,1), total(1,1))
	total(5, 2) = doc.div(total(5,1), total(1,1))

	total(0, 6) = "OUTPUT KG "
	total(1, 6) = total(1,6) + h5
	total(3, 6) = total(3,6) + h7
	total(4, 6) = total(4,6) + h8
	total(5, 6) = total(5,6) + h9

	total(2, 6) = total(3,6) + total(4,6)+ total(5,6)


	total(0, 3) = "AVERAGE LOTSIZE"
	total(1, 3) = doc.div(total(1,6), total(1,1))
	total(2, 3) = doc.div(total(2,6), total(2,1))
	total(3, 3) = doc.div(total(3,6), total(3,1))
	total(4, 3) = doc.div(total(4,6), total(4,1))
	total(5, 3) = doc.div(total(5,6), total(5,1))

	total(0, 4) = "INPUT KG"
	total(1, 4) = total(1,4) + f5
	total(3, 4) = total(3,4) + f7
	total(4, 4) = total(4,4) + f8
	total(5, 4) = total(5,4) + f9

	total(2, 4) = total(3,4) + total(4,4)+ total(5,4)


	total(0, 5) = "INPUT %"
	total(1, 5) = "100"
	total(2, 5) = doc.div(total(2,4), total(1,4))
	total(3, 5) = doc.div(total(3,4), total(1,4))
	total(4, 5) = doc.div(total(4,4), total(1,4))
	total(5, 5) = doc.div(total(5,4), total(1,4))



	total(0, 7) = "OUTPUT %"
	total(1, 7) = "100"
	total(2, 7) = doc.div(total(2,6), total(1,6))
	total(3, 7) = doc.div(total(3,6), total(1,6))
	total(4, 7) = doc.div(total(4,6), total(1,6))
	total(5, 7) = doc.div(total(5,6), total(1,6))


	total(0, 8) = "IN / OUT DIFF. KG"
	total(1, 8) = total(1,8) + j5
	total(3, 8) = total(3,8) + j7
	total(4, 8) = total(4,8) + j8
	total(5, 8) = total(5,8) + j9

	total(2, 8) = total(3,8) + total(4,8)+ total(5,8)



	total(0, 9) = "DIFF. %"
	total(1, 9) = doc.div(total(1,8), total(1,4))
	total(2, 9) = doc.div(total(2,8), total(2,4))
	total(3, 9) = doc.div(total(3,8), total(3,4))
	total(4, 9) = doc.div(total(4,8), total(4,4))
	total(5, 9) = doc.div(total(5,8), total(5,4))

	total(0, 10) = "HOURS"
	total(1, 10) = total(1,10) + l5
	total(3, 10) = total(3,10) + l7
	total(4, 10) = total(4,10) + l8
	total(5, 10) = total(5,10) + l9

	total(2, 10) = total(3,10) + total(4,10)+ total(5,10)



	total(0, 11) = "KG / HR"
	total(1, 11) = doc.div(total(1,6), total(1,10))
	total(2, 11) = doc.div(total(2,6), total(2,10))
	total(3, 11) = doc.div(total(3,6), total(3,10))
	total(4, 11) = doc.div(total(4,6), total(4,10))
	total(5, 11) = doc.div(total(5,6), total(5,10))

'----------------------
next
	s=s+5
	Sh1.Cells(s,2).Select
	excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
		

	for xx=0 to 5
		s=s+1
		for z=0 to 11
			Sh1.Cells(s,2).Select
			excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
			if xx=0 then
				Sh1.Cells(s,z+2).Select
				excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
			end if

			if z=0 or xx=0 then
				Sh1.Cells(s,z+2)= total(xx, z)
			elseif z = 9 then
				Sh1.Cells(s,z+2)= doc.numformat(total(xx, z)*100, 10,3)

			else
				Sh1.Cells(s,z+2)= doc.numformat(total(xx, z), 10,2)
			end if
		Next
	next
	Call Excele_yaz2()
	Excel.Visible = True

End Sub
Sub Excele_yaz(satirno)

	Sh1.Cells(2,5).Select
	Sh1.Columns(2).ColumnWidth = 45

	s=s+5
	
	for c=2 to 13
		Sh1.Cells(s,c).Select
		excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	next
	Sh1.Cells(s,2)= LISTET.KOD(satirno)
	Sh1.Cells(s,3)= "NR OF LOTS"
	Sh1.Cells(s,4)= "LOT %"
	Sh1.Cells(s,5)= "AVERAGE LOT SIZE"
	Sh1.Cells(s,6)= "INPUT KG"
	Sh1.Cells(s,7)= "INPUT %"
	Sh1.Cells(s,8)= "OUTPUT KG "
	Sh1.Cells(s,9)= "OUTPUT %"
	Sh1.Cells(s,10)= "IN / OUT DIFF. KG"
	Sh1.Cells(s,11)= "DIFF. %"
	Sh1.Cells(s,12)= "HOURS"
	Sh1.Cells(s,13)= "KG / HR"


	s=s+1
	Sh1.Cells(s,2)= "PRODUCTION OF FINISHED GOODS "
	Sh1.Cells(s,3)= doc.numformat(c5, 10,2)
	Sh1.Cells(s,4)= doc.numformat(d5, 10,2)
	Sh1.Cells(s,5)= doc.numformat(e5, 10,2)
	Sh1.Cells(s,6)= doc.numformat(f5, 10,2)
	Sh1.Cells(s,7)= doc.numformat(g5, 10,2)
	Sh1.Cells(s,8)= doc.numformat(h5, 10,2)
	Sh1.Cells(s,9)= doc.numformat(i5, 10,2)
	Sh1.Cells(s,10)= doc.numformat(j5, 10,2)
	Sh1.Cells(s,11)= doc.numformat(k5, 10,3)
	Sh1.Cells(s,12)= doc.numformat(l5, 10,2)
	Sh1.Cells(s,13)= doc.numformat(m5, 10,2)
' ***
	s=s+1

	Sh1.Cells(s,4)= doc.numformat(d6, 10,2)

	Sh1.Cells(s,11)= doc.numformat(k6, 10,3)

	Sh1.Cells(s,13)= doc.numformat(m6, 10,2)
' ***
	s=s+1
	Sh1.Cells(s,2)= "Rework With  Material"
	Sh1.Cells(s,3)= doc.numformat(c7, 10,2)
	Sh1.Cells(s,4)= doc.numformat(d7, 10,2)

	Sh1.Cells(s,6)= doc.numformat(f7, 10,2)
	Sh1.Cells(s,7)= doc.numformat(g7, 10,2)
	Sh1.Cells(s,8)= doc.numformat(h7, 10,2)
	Sh1.Cells(s,9)= doc.numformat(i7, 10,2)
	Sh1.Cells(s,10)= doc.numformat(j7, 10,2)
	Sh1.Cells(s,11)= doc.numformat(k7, 10,3)
	Sh1.Cells(s,12)= doc.numformat(l7, 10,2)
	Sh1.Cells(s,13)= doc.numformat(m7, 10,2)
' ***
	s=s+1
	Sh1.Cells(s,2)= "Rework  scheduled"
	Sh1.Cells(s,3)= doc.numformat(c8, 10,2)
	Sh1.Cells(s,4)= doc.numformat(d8, 10,2)
	Sh1.Cells(s,5)= doc.numformat(e8, 10,2)
	Sh1.Cells(s,6)= doc.numformat(f8, 10,2)
	Sh1.Cells(s,7)= doc.numformat(g8, 10,2)
	Sh1.Cells(s,8)= doc.numformat(h8, 10,2)
	Sh1.Cells(s,9)= doc.numformat(i8, 10,2)

	Sh1.Cells(s,12)= doc.numformat(l8, 10,2)
	Sh1.Cells(s,13)= doc.numformat(m8, 10,2)

Sub Excele_yaz2()
	Set Sh2 = Wb.Sheets.Add
	Sh2.Name = "TOTAL PROD+REWORK+PREMIX"

	

	Sh2.Range("C2:C4").Select
	
'	excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True

	Sh2.Cells(2,2)= "TOTAL PRODUCTION , REWORK & PREMIX"

	Sh2.Cells(5,2)= "MONTHLY PRODUCTION"
	Sh2.Cells(5,3)= "NR OF LOTS"
	Sh2.Cells(5,4)= "LOT %"
	Sh2.Cells(5,5)= "AVR. LOT SIZE"
	Sh2.Cells(5,6)= "INPUT KG"
	Sh2.Cells(5,7)= "INPUT %"
	Sh2.Cells(5,8)= "OUTPUT KG "
	Sh2.Cells(5,9)= "OUTPUT %"
	Sh2.Cells(5,10)= "IN / OUT DIFF. KG"
	Sh2.Cells(5,11)= "DIFF. %"
	Sh2.Cells(5,12)= "HOURS"
	Sh2.Cells(5,13)= "KG / HR"

	Sh2.Cells(7,2)= "PRODUCTION OF  FINISHED GOODS "
	Sh2.Cells(9,2)= "TOTAL REWORK (Total B Grade but transformed to A)"
	Sh2.Cells(10,2)= "Rework With  Material"
	Sh2.Cells(11,2)= "Rework  Unscheduled "
	Sh2.Cells(13,2)= "PREMIX"
	Sh2.Cells(13,3)= "NR OF PREMIX"
	Sh2.Cells(13,5)= "KG"
	Sh2.Cells(13,6)= "KG Percentage to A Grade  Finished Goods Production ( TO CELL H7 )"
	Sh2.Cells(13,12)= "HOURS"
	Sh2.Cells(13,13)= "KG / HR"

	Sh2.Cells(14,2)= "Total Premix"

	Sh2.Cells(17,2)= "REWORK PREMIX"
	Sh2.Cells(18,2)= "Total of Rework & Premix"
	Sh2.Cells(17,3)= "NR OF LOTS"
	Sh2.Cells(17,4)= "KG"
	Sh2.Cells(17,6)= "KG Percentage to A Grade  Finished Goods Production ( TO CELL H7 )"
	Sh2.Cells(17,6)= "KG Percentage to A Grade  Finished Goods Production ( TO CELL H7 )"


	Sh2.Cells(22,2)= " KG DIFFERENCE BETWEEN INPUT&OUTPUT (Cell J7 )"
	Sh2.Cells(24,2)= " KG Given to Labo from Prod. as Std. Samples of A Grade Finished Goods / Lot  (first time)"

Sub SureHesapla(satirn)
	Set LISTEG = Doc.Select1("___tABLE10XXCCX","SFDC32E","X_T_ISMERKEZI='" & LISTET.KOD(satirn) & "'","",-1)
	for tt=1 to LISTEG.GETRECCOUNT
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Süreler Hesaplanýyor : " & trim(LISTET.KOD(satirn)) & " - " & tt & " / " & LISTEG.GETRECCOUNT
		mpsno = LISTEG.MPSNO(tt)
		lbc = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",LISTEG.EVRAKNO(tt),"LBC",0)

		Kriter=""
		Kriter=Kriter&"MPSNO='"&mpsno &"'"
		Kriter=Kriter&" & "
		Kriter=Kriter&"TARIH >='"&tarih1 &"'"
		Kriter=Kriter&" & "
		Kriter=Kriter&"TARIH <='"&tarih2&"'"

		Set s70t=Doc.Select1("___tABLE10","STOK20T", kriter,"",-1)
		if trim(lbc) = "A" then
			if s70t.getreccount > 0 then
				SF.ADDROW
				s1=sf.getreccount
				sf.evrakno(s1) = LISTEG.evrakno(tt)
				sf.MPSNO(s1) = LISTEG.MPSNO(tt)
				sf.RECTYPE(s1) = LISTEG.RECTYPE(tt)
				sf.X_T_ISMERKEZI(s1) = LISTEG.X_T_ISMERKEZI(tt)
				sf.rectarih1(s1) = LISTEG.RECTARIH1(tt)
				sf.rectime1(s1) = LISTEG.RECTIME1(tt)
				sf.rectarih2(s1) = LISTEG.RECTARIH2(tt)
				sf.rectime2(s1) = LISTEG.RECTIME2(tt)
				sf.NOTES(s1) = trim(lbc)
			End If
		else
			if LISTEG.RECTARIH1(tt)>= tarih1 and LISTEG.RECTARIH2(tt)<= tarih2 then
				SF.ADDROW
				s1=sf.getreccount
				sf.evrakno(s1) = LISTEG.evrakno(tt)
				sf.MPSNO(s1) = LISTEG.MPSNO(tt)
				sf.RECTYPE(s1) = LISTEG.RECTYPE(tt)
				sf.X_T_ISMERKEZI(s1) = LISTEG.X_T_ISMERKEZI(tt)
				sf.rectarih1(s1) = LISTEG.RECTARIH1(tt)
				sf.rectime1(s1) = LISTEG.RECTIME1(tt)
				sf.rectarih2(s1) = LISTEG.RECTARIH2(tt)
				sf.rectime2(s1) = LISTEG.RECTIME2(tt)
				sf.NOTES(s1) = trim(lbc)
			end if
		end if
	Next
End Sub
Sub KutuCiz(aralik)
		Sh2.Range(aralik).Select
    excel.Selection.Borders(xlDiagonalDown).LineStyle = xlNone
    excel.Selection.Borders(xlDiagonalUp).LineStyle = xlNone
    
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeLeft).LineStyle = xlContinuous
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeLeft).Weight = xlThin
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeLeft).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic

		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeTop).LineStyle = xlContinuous
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeTop).Weight = xlThin
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeTop).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic

		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeBottom).LineStyle = xlContinuous
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeBottom).Weight = xlThin
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeBottom).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic

    	excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeRight).LineStyle = xlContinuous
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeRight).Weight = xlThin
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeRight).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
   
    excel.Selection.Borders(xlInsideVertical).LineStyle = xlNone
End Sub
Sub BaslikAyarla(aralik)
		Sh2.Range(aralik).Select
		excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
		excel.Selection.Font.Name = "Arial Tur"
		excel.Selection.Font.Size = 12
		excel.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = xlCenterAcrossSelection
		excel.Selection.VerticalAlignment = xlBottom
		excel.Selection.WrapText = False
     excel.Selection.Orientation = 0
		excel.Selection.AddIndent = False
		excel.Selection.IndentLevel = 0
		excel.Selection.ShrinkToFit = False
		excel.Selection.ReadingOrder = xlContext
		excel.Selection.MergeCells = False    
    
End Sub
Sub KutuCiz1(aralik)
		Sh2.Range(aralik).Select
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlDiagonalDown).LineStyle = xlNone
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlDiagonalUp).LineStyle = xlNone
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeLeft).LineStyle = xlContinuous
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeLeft).Weight = xlThin
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeLeft).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeTop).LineStyle = xlContinuous
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeTop).Weight = xlThin
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeTop).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeBottom).LineStyle = xlContinuous
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeBottom).Weight = xlThin
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeBottom).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeRight).LineStyle = xlContinuous
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeRight).Weight = xlThin
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeRight).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlInsideVertical).LineStyle = xlContinuous
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlInsideVertical).Weight = xlThin
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlInsideVertical).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlInsideHorizontal).LineStyle = xlContinuous
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlInsideHorizontal).Weight = xlThin
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlInsideHorizontal).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
End Sub
Sub Belirle(aralik)
'************************
		Sh2.Range(aralik).Select
		excel.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = xlGeneral
		excel.Selection.VerticalAlignment = xlBottom
		excel.Selection.WrapText = True
		excel.Selection.Orientation = 0
		excel.Selection.AddIndent = False
		excel.Selection.IndentLevel = 0
		excel.Selection.ShrinkToFit = False
		excel.Selection.ReadingOrder = xlContext
		excel.Selection.MergeCells = True
End Sub
Sub SutunGenisligi(aralik, deger)
	sh2.Columns(aralik).Select
	excel.Selection.ColumnWidth = deger

End Sub
Sub Boya(aralik, renk)
  Sh2.Range(aralik).Select
  excel.Selection.Interior.ColorIndex = renk
	excel.Selection.Interior.Pattern = xlSolid
End Sub
Sub Satiryuksekligi(aralik, deger)
  Sh2.Rows(aralik).Select
	excel.Selection.RowHeight = deger
End Sub

dim s, wb, excel, sh1, sh2, c5, d5, e5, f5, g5, h5, i5, j5, k5, l5, m5, tem, tem1, sh3

Sub Makro1()
	Donem = Inputbox("Tarih Aralýðý Giriniz","Dinamo","2009/10/01-2009/10/31")
	tarih1 = mid(trim(Donem), 1, 10)
	tarih2 = mid(trim(Donem), 12, 10)
	XXC8=0
	XC8=0
	tem = 0
	tem1 = 0
	s=0
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb = Excel.Workbooks.Add	

	Set Sh1 = Wb.Sheets.Add
	Sh1.Name = "PRODUCTION SPLIT BY MACHINE"

	Sh1.Cells(1,3).Select
	Sh1.Cells(1,3).Font.Name="Arial"
	Sh1.Cells(1,3).Font.Size=12

	Sh1.Cells(1,3)= "PRODUCTION SPLIT BY MACHINE"
	excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	ReDim total(5, 13)

	Doc.CreateTableObject "ABCXYZ=SFDC32E","TMPCOPY"
	Set SF = Doc.GetTableObject("ABCXYZ") 

	
FOR i=1 TO listet.getreccount
	c5 = 0

	g9 = 0

	h5 = 0

	h7 = 0
	h8 = 0

	i5 = 0

	i7 = 0
	i8 = 0
	i9 = 0
	
	j5 = 0
	j6 = 0
	j7 = 0
	j8 = 0
	j9 = 0

	k5 = 0
	k6 = 0
	k7 = 0
	k8 = 0
	k9 = 0

	l5 = 0
	l6 = 0
	l7 = 0
	l8 = 0
	l9 = 0

	m5 = 0
	m6 = 0
	m7 = 0
	m8 = 0
	m9 = 0

'*******************
	mpsno1=""
	sf.empty
	call surehesapla(i)
	SF.sort("mpsno+notes")
for jj=1 to SF.getreccount
if trim(SF.RECTYPE(jj))<>"D" and trim(SF.RECTYPE(jj))<>"P" then
	ia = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"IADE",0)
	tip = SF.RECTYPE(jj)
	ab = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"LBC",0)
	neden = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"BGR",0)
	input = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"INPUT",0)
	netinput=input - Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"IADE",0)
	output = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"OUTPUT",0)	
	elekalti = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"EAA",0)
	NM = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"NM",0)
	kme = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"ME",0)	
	kmb = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"MB",0)	
	netoutput=output+elekalti
	tem = tem + Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"PP",0) + Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"PE",0)+Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"HDPE",0) + Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"KIRMA",0)
	tem1 = tem1 + Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"PP",0) + Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"PE",0)+Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"HDPE",0) 
	farkg=doc.DTDTDif1(SF.RECTARIH1(jj),SF.RECTIME1(jj), SF.RECTARIH2(jj),SF.RECTIME2(jj))
	sure = sf.SURE(jj)

	L5 = l5 + kme - kmb

	if trim(SF.MPSNO(jj))<>trim(mpsno1) then

		mpsno1 = SF.MPSNO(jj)
		Dim Kriter
		Kriter=""
		Kriter=Kriter&"MPSNO='"&mpsno1 &"'"
		Kriter=Kriter&" & "
		Kriter=Kriter&"RECTARIH1<='"&tarih2 &"'"
		sab1 = 0 
		Set S322=Doc.Select1("___tABLESFDC","SFDC32E",Kriter,"",-1)
		Krit=""
		Krit=Krit&"MPSNO='"&mpsno1 &"'"
		Krit=Krit&" & "
		Krit=Krit&"TARIH >='"&tarih1 &"'"
		Krit=Krit&" & "
		Krit=Krit&"TARIH <='"&tarih2&"'"

	Set s20t=Doc.Select1("___tABLE10zzzzz","STOK20T", krit,"",-1)

		for b=1 to S322.getreccount
			ab1 = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",S322.EVRAKNO(b),"LBC",0)
			if trim(ab1)="A" then
				sab1=1
			end if	
		next	 	
	
		select case trim(ab)
			case "A" 
					F5 = f5 + input-ia
					sirano = sirano+1
					Sh1.Cells(sirano+95,2)= Mpsno1
					Sh1.Cells(sirano+95,3)= input-ia

					H5 = h5 +netoutput
					C5 = C5+1
			case "B"
				if sab1 = 0 and s20t.getreccount > 0 then
					C5 = C5+1
					F5 = f5 + input-ia
					H5 = h5 +netoutput
					sirano = sirano+1

				Sh1.Cells(sirano+95,2)= Mpsno1
					Sh1.Cells(sirano+95,3)= input-ia

				end if
				if trim(neden)="RTF" then
					C7 = C7 + 1	
				end if
			case "C" 
		
				C8 = C8 + 1
				F8 = F8 + netinput	
				H8 = H8 + netoutput	
				L8 = L8 + (kme - kmb)	
		end select
	'****************************
		if trim(tip)="R" then
				C9 = C9 + 1
				F9 = F9 + netinput	
				H9 = H9 + netoutput	
				L9 = L9 + (kme - kmb)	

		end if
		if trim(tip)="P" then
			XC8 = XC8 + 1
			XXC8 = XXC8 + netoutput

		end if

	END if
'******
	select case trim(ab)
		case "B" 
			if trim(neden)="RTF" then
				F7 = F7 + netinput	
				H7 = H7 + netoutput	
				L7 = L7 + (kme - kmb)	
				
			end if
		case "C" 
		
		end select
End If
Next
'/////////////////////////////////
C6 = C7 + C8
C5= C5 + C6
D5 = 100
E5 = DOC.DIV(H5, C5)

G5 = 100
I5 = 100
J5 = F5 - H5
K5 = DOC.DIV(J5, F5)*100
M5 = DOC.DIV(H5, L5)
D6 = DOC.DIV(C6, C5)*100
E6 = DOC.DIV(H6, C6)
F6 = F7 + F8
G6 = DOC.DIV(F6, F5)*100
H6 = H7 + H8
I6 = DOC.DIV(H6, H5)*100
J6 = J7 + J8
K6 = DOC.DIV(J6, F6)*100
L6 = L7 + L8
M6 = DOC.DIV(H6, L6)
'******************
D7 = DOC.DIV(C7, C5)*100
E7 = DOC.DIV(H7, C7)
G7 = DOC.DIV(F7, F5)*100
I7 = DOC.DIV(H7, H5)*100
J7 = F7 - H7
K7 = DOC.DIV(J7, F7)*100
M7 = DOC.DIV(H7, L7)
'*****************
D8 = DOC.DIV(C8, C5)*100
E8 = DOC.DIV(H8, C8)
G8 = DOC.DIV(F8, F5)*100
I8 = DOC.DIV(H8, H5)*100
J8 = F8 - H8
K8 = DOC.DIV(J8, F8)*100
M8 = DOC.DIV(H8, L8)
'*********************
D9 = DOC.DIV(C9, C5)*100
E9 = DOC.DIV(H9, C9)
G9 = DOC.DIV(F9, F5)*100
I9 = DOC.DIV(H9, H5)*100
J9 = F9 - H9
K9 = DOC.DIV(J9, F9)*100
M9 = DOC.DIV(H9, L9)

'*********************
	call excele_yaz(i)


'*******************************
	total(0, 0) = "TOTAL "
	total(1, 0) = "PRODUCTION OF FINISHED GOODS "
	total(2, 0) = "Total Rework (Total B Grade but transformed to A)"
	total(3, 0) = "Rework With  Material"
	total(4, 0) = "Rework With  Material"
	total(5, 0) = "Rework  Unscheduled "

	total(0, 1) = "NR OF LOTS"
	
	total(1, 1) = total(1,1) + c5
	total(3, 1) = total(3,1) + c7
	total(4, 1) = total(4,1) + c8
	total(5, 1) = total(5,1) + c9
	total(2, 1) = total(3,1) + total(4,1)

	total(0, 2) = "LOT %"
	total(1, 2) = "100%"
	total(2, 2) = doc.div(total(2,1), total(1,1))
	total(3, 2) = doc.div(total(3,1), total(1,1))
	total(4, 2) = doc.div(total(4,1), total(1,1))
	total(5, 2) = doc.div(total(5,1), total(1,1))

	total(0, 6) = "OUTPUT KG "
	total(1, 6) = total(1,6) + h5
	total(3, 6) = total(3,6) + h7
	total(4, 6) = total(4,6) + h8
	total(5, 6) = total(5,6) + h9

	total(2, 6) = total(3,6) + total(4,6)+ total(5,6)


	total(0, 3) = "AVERAGE LOTSIZE"
	total(1, 3) = doc.div(total(1,6), total(1,1))
	total(2, 3) = doc.div(total(2,6), total(2,1))
	total(3, 3) = doc.div(total(3,6), total(3,1))
	total(4, 3) = doc.div(total(4,6), total(4,1))
	total(5, 3) = doc.div(total(5,6), total(5,1))

	total(0, 4) = "INPUT KG"
	total(1, 4) = total(1,4) + f5
	total(3, 4) = total(3,4) + f7
	total(4, 4) = total(4,4) + f8
	total(5, 4) = total(5,4) + f9

	total(2, 4) = total(3,4) + total(4,4)+ total(5,4)


	total(0, 5) = "INPUT %"
	total(1, 5) = "100"
	total(2, 5) = doc.div(total(2,4), total(1,4))
	total(3, 5) = doc.div(total(3,4), total(1,4))
	total(4, 5) = doc.div(total(4,4), total(1,4))
	total(5, 5) = doc.div(total(5,4), total(1,4))



	total(0, 7) = "OUTPUT %"
	total(1, 7) = "100"
	total(2, 7) = doc.div(total(2,6), total(1,6))
	total(3, 7) = doc.div(total(3,6), total(1,6))
	total(4, 7) = doc.div(total(4,6), total(1,6))
	total(5, 7) = doc.div(total(5,6), total(1,6))


	total(0, 8) = "IN / OUT DIFF. KG"
	total(1, 8) = total(1,8) + j5
	total(3, 8) = total(3,8) + j7
	total(4, 8) = total(4,8) + j8
	total(5, 8) = total(5,8) + j9

	total(2, 8) = total(3,8) + total(4,8)+ total(5,8)



	total(0, 9) = "DIFF. %"
	total(1, 9) = doc.div(total(1,8), total(1,4))
	total(2, 9) = doc.div(total(2,8), total(2,4))
	total(3, 9) = doc.div(total(3,8), total(3,4))
	total(4, 9) = doc.div(total(4,8), total(4,4))
	total(5, 9) = doc.div(total(5,8), total(5,4))

	total(0, 10) = "HOURS"
	total(1, 10) = total(1,10) + l5
	total(3, 10) = total(3,10) + l7
	total(4, 10) = total(4,10) + l8
	total(4, 10) = total(5,10) + l9

	total(2, 10) = total(3,10) + total(4,10)+ total(5,10)



	total(0, 11) = "KG / HR"
	total(1, 11) = doc.div(total(1,6), total(1,10))
	total(2, 11) = doc.div(total(2,6), total(2,10))
	total(3, 11) = doc.div(total(3,6), total(3,10))
	total(4, 11) = doc.div(total(4,6), total(4,10))
	total(5, 11) = doc.div(total(5,6), total(5,10))

'----------------------
next
	s=s+5
	Sh1.Cells(s,2).Select
	excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
		

	for xx=0 to 5
		s=s+1
		for z=0 to 11
			Sh1.Cells(s,2).Select
			excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
			if xx=0 then
				Sh1.Cells(s,z+2).Select
				excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True

			if z=0 or xx=0 then
				Sh1.Cells(s,z+2)= total(xx, z)
			else
				Sh1.Cells(s,z+2)= doc.numformat(total(xx, z), 10,2)
			end if
		Next
	next
	Call Excele_yaz2()
	Excel.Visible = True

End Sub
Sub Excele_yaz(satirno)

		Sh1.Cells(s,c).Select

	next

	Sh2.Cells(9,6)=  doc.numformat((Sh2.Cells(10,6)+sh2.Cells(11,6)),12,2)
	Sh2.Cells(9,7)=  doc.numformat(doc.div(Sh2.Cells(9,6),Sh2.Cells(7,8))*100,12,2)
	Sh2.Cells(9,8)=  doc.numformat((Sh2.Cells(10,8)+Sh2.Cells(11,8)),12,2)
	Sh2.Cells(9,10)=  doc.numformat((Sh2.Cells(10,10)+Sh2.Cells(11,10)),12,2)
	Sh2.Cells(9,11)=  doc.numformat(doc.div(Sh2.Cells(9,10),Sh2.Cells(9,6))*100,12,2)&"%"
	Sh2.Cells(9,12)=  doc.numformat((Sh2.Cells(10,12)+Sh2.Cells(11,12)),12,2)
	Sh2.Cells(9,13)=  doc.numformat(doc.div(Sh2.Cells(9,8),Sh2.Cells(9,12)),12,2)


	Sh2.Cells(14,3)=  XC8
	Sh2.Cells(14,5)=  "5808"
	Sh2.Cells(14,6)=  doc.numformat(DOC.Div(Sh2.Cells(14,5), Total(1,6))*100,12,2)
	Sh2.Cells(14,12)=  "90,36"

	Sh2.Cells(14,13)=  doc.numformat(doc.div(Sh2.Cells(14,5), Sh2.Cells(14,12)),12,2)


	Sh2.Cells(18,3)=  doc.numformat((Sh2.Cells(14,3) + Sh2.Cells(9,3)),12,2)
	Sh2.Cells(18,4)=  doc.numformat((Sh2.Cells(9,6) + Sh2.Cells(14,5)),12,2)
	Sh2.Cells(18,6)=  doc.numformat(doc.div(Sh2.Cells(18,4), Sh2.Cells(7,8))*100,12,2)

	Sh2.Cells(22,6)=  Sh2.Cells(7,10)

	Sh2.Cells(26,6)=  Doc.numformat((doc.div(Sh2.Cells(7,6),25)*0.02),12,2)
	Sh2.Cells(27,6)=  doc.numformat((Sh2.Cells(24,6) + Sh2.Cells(25,6) + Sh2.Cells(26,6)),12,2)
	Sh2.Cells(29,6)=  doc.numformat((Sh2.Cells(22,6) - Sh2.Cells(27,6)),12,2)
	Sh2.Cells(29,7)=  doc.numformat((doc.div(Sh2.Cells(29,6), Sh2.Cells(7,6)))*100,12,4)&"%"

	Sh2.Cells(33,4)=  doc.numformat(tem,12,2)
	Sh2.Cells(36,3)=  doc.numformat(Sh2.Cells(7,3),12,2)
	Sh2.Cells(37,3)=  doc.numformat((Sh2.Cells(7,3)+Sh2.Cells(9,3)),12,2)
	Sh2.Cells(38,3)=  doc.numformat((Sh2.Cells(7,3)+Sh2.Cells(9,3)+Sh2.Cells(14,3)),12,2)

	Sh2.Cells(36,4)=  doc.numformat(doc.div(Sh2.Cells(33,4),Sh2.Cells(36,3)),12,2)
	Sh2.Cells(37,4)=  doc.numformat(doc.div(Sh2.Cells(33,4),Sh2.Cells(37,3)),12,2)
	Sh2.Cells(38,4)=  doc.numformat(doc.div(Sh2.Cells(33,4),Sh2.Cells(38,3)),12,2)

	Sh2.Cells(40,4)=  doc.numformat(tem1,12,2)
	Sh2.Cells(43,3)=  doc.numformat(Sh2.Cells(7,3),12,2)
	Sh2.Cells(44,3)=  doc.numformat((Sh2.Cells(7,3)+Sh2.Cells(9,3)),12,2)
	Sh2.Cells(45,3)=  doc.numformat((Sh2.Cells(7,3)+Sh2.Cells(9,3)+Sh2.Cells(14,3)),12,2)

	Sh2.Cells(43,4)=  doc.numformat(doc.div(Sh2.Cells(40,4),Sh2.Cells(43,3)),12,2)
	Sh2.Cells(44,4)=  doc.numformat(doc.div(Sh2.Cells(40,4),Sh2.Cells(44,3)),12,2)
	Sh2.Cells(45,4)=  doc.numformat(doc.div(Sh2.Cells(40,4),Sh2.Cells(45,3)),12,2)
	Sh2.Cells(50, 3)= Sh2.Cells(7,8)
	Sh2.Cells(51, 3)= DOC.DIV(Sh2.Cells(49,3), Sh2.Cells(50,3))*1000

	Call KutuCiz("B2:M2")
	'Call BaslikAyarla("B2:M2")
	Call KutuCiz1("B5:M11")
	Call Belirle("F13:K13")
	Call Belirle("F14:K14")
	Call KutuCiz1("B13:M14")
	Call Belirle("F17:M17")
	Call Belirle("F18:M18")
	Call KutuCiz1("B17:M18")
	Call KutuCiz("B22:F22")
	Call KutuCiz("B24:F26")
	Call KutuCiz("B29:F29")
	Call KutuCiz("B33:B33")
	Call KutuCiz("D33:D33")
	Call KutuCiz1("B35:D38")
	Call KutuCiz("B40:B40")
	Call KutuCiz("D40:D40")
	Call KutuCiz1("B42:D45")
	Call KutuCiz1("B49:C51")
	Call SutunGenisligi("B:B", 70)
	Call SutunGenisligi("C:M", 14)
	Call Boya("B5", 6)
	Call Boya("B7", 6)
	Call Boya("B9", 6)
	Call Boya("B17", 6)
	Call Boya("F13:K13", 6)
	Call Boya("F17:M17", 6)
	Call Boya("B22:F22", 35)
	Call Boya("B24:F26", 35)
	Call Satiryuksekligi("5:5", 30)
	Call Satiryuksekligi("7:7", 30)
	Call Satiryuksekligi("9:9", 30)
	Call Satiryuksekligi("13:13", 30)
	Call Satiryuksekligi("17:17", 30)
	Call Satiryuksekligi("24:24", 22)
	Call Satiryuksekligi("25:25", 22)
	Call Satiryuksekligi("26:26", 22)
	Sh2.Cells(29,2).Select
	excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	Sh2.Cells(33,2).Select
	excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	Sh2.Cells(40,2).Select
	excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	Sh2.Cells(51,2).Select
	excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True

	

End Sub
Sub SureHesapla(satirn)

'***********************
	


'***********************

	Set LISTEG = Doc.Select1("___tABLE10XXCCX","SFDC32E","X_T_ISMERKEZI='"&LISTET.KOD(satirn) &"'","",-1)

for tt=1 to LISTEG.GETRECCOUNT

	mpsno = LISTEG.MPSNO(tt)
	lbc = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",LISTEG.EVRAKNO(tt),"LBC",0)


	Kriter=""
	Kriter=Kriter&"MPSNO='"&mpsno &"'"
	Kriter=Kriter&" & "
	Kriter=Kriter&"TARIH >='"&tarih1 &"'"
	Kriter=Kriter&" & "
	Kriter=Kriter&"TARIH <='"&tarih2&"'"

	Set s70t=Doc.Select1("___tABLE10","STOK20T", kriter,"",-1)
	if trim(lbc) = "A" then
		if s70t.getreccount > 0 then
			SF.ADDROW
			s1=sf.getreccount
			sf.evrakno(s1) = LISTEG.evrakno(tt)
			sf.MPSNO(s1) = LISTEG.MPSNO(tt)
			sf.RECTYPE(s1) = LISTEG.RECTYPE(tt)
			sf.X_T_ISMERKEZI(s1) = LISTEG.X_T_ISMERKEZI(tt)
			sf.rectarih1(s1) = LISTEG.RECTARIH1(tt)
			sf.rectime1(s1) = LISTEG.RECTIME1(tt)
			sf.rectarih2(s1) = LISTEG.RECTARIH2(tt)
			sf.rectime2(s1) = LISTEG.RECTIME2(tt)
			sf.NOTES(s1) = trim(lbc)
		End If
	else
		if LISTEG.RECTARIH1(tt)>= tarih1 and LISTEG.RECTARIH2(tt)<= tarih2 then
			SF.ADDROW
			s1=sf.getreccount
			sf.evrakno(s1) = LISTEG.evrakno(tt)
			sf.MPSNO(s1) = LISTEG.MPSNO(tt)
			sf.RECTYPE(s1) = LISTEG.RECTYPE(tt)
			sf.X_T_ISMERKEZI(s1) = LISTEG.X_T_ISMERKEZI(tt)
			sf.rectarih1(s1) = LISTEG.RECTARIH1(tt)
			sf.rectime1(s1) = LISTEG.RECTIME1(tt)
			sf.rectarih2(s1) = LISTEG.RECTARIH2(tt)
			sf.rectime2(s1) = LISTEG.RECTIME2(tt)
			sf.NOTES(s1) = trim(lbc)

		end if
	end if
	Next

End Sub
Sub KutuCiz(aralik)
		Sh2.Range(aralik).Select
    excel.Selection.Borders(xlDiagonalDown).LineStyle = xlNone
    excel.Selection.Borders(xlDiagonalUp).LineStyle = xlNone
    
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeLeft).LineStyle = xlContinuous
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeLeft).Weight = xlThin
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeLeft).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic

		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeTop).LineStyle = xlContinuous
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeTop).Weight = xlThin
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeTop).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic

		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeBottom).LineStyle = xlContinuous
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeBottom).Weight = xlThin
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeBottom).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic

    	excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeRight).LineStyle = xlContinuous
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeRight).Weight = xlThin
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeRight).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
   
    excel.Selection.Borders(xlInsideVertical).LineStyle = xlNone
End Sub
Sub BaslikAyarla(aralik)
		Sh2.Range(aralik).Select
		excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
		excel.Selection.Font.Name = "Arial Tur"
		excel.Selection.Font.Size = 12
		excel.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = xlCenterAcrossSelection
		excel.Selection.VerticalAlignment = xlBottom
		excel.Selection.WrapText = False
     excel.Selection.Orientation = 0
		excel.Selection.AddIndent = False
		excel.Selection.IndentLevel = 0
		excel.Selection.ShrinkToFit = False
		excel.Selection.ReadingOrder = xlContext
		excel.Selection.MergeCells = False    
    
End Sub
Sub KutuCiz1(aralik)
		Sh2.Range(aralik).Select
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlDiagonalDown).LineStyle = xlNone
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlDiagonalUp).LineStyle = xlNone
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeLeft).LineStyle = xlContinuous
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeLeft).Weight = xlThin
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeLeft).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeTop).LineStyle = xlContinuous
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeTop).Weight = xlThin
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeTop).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeBottom).LineStyle = xlContinuous
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeBottom).Weight = xlThin
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeBottom).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeRight).LineStyle = xlContinuous
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeRight).Weight = xlThin
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeRight).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlInsideVertical).LineStyle = xlContinuous
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlInsideVertical).Weight = xlThin
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlInsideVertical).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlInsideHorizontal).LineStyle = xlContinuous
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlInsideHorizontal).Weight = xlThin
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlInsideHorizontal).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
End Sub
Sub Belirle(aralik)
'************************
		Sh2.Range(aralik).Select
		excel.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = xlGeneral
		excel.Selection.VerticalAlignment = xlBottom
		excel.Selection.WrapText = True
		excel.Selection.Orientation = 0
		excel.Selection.AddIndent = False
		excel.Selection.IndentLevel = 0
		excel.Selection.ShrinkToFit = False
		excel.Selection.ReadingOrder = xlContext
		excel.Selection.MergeCells = True
End Sub
Sub SutunGenisligi(aralik, deger)
	sh2.Columns(aralik).Select
	excel.Selection.ColumnWidth = deger

End Sub
Sub Boya(aralik, renk)
  Sh2.Range(aralik).Select
  excel.Selection.Interior.ColorIndex = renk
	excel.Selection.Interior.Pattern = xlSolid
End Sub
Sub Satiryuksekligi(aralik, deger)
  Sh2.Rows(aralik).Select
	excel.Selection.RowHeight = deger
End Sub

Açýklama : PRDUCTION SPLIT BY MACHINE
Kod : 
dim s, wb, excel, sh1, sh2, c5, d5, e5, f5, g5, h5, i5, j5, k5, l5, m5, tem, tem1
dim c6, d6, e6, f6, g6, h6, i6, j6, k6, l6, m6
dim c7, d7, e7, f7, g7, h7, i7, j7, k7, l7, m7
dim c8, d8, e8, f8, g8, h8, i8, j8, k8, l8, m8, tarih1, tarih2, SF, XC8, XXC8
dim c9, d9, e9, f9, g9, h9, i9, j9, k9, l9, m9

dim total()
	dim S32E, jj, mpsno1, ab, input, output, tip, neden, dolum, netinput, netoutput, elekalti, kMe,kmb,ia

Const xlAutomatic = -4105
Const xlNone = -4142
Const xlContinuous = 1
Const xlThin = 2
Const xlDiagonalDown = 5
Const xlDiagonalUp = 6
Const xlEdgeLeft = 7
Const xlEdgeTop = 8
Const xlEdgeBottom = 9
Const xlEdgeRight = 10
Const xlInsideVertical = 11
Const xlInsideHorizontal = 12
Const xlGeneral = 1
Const xlBottom = -4107 
Const xlContext = -5002
Const xlLineStyleNone = -4142 
Const xlCenterAcrossSelection = 7
Const xlSolid = 1 

Sub Makro1()
	Donem = Inputbox("Tarih Aralýðý Giriniz","Dinamo","2009/10/01-2009/10/31")
	tarih1 = mid(trim(Donem), 1, 10)
	tarih2 = mid(trim(Donem), 12, 10)
	XXC8=0
	XC8=0
	tem = 0
	tem1 = 0
	s=0
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb = Excel.Workbooks.Add	

	Set Sh1 = Wb.Sheets.Add
	Sh1.Name = "PRODUCTION SPLIT BY MACHINE"

	Sh1.Cells(1,3).Select
	Sh1.Cells(1,3).Font.Name="Arial"
	Sh1.Cells(1,3).Font.Size=12

	Sh1.Cells(1,3)= "PRODUCTION SPLIT BY MACHINE"
	excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	ReDim total(5, 13)

	Doc.CreateTableObject "ABCXYZ=SFDC32E","TMPCOPY"
	Set SF = Doc.GetTableObject("ABCXYZ") 


FOR i=1 TO listet.getreccount
	c5 = 0
	c6 = 0
	c7 = 0
	c8 = 0
	c9 = 0

	d5 = 0
	d6 = 0
	d7 = 0
	d8 = 0
	d9 = 0

	e5 = 0
	e6 = 0
	e7 = 0
	e8 = 0
	e9 = 0

	f5 = 0
	f6 = 0
	f7 = 0
	f8 = 0
	f9 = 0

	g5 = 0
	g6 = 0
	g7 = 0
	g8 = 0
	g9 = 0

	h5 = 0
	h6 = 0
	h7 = 0
	h8 = 0
	h9 = 0

	i5 = 0
	i6 = 0
	i7 = 0
	i8 = 0
	i9 = 0
	
	j5 = 0
	j6 = 0
	j7 = 0
	j8 = 0
	j9 = 0

	k5 = 0
	k6 = 0
	k7 = 0
	k8 = 0
	k9 = 0

	l5 = 0
	l6 = 0
	l7 = 0
	l8 = 0
	l9 = 0

	m5 = 0
	m6 = 0
	m7 = 0
	m8 = 0
	m9 = 0

'*******************
	mpsno1=""
	sf.empty
	call surehesapla(i)
	SF.sort("mpsno")

for jj=1 to SF.getreccount
if SF.RECTARIH1(jj)>= tarih1 and SF.RECTARIH1(jj)<= tarih2 then
	ia = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"IADE",0)
	tip = SF.RECTYPE(jj)
	ab = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"LBC",0)
	neden = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"BGR",0)
	input = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"INPUT",0)
	netinput=input - Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"IADE",0)
	output = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"OUTPUT",0)	
	elekalti = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"EAA",0)
	NM = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"NM",0)
	
	kme = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"ME",0)	
	kmb = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"MB",0)	
	netoutput=output+elekalti+nm
	tem = tem + Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"PP",0) + Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"PE",0)+Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"HDPE",0) + Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"KIRMA",0)
	tem1 = tem1 + Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"PP",0) + Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"PE",0)+Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"HDPE",0) 

	L5 = l5 + (kme - kmb)
	if trim(SF.MPSNO(jj))<>trim(mpsno1) then

		mpsno1 = SF.MPSNO(jj)
		Dim Kriter
		Kriter=""
		Kriter=Kriter&"MPSNO='"&mpsno1 &"'"
		Kriter=Kriter&" & "
		Kriter=Kriter&"RECTARIH1<='"&tarih2 &"'"
		sab1 = 0 
		Set S322=Doc.Select1("___tABLESFDC","SFDC32E",Kriter,"",-1)

		for b=1 to S322.getreccount
			ab1 = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",S322.EVRAKNO(b),"LBC",0)
			if trim(ab1)="A" then
				sab1=1
			end if	
		next	 	
	
		select case trim(ab)
			case "A" 
					F5 = f5 + input-ia
					H5 = h5 +netoutput

					C5 = C5+1
			case "B"
				if sab1 = 0 then
					C5 = C5+1
					F5 = f5 + input-ia
					H5 = h5 +netoutput

				end if
				if trim(neden)="RTF" then
					C7 = C7 + 1	
				end if
			case "C" 
				if sab1 = 0 then
					C5 = C5+1
					F5 = f5 + input-ia
					H5 = h5 +netoutput

				end if
				C8 = C8 + 1
				F8 = F8 + netinput	
				H8 = H8 + netoutput	
				L8 = L8 + (kme - kmb)	
		end select
	'****************************
		if trim(tip)="R" then
				C9 = C9 + 1
				F9 = F9 + netinput	
				H9 = H9 + netoutput	
				L9 = L9 + (kme - kmb)	

		end if
		if trim(tip)="P" then
			XC8 = XC8 + 1
			XXC8 = XXC8 + netoutput

		end if

	END if
'******
	select case trim(ab)
		case "B" 
			if trim(neden)="RTF" then
				F7 = F7 + netinput	
				H7 = H7 + netoutput	
				L7 = L7 + (kme - kmb)	
				
			end if
		case "C" 
		
		end select
End If
Next
'/////////////////////////////////
C6 = C7 + C8
C5= C5 + C6
D5 = 100
E5 = DOC.DIV(H5, C5)

G5 = 100
I5 = 100
J5 = F5 - H5
K5 = DOC.DIV(J5, F5)*100
M5 = DOC.DIV(H5, L5)
D6 = DOC.DIV(C6, C5)
E6 = DOC.DIV(H6, C6)
F6 = F7 + F8
G6 = DOC.DIV(F6, F5)
H6 = H7 + H8
I6 = DOC.DIV(H6, H5)
J6 = J7 + J8
K6 = DOC.DIV(J6, F6)
L6 = L7 + L8
M6 = DOC.DIV(H6, L6)
'******************
D7 = DOC.DIV(C7, C5)
E7 = DOC.DIV(H7, C7)
G7 = DOC.DIV(F7, F5)
I7 = DOC.DIV(H7, H5)
J7 = F7 - H7
K7 = DOC.DIV(J7, F7)*100
M7 = DOC.DIV(H7, L7)
'*****************
D8 = DOC.DIV(C8, C5)
E8 = DOC.DIV(H8, C8)
G8 = DOC.DIV(F8, F5)
I8 = DOC.DIV(H8, H5)
J8 = F8 - H8
K8 = DOC.DIV(J8, F8)*100
M8 = DOC.DIV(H8, L8)
'*********************
D9 = DOC.DIV(C9, C5)
E9 = DOC.DIV(H9, C9)
G9 = DOC.DIV(F9, F5)
I9 = DOC.DIV(H9, H5)
J9 = F9 - H9
K9 = DOC.DIV(J9, F9)*100
M9 = DOC.DIV(H9, L9)

'*********************
	call excele_yaz(i)


'*******************************
	total(0, 0) = "TOTAL "
	total(1, 0) = "PRODUCTION OF FINISHED GOODS "
	total(2, 0) = "Total Rework (Total B Grade but transformed to A)"
	total(3, 0) = "Rework With  Material"
	total(4, 0) = "Rework With  Material"
	total(5, 0) = "Rework  Unscheduled "

	total(0, 1) = "NR OF LOTS"
	
	total(1, 1) = total(1,1) + c5
	total(3, 1) = total(3,1) + c7
	total(4, 1) = total(4,1) + c8
	total(5, 1) = total(5,1) + c9
	total(2, 1) = total(3,1) + total(4,1)

	total(0, 2) = "LOT %"
	total(1, 2) = "100%"
	total(2, 2) = doc.div(total(2,1), total(1,1))
	total(3, 2) = doc.div(total(3,1), total(1,1))
	total(4, 2) = doc.div(total(4,1), total(1,1))
	total(5, 2) = doc.div(total(5,1), total(1,1))

	total(0, 6) = "OUTPUT KG "
	total(1, 6) = total(1,6) + h5
	total(3, 6) = total(3,6) + h7
	total(4, 6) = total(4,6) + h8
	total(5, 6) = total(5,6) + h9

	total(2, 6) = total(3,6) + total(4,6)+ total(5,6)


	total(0, 3) = "AVERAGE LOTSIZE"
	total(1, 3) = doc.div(total(1,6), total(1,1))
	total(2, 3) = doc.div(total(2,6), total(2,1))
	total(3, 3) = doc.div(total(3,6), total(3,1))
	total(4, 3) = doc.div(total(4,6), total(4,1))
	total(5, 3) = doc.div(total(5,6), total(5,1))

	total(0, 4) = "INPUT KG"
	total(1, 4) = total(1,4) + f5
	total(3, 4) = total(3,4) + f7
	total(4, 4) = total(4,4) + f8
	total(5, 4) = total(5,4) + f9

	total(2, 4) = total(3,4) + total(4,4)+ total(5,4)


	total(0, 5) = "INPUT %"
	total(1, 5) = "100"
	total(2, 5) = doc.div(total(2,4), total(1,4))
	total(3, 5) = doc.div(total(3,4), total(1,4))
	total(4, 5) = doc.div(total(4,4), total(1,4))
	total(5, 5) = doc.div(total(5,4), total(1,4))



	total(0, 7) = "OUTPUT %"
	total(1, 7) = "100"
	total(2, 7) = doc.div(total(2,6), total(1,6))
	total(3, 7) = doc.div(total(3,6), total(1,6))
	total(4, 7) = doc.div(total(4,6), total(1,6))
	total(5, 7) = doc.div(total(5,6), total(1,6))


	total(0, 8) = "IN / OUT DIFF. KG"
	total(1, 8) = total(1,8) + j5
	total(3, 8) = total(3,8) + j7
	total(4, 8) = total(4,8) + j8
	total(5, 8) = total(5,8) + j9

	total(2, 8) = total(3,8) + total(4,8)+ total(5,8)



	total(0, 9) = "DIFF. %"
	total(1, 9) = doc.div(total(1,8), total(1,4))
	total(2, 9) = doc.div(total(2,8), total(2,4))
	total(3, 9) = doc.div(total(3,8), total(3,4))
	total(4, 9) = doc.div(total(4,8), total(4,4))
	total(5, 9) = doc.div(total(5,8), total(5,4))

	total(0, 10) = "HOURS"
	total(1, 10) = total(1,10) + l5
	total(3, 10) = total(3,10) + l7
	total(4, 10) = total(4,10) + l8
	total(4, 10) = total(5,10) + l9

	total(2, 10) = total(3,10) + total(4,10)+ total(5,10)



	total(0, 11) = "KG / HR"
	total(1, 11) = doc.div(total(1,6), total(1,10))
	total(2, 11) = doc.div(total(2,6), total(2,10))
	total(3, 11) = doc.div(total(3,6), total(3,10))
	total(4, 11) = doc.div(total(4,6), total(4,10))
	total(5, 11) = doc.div(total(5,6), total(5,10))

'----------------------
next
	s=s+5
	Sh1.Cells(s,2).Select
	excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
		

	for xx=0 to 5
		s=s+1
		for z=0 to 11
			Sh1.Cells(s,2).Select
			excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
			if xx=0 then
				Sh1.Cells(s,z+2).Select
				excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
			end if

			if z=0 or xx=0 then
				Sh1.Cells(s,z+2)= total(xx, z)
			else
				Sh1.Cells(s,z+2)= doc.numformat(total(xx, z), 10,2)
			end if
		Next
	next
	Call Excele_yaz2()
	Excel.Visible = True

End Sub

Sub SureHesapla(satirn)
	yenitarih=tarih1
	Set LISTEG=Doc.Select1("___tABLE10XXX","SFDC32E","X_T_ISMERKEZI='"&LISTET.KOD(satirn) &"'","",-1)

for TT=1 to LISTEG.GETRECCOUNT
	t=0
	sorgu = 0
	EVRAKNO = LISTEG.evrakno(tT)
	Set s70t=Doc.Select1("___tABLE10","SFDC32E",""&"EVRAKNO='"&evrakno &"'","",-1)
	t0=Doc.Date_NumToStr(doc.CDateToInt(S70T.RECTARIH1(1)) - 1)
	t1=Doc.Date_NumToStr(doc.CDateToInt(S70T.RECTARIH1(1)) + 1)
	t2=Doc.Date_NumToStr(doc.CDateToInt(S70T.RECTARIH1(1)) + 2)
	t3=Doc.Date_NumToStr(doc.CDateToInt(S70T.RECTARIH1(1)) + 3)
	if S70T.RECTIME1(1) < "09:00" then
		SF.ADDROW
		s1=sf.getreccount
		sf.evrakno(s1) = S70T.evrakno(1)
		sf.MPSNO(s1) = S70T.MPSNO(1)
		sf.RECTYPE(s1) = S70T.RECTYPE(1)
		sf.X_T_ISMERKEZI(s1) = S70T.X_T_ISMERKEZI(1)
		sf.rectarih1(s1) = t0
		sf.rectime1(s1) = S70T.rectime1(1)
		if S70T.RECTIME2< "09:00" and s70t.rectarih2 = s70t.rectarih1 then
			sorgu=sorgu+1
			sf.rectarih2(s1) = t0
			sf.rectime2(s1) = S70T.RECTIME2
		else
			sf.rectarih2(s1) = t0
			sf.rectime2(s1) = "09:00"
			S70T.rectime1(1) = "09:00"
		end if

		sf.sure(s1) = S70T.sure(1)
		T=t+doc.DTDTDif1(S70T.RECTARIH1,S70T.RECTIME1,S70T.RECTARIH1,"09:00")
	end if
	if sorgu =0 then

	fark1=doc.DTDTDif1(S70T.RECTARIH1,S70T.RECTIME1,t1,"09:00")
	t=t+fark1
	IF t >= s70t.sure THEN
		SF.ADDROW
		S1=sf.getreccount
		sf.evrakno(s1) = S70T.evrakno(1)
		sf.MPSNO(s1) = S70T.MPSNO(1)
		sf.RECTYPE(s1) = S70T.RECTYPE(1)
		sf.X_T_ISMERKEZI(s1) = S70T.X_T_ISMERKEZI(1)
		sf.rectarih1(s1) = S70T.RECTARIH1(1)
		sf.rectime1(s1) = S70T.rectime1(1)
		sf.rectarih2(s1) = S70T.RECTARIH2(1)
		sf.rectime2(s1) = S70T.RECTIME2(1)
		sf.sure(s1) = S70T.sure(1)
		sorgu=sorgu+1
	
	ELSE

		SF.ADDROW
		S1=sf.getreccount
		sf.evrakno(s1) = S70T.evrakno(1)
		sf.MPSNO(s1) = S70T.MPSNO(1)
		sf.RECTYPE(s1) = S70T.RECTYPE(1)
		sf.X_T_ISMERKEZI(s1) = S70T.X_T_ISMERKEZI(1)
		sf.rectarih1(s1) = S70T.RECTARIH1(1)
		sf.rectime1(s1) = S70T.rectime1(1)
		sf.rectarih2(s1) = t1
		sf.rectime2(s1) = "09:00"
		sf.sure(s1) = S70T.sure(1)
		S70T.rectarih1 = t1
		S70T.rectime1 = "09:00"
	END IF
	end if
	fark1=doc.DTDTDif1(S70T.RECTARIH1,S70T.RECTIME1,t2,"09:00")
	t=t+fark1
	if sorgu = 0 then
	IF t >= s70t.sure THEN
		SF.ADDROW
		S1=sf.getreccount
		sf.evrakno(s1) = S70T.evrakno(1)
		sf.MPSNO(s1) = S70T.MPSNO(1)
		sf.RECTYPE(s1) = S70T.RECTYPE(1)
		sf.X_T_ISMERKEZI(s1) = S70T.X_T_ISMERKEZI(1)
		sf.rectarih1(s1) = S70T.RECTARIH1(1)
		sf.rectime1(s1) = S70T.rectime1(1)
		sf.rectarih2(s1) = S70T.RECTARIH2(1)
		sf.rectime2(s1) = S70T.RECTIME2(1)
		sf.sure(s1) = S70T.sure(1)
		sorgu=sorgu+1

	ELSE
		SF.ADDROW
		S1=sf.getreccount
		sf.evrakno(s1) = S70T.evrakno(1)
		sf.MPSNO(s1) = S70T.MPSNO(1)
		sf.RECTYPE(s1) = S70T.RECTYPE(1)
		sf.X_T_ISMERKEZI(s1) = S70T.X_T_ISMERKEZI(1)
		sf.rectarih1(s1) = S70T.RECTARIH1(1)
		sf.rectime1(s1) = S70T.rectime1(1)
		sf.rectarih2(s1) = t2
		sf.rectime2(s1) = "09:00"
		sf.sure(s1) = S70T.sure(1)
		S70T.rectarih1 = t2
		S70T.rectime1 = "09:00"
	END IF
	end if

	fark1=doc.DTDTDif1(S70T.RECTARIH1,S70T.RECTIME1,t3,"09:00")
	t=t+fark1
	if sorgu = 0 then

	IF t >= s70t.sure THEN
		SF.ADDROW
		S1=sf.getreccount
		sf.evrakno(s1) = S70T.evrakno(1)
		sf.MPSNO(s1) = S70T.MPSNO(1)
		sf.RECTYPE(s1) = S70T.RECTYPE(1)
		sf.RECTYPE(s1) = S70T.RECTYPE(1)
		sf.X_T_ISMERKEZI(s1) = S70T.X_T_ISMERKEZI(1)
		sf.rectarih1(s1) = S70T.RECTARIH1(1)
		sf.rectime1(s1) = S70T.rectime1(1)
		sf.rectarih2(s1) = S70T.RECTARIH2(1)
		sf.rectime2(s1) = S70T.RECTIME2(1)
		sf.sure(s1) = S70T.sure(1)
	ELSE
		SF.ADDROW
		S1=sf.getreccount
		sf.evrakno(s1) = S70T.evrakno(1)
		sf.MPSNO(s1) = S70T.MPSNO(1)
		sf.RECTYPE(s1) = S70T.RECTYPE(1)
		sf.X_T_ISMERKEZI(s1) = S70T.X_T_ISMERKEZI(1)
		sf.rectarih1(s1) = S70T.RECTARIH1(1)
		sf.rectime1(s1) = S70T.rectime1(1)
		sf.rectarih2(s1) = t3
		sf.rectime2(s1) = "09:00"
		sf.sure(s1) = S70T.sure(1)

		S70T.rectarih1 = t3
		S70T.rectime1 = "09:00"
	END IF
	end if
next
	sf.sort("RECTARIH1+MPSNO")
End Sub
Sub KutuCiz(aralik)
		Sh2.Range(aralik).Select
    excel.Selection.Borders(xlDiagonalDown).LineStyle = xlNone
    excel.Selection.Borders(xlDiagonalUp).LineStyle = xlNone
    
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeLeft).LineStyle = xlContinuous
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeLeft).Weight = xlThin
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeLeft).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic

		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeTop).LineStyle = xlContinuous
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeTop).Weight = xlThin
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeTop).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic

		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeBottom).LineStyle = xlContinuous
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeBottom).Weight = xlThin
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeBottom).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic

    	excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeRight).LineStyle = xlContinuous
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeRight).Weight = xlThin
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeRight).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
   
    excel.Selection.Borders(xlInsideVertical).LineStyle = xlNone
End Sub
Sub BaslikAyarla(aralik)
		Sh2.Range(aralik).Select
		excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
		excel.Selection.Font.Name = "Arial Tur"
		excel.Selection.Font.Size = 12
		excel.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = xlCenterAcrossSelection
		excel.Selection.VerticalAlignment = xlBottom
		excel.Selection.WrapText = False
     excel.Selection.Orientation = 0
		excel.Selection.AddIndent = False
		excel.Selection.IndentLevel = 0
		excel.Selection.ShrinkToFit = False
		excel.Selection.ReadingOrder = xlContext
		excel.Selection.MergeCells = False    
    
End Sub
Sub KutuCiz1(aralik)
		Sh2.Range(aralik).Select
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlDiagonalDown).LineStyle = xlNone
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlDiagonalUp).LineStyle = xlNone
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeLeft).LineStyle = xlContinuous
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeLeft).Weight = xlThin
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeLeft).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeTop).LineStyle = xlContinuous
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeTop).Weight = xlThin
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeTop).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeBottom).LineStyle = xlContinuous
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeBottom).Weight = xlThin
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeBottom).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeRight).LineStyle = xlContinuous
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeRight).Weight = xlThin
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeRight).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlInsideVertical).LineStyle = xlContinuous
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlInsideVertical).Weight = xlThin
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlInsideVertical).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlInsideHorizontal).LineStyle = xlContinuous
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlInsideHorizontal).Weight = xlThin
		excel.Selection.Borders(xlInsideHorizontal).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
End Sub
Sub Belirle(aralik)
'************************
		Sh2.Range(aralik).Select
		excel.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = xlGeneral
		excel.Selection.VerticalAlignment = xlBottom
		excel.Selection.WrapText = True
		excel.Selection.Orientation = 0
		excel.Selection.AddIndent = False
		excel.Selection.IndentLevel = 0
		excel.Selection.ShrinkToFit = False
		excel.Selection.ReadingOrder = xlContext
		excel.Selection.MergeCells = True
End Sub
Sub SutunGenisligi(aralik, deger)
	sh2.Columns(aralik).Select
	excel.Selection.ColumnWidth = deger

End Sub
Sub Boya(aralik, renk)
  Sh2.Range(aralik).Select
  excel.Selection.Interior.ColorIndex = renk
	excel.Selection.Interior.Pattern = xlSolid
End Sub
Sub Satiryuksekligi(aralik, deger)
  Sh2.Rows(aralik).Select
	excel.Selection.RowHeight = deger
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : SNK10 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : *02
Hangi Tablo için : IMLT00
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : PRDUCTION SPLIT BY MACHINE
Kod : 
dim s, wb, excel, sh1, sh2, c5, d5, e5, f5, g5, h5, i5, j5, k5, l5, m5, tem, tem1, sh3
dim c6, d6, e6, f6, g6, h6, i6, j6, k6, l6, m6
dim c7, d7, e7, f7, g7, h7, i7, j7, k7, l7, m7
dim c8, d8, e8, f8, g8, h8, i8, j8, k8, l8, m8, tarih1, tarih2, SF, XC8, XXC8, XXXC8
dim c9, d9, e9, f9, g9, h9, i9, j9, k9, l9, m9, toplamnm

dim total()
dim S32E, jj, mpsno1, ab, input, output, tip, neden, dolum, netinput, netoutput, elekalti, kMe,kmb,ia

Const xlAutomatic = -4105
Const xlNone = -4142
Const xlContinuous = 1
Const xlThin = 2
Const xlDiagonalDown = 5
Const xlDiagonalUp = 6
Const xlEdgeLeft = 7
Const xlEdgeTop = 8
Const xlEdgeBottom = 9
Const xlEdgeRight = 10
Const xlInsideVertical = 11
Const xlInsideHorizontal = 12
Const xlGeneral = 1
Const xlBottom = -4107
Const xlContext = -5002
Const xlLineStyleNone = -4142
Const xlCenterAcrossSelection = 7
Const xlSolid = 1

Sub Makro1()
	toplamnm = 0
	MlYilb = Doc.Customp_Gets("ML_YILB")
	MaliYilbasi = Right(MlYilb, 4) & "/" & Mid(MlYilb, 4,2) & "/" & Left(MlYilb, 2)
	
	Donem = Inputbox("Tarih Aralýðý Giriniz","Dinamo",MaliYilbasi & "-" & Doc.BuGun)
	if trim(Donem) = "" Then Exit Sub

	tarih1 = mid(trim(Donem), 1, 10)
	tarih2 = mid(trim(Donem), 12, 10)
	
	XXC8=0
	XC8=0
	tem = 0
	tem1 = 0
	s=0
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb = Excel.Workbooks.Add
	
	Set Sh1 = Wb.Sheets.Add
	Sh1.Name = "PRODUCTION SPLIT BY MACHINE"
	
	Sh1.Cells(1,3).Select
	Sh1.Cells(1,3).Font.Name="Arial"
	Sh1.Cells(1,3).Font.Size=12
	
	Sh1.Cells(1,3)= "PRODUCTION SPLIT BY MACHINE"
	excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	ReDim total(5, 13)
	
	Doc.CreateTableObject "ABCXYZ=SFDC32E","TMPCOPY"
	Set SF = Doc.GetTableObject("ABCXYZ")
	
	
	FOR i=1 TO LISTET.GetRowCount()
		Call Sifirla()		
		mpsno1=""

		sf.empty
		call surehesapla(i)
		SF.sort("mpsno+notes")
		for jj=1 to SF.GetRowCount()
			Doc.StatusBarMessage "Atölye kayýtlarý okunuyor : " & LISTET.KOD(i) & " - " & jj & " / " & SF.GetRowCount()
			ia = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"IADE",0)
			tip = SF.RECTYPE(jj)
			ab = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"LBC",0)
			neden = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"BGR",0)
			input = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"INPUT",0)
			netinput=input - Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"IADE",0)
			output = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"OUTPUT",0)
			elekalti = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"EAA",0)
			NM = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"NM",0)
			kme = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"ME",0)
			kmb = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"MB",0)
			netoutput=output+elekalti
			tem = tem + Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"PP",0) + Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"PE",0)+Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"HDPE",0) + Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"KIRMA",0)
			tem1 = tem1 + Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"PP",0) + Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"PE",0)+Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"HDPE",0)
			farkg=doc.DTDTDif1(SF.RECTARIH1(jj),SF.RECTIME1(jj), SF.RECTARIH2(jj),SF.RECTIME2(jj))
			sure = sf.SURE(jj)
			toplamnm = toplamnm + nm
			L5 = l5 + kme - kmb
			
			Kriter = "MPSNO='" & mpsno1 & "'&RECTARIH1<='" & tarih2 & "'"
			sab1 = 0
			Set S322=Doc.Select1("___tABLESFDC","SFDC32E",Kriter,"",-1)
			
			QStr = "MPSNO='" & mpsno1 & "'&TARIH>='" & tarih1 & "'&TARIH<='" & tarih2 & "'"
			Set s20t=Doc.Select1("___tABLE10zzzzz","STOK20T", QStr,"",-1)
			
			for b=1 to S322.GetRowCount()
				ab1 = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",S322.EVRAKNO(b),"LBC",0)
				if trim(ab1)="A" then
					sab1=1
				end if
			next
			if trim(SF.RECTYPE(jj))<>"D" and trim(SF.RECTYPE(jj))<>"P" then
				
				if trim(SF.MPSNO(jj))<>trim(mpsno1) then
					
					mpsno1 = SF.MPSNO(jj)
					
					select case trim(ab)
						case "A"
							F5 = f5 + input-ia
							H5 = h5 + netoutput
							C5 = C5 + 1
							sirano = sirano+1
							Sh1.Cells(sirano+95,2)= Mpsno1
							Sh1.Cells(sirano+95,3)= input-ia
							Sh1.Cells(sirano+95,4)= C5
						case "B"
							if sab1 = 0 and s20t.GetRowCount() > 0 then
								C5 = C5+1
								F5 = f5 + input-ia
								H5 = h5 +netoutput
								sirano = sirano+1
								Sh1.Cells(sirano+95,2)= Mpsno1
								Sh1.Cells(sirano+95,3)= input-ia
								
								Sh1.Cells(sirano+95,4)= C5
								
							end if
					end select
				end if
				select case trim(ab)
					case "B"
						if trim(neden)="RTF" or trim(neden)="RFT" then
							C7 = C7 + 1
							F7 = F7 + netinput
							H7 = H7 + netoutput
							L7 = L7 + (kme - kmb)
						else
							C9 = C9 + 1
							F9 = F9 + netinput
							H9 = H9 + netoutput
							L9 = L9 + (kme - kmb)
						end if
					case "C"
						
				end select
			END if
			
			'****************************
			
			if trim(tip)="D" then
				C8 = C8 + 1
				F8 = F8 + netinput
				H8 = H8 + netoutput
				L8 = L8 + (kme - kmb)
			end if
			if trim(tip)="P" then
				XC8 = XC8 + 1
				XXC8 = XXC8 + netoutput
				XXXC8 = XXXC8 + (kme - kmb)
			end if
			
			'******
			select case trim(ab)
				case "B"
					if trim(neden)="RTF" then
						
						
					end if
				case "C"
					
			end select
			'End If
		Next
		'/////////////////////////////////
		C6 = C7 + C8 + C9 '
		C5 = C5 + C6 '?
		D5 = 100
		E5 = DOC.DIV(H5, C5) '
		
		G5 = 100
		I5 = 100
		J5 = F5 - H5 '
		K5 = DOC.DIV(J5, F5) * 100 '
		M5 = DOC.DIV(H5, L5) '
		D6 = DOC.DIV(C6, C5) * 100 '
		F6 = F7 + F8 + F9 '
		G6 = DOC.DIV(F6, F5) * 100 '
		H6 = H7 + H8 + H9 '
		E6 = DOC.DIV(H6, C6) '
		
		I6 = DOC.DIV(H6, H5) * 100 '
'		J6 = J7 + J8
		J6 = F6 - H6 '
		K6 = DOC.DIV(J6, F6) * 100 '
		L6 = L7 + L8 + L9 '
		M6 = DOC.DIV(H6, L6) '
		'******************
		D7 = DOC.DIV(C7, C5)*100 '
		E7 = DOC.DIV(H7, C7) '
		G7 = DOC.DIV(F7, F5)*100 '
		I7 = DOC.DIV(H7, H5)*100 '
		J7 = F7 - H7 '
		K7 = DOC.DIV(J7, F7)*100 '
		M7 = DOC.DIV(H7, L7) '
		'*****************
		D8 = DOC.DIV(C8, C5)*100 '
		E8 = DOC.DIV(H8, C8) '
		G8 = DOC.DIV(F8, F5)*100 '
		I8 = DOC.DIV(H8, H5)*100 '
		J8 = F8 - H8 '
		K8 = DOC.DIV(J8, F8)*100 '
		M8 = DOC.DIV(H8, L8)' 
		'*********************
		D9 = DOC.DIV(C9, C5)*100
		E9 = DOC.DIV(H9, C9) '
		G9 = DOC.DIV(F9, F5)*100 '
		I9 = DOC.DIV(H9, H5)*100 '
		J9 = F9 - H9 '
		K9 = DOC.DIV(J9, F9)*100 '
		M9 = DOC.DIV(H9, L9) '
		
		'*********************
		call excele_yaz(i)
		
		
		'*******************************
		if trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00", "GK_3",LISTET.KOD(i) ,"")) <> "T" Then
			total(0, 0) = "TOTAL "
			total(1, 0) = "PRODUCTION OF FINISHED GOODS "
			total(2, 0) = "Total Rework (Total B Grade but transformed to A)"
			total(3, 0) = "Rework With  Material"
			total(4, 0) = "Rework  scheduled"
			total(5, 0) = "Rework  Unscheduled "
		
			total(0, 1) = "NR OF LOTS"
		
			total(1, 1) = total(1,1) + c5
			total(3, 1) = total(3,1) + c7
			total(4, 1) = total(4,1) + c8
			total(5, 1) = total(5,1) + c9
			total(2, 1) = total(3,1) + total(4,1) + total(5,1)
		
			total(0, 2) = "LOT %"
			total(1, 2) = "100%"
			total(2, 2) = doc.div(total(2,1), total(1,1))
			total(3, 2) = doc.div(total(3,1), total(1,1))
			total(4, 2) = doc.div(total(4,1), total(1,1))
			total(5, 2) = doc.div(total(5,1), total(1,1))
		
			total(0, 6) = "OUTPUT KG "
			total(1, 6) = total(1,6) + h5
			total(3, 6) = total(3,6) + h7
			total(4, 6) = total(4,6) + h8
			total(5, 6) = total(5,6) + h9
		
			total(2, 6) = total(3,6) + total(4,6)+ total(5,6)
		
			total(0, 3) = "AVERAGE LOTSIZE"
			total(1, 3) = doc.div(total(1,6), total(1,1))
			total(2, 3) = doc.div(total(2,6), total(2,1))
			total(3, 3) = doc.div(total(3,6), total(3,1))
			total(4, 3) = doc.div(total(4,6), total(4,1))
			total(5, 3) = doc.div(total(5,6), total(5,1))
		
			total(0, 4) = "INPUT KG"
			total(1, 4) = total(1,4) + f5
			total(3, 4) = total(3,4) + f7
			total(4, 4) = total(4,4) + f8
			total(5, 4) = total(5,4) + f9
		
			total(2, 4) = total(3,4) + total(4,4)+ total(5,4)
		
		
			total(0, 5) = "INPUT %"
			total(1, 5) = "100"
			total(2, 5) = doc.div(total(2,4), total(1,4))
			total(3, 5) = doc.div(total(3,4), total(1,4))
			total(4, 5) = doc.div(total(4,4), total(1,4))
			total(5, 5) = doc.div(total(5,4), total(1,4))
		
			total(0, 7) = "OUTPUT %"
			total(1, 7) = "100"
			total(2, 7) = doc.div(total(2,6), total(1,6))
			total(3, 7) = doc.div(total(3,6), total(1,6))
			total(4, 7) = doc.div(total(4,6), total(1,6))
			total(5, 7) = doc.div(total(5,6), total(1,6))
		
		
			total(0, 8) = "IN / OUT DIFF. KG"
			total(1, 8) = total(1,8) + j5
			total(3, 8) = total(3,8) + j7
			total(4, 8) = total(4,8) + j8
			total(5, 8) = total(5,8) + j9
		
			total(2, 8) = total(3,8) + total(4,8)+ total(5,8)
				
			total(0, 9) = "DIFF. %"
			total(1, 9) = doc.div(total(1,8), total(1,4))
			total(2, 9) = doc.div(total(2,8), total(2,4))
			total(3, 9) = doc.div(total(3,8), total(3,4))
			total(4, 9) = doc.div(total(4,8), total(4,4))
			total(5, 9) = doc.div(total(5,8), total(5,4))
		
			total(0, 10) = "HOURS"
			total(1, 10) = total(1,10) + l5
			total(3, 10) = total(3,10) + l7
			total(4, 10) = total(4,10) + l8
			total(5, 10) = total(5,10) + l9
		
			total(2, 10) = total(3,10) + total(4,10)+ total(5,10)
		
			total(0, 11) = "KG / HR"
			total(1, 11) = doc.div(total(1,6), total(1,10))
			total(2, 11) = doc.div(total(2,6), total(2,10))
			total(3, 11) = doc.div(total(3,6), total(3,10))
			total(4, 11) = doc.div(total(4,6), total(4,10))
			total(5, 11) = doc.div(total(5,6), total(5,10))
		End if
		'----------------------
	next
	s=s+5
	Sh1.Cells(s,2).Select
	excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	
	
	for xx=0 to 5
		s=s+1
		for z=0 to 11
			Sh1.Cells(s,2).Select
			excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
			if xx=0 then
				Sh1.Cells(s,z+2).Select
				excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
			end if
			
			if z=0 or xx=0 then
				Sh1.Cells(s,z+2)= total(xx, z)
			elseif z = 9 then
				Sh1.Cells(s,z+2)= doc.numformat(total(xx, z)*100, 10,3)
				
			else
				Sh1.Cells(s,z+2)= doc.numformat(total(xx, z), 10,2)
			end if
		Next
	Next
	Call Excele_yaz2()
	Excel.Visible = True
	
End Sub

	Sh2.Cells(33,2).Select

	Sh2.Cells(40,2).Select
	excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	Sh2.Cells(51,2).Select
	excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	Sh2.Cells(47,2).Select
	excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	
	
	
End Sub
Sub SureHesapla(satirn)
	Set LISTEG = Doc.Select1("___tABLE10XXCCX","SFDC32E","X_T_ISMERKEZI='" & LISTET.KOD(satirn) & "'","",-1)
	for tt=1 to LISTEG.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Süreler Hesaplanýyor : " & trim(LISTET.KOD(satirn)) & " - " & tt & " / " & LISTEG.GetRowCount()
		mpsno = LISTEG.MPSNO(tt)
		lbc = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",LISTEG.EVRAKNO(tt),"LBC",0)
		
		Kriter=""
		Kriter=Kriter&"MPSNO='"&mpsno &"'"
		Kriter=Kriter&" & "
		Kriter=Kriter&"TARIH >='"&tarih1 &"'"
		Kriter=Kriter&" & "
		Kriter=Kriter&"TARIH <='"&tarih2&"'"
		
		Set s70t=Doc.Select1("___tABLE10","STOK20T", kriter,"",-1)
		if trim(lbc) = "A" then
			if s70t.GetRowCount() > 0 then
				SF.ADDROW
				s1=sf.GetRowCount()
				sf.evrakno(s1) = LISTEG.evrakno(tt)
				sf.MPSNO(s1) = LISTEG.MPSNO(tt)
				sf.RECTYPE(s1) = LISTEG.RECTYPE(tt)
				sf.X_T_ISMERKEZI(s1) = LISTEG.X_T_ISMERKEZI(tt)
				sf.rectarih1(s1) = LISTEG.RECTARIH1(tt)
				sf.rectime1(s1) = LISTEG.RECTIME1(tt)
				sf.rectarih2(s1) = LISTEG.RECTARIH2(tt)
				sf.rectime2(s1) = LISTEG.RECTIME2(tt)
				sf.NOTES(s1) = trim(lbc)
			End If
		else
			if LISTEG.RECTARIH1(tt)>= tarih1 and LISTEG.RECTARIH2(tt)<= tarih2 then
				SF.ADDROW
				s1=sf.GetRowCount()
				sf.evrakno(s1) = LISTEG.evrakno(tt)
				sf.MPSNO(s1) = LISTEG.MPSNO(tt)
				sf.RECTYPE(s1) = LISTEG.RECTYPE(tt)
				sf.X_T_ISMERKEZI(s1) = LISTEG.X_T_ISMERKEZI(tt)
				sf.rectarih1(s1) = LISTEG.RECTARIH1(tt)
				sf.rectime1(s1) = LISTEG.RECTIME1(tt)
				sf.rectarih2(s1) = LISTEG.RECTARIH2(tt)
				sf.rectime2(s1) = LISTEG.RECTIME2(tt)
				sf.NOTES(s1) = trim(lbc)
			end if
		end if
	Next
End Sub
Sub KutuCiz(aralik)
	Sh2.Range(aralik).Select
	excel.Selection.Borders(xlDiagonalDown).LineStyle = xlNone
	excel.Selection.Borders(xlDiagonalUp).LineStyle = xlNone
	
	excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeLeft).LineStyle = xlContinuous
	excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeLeft).Weight = xlThin
	excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeLeft).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
	
	excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeTop).LineStyle = xlContinuous
	excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeTop).Weight = xlThin
	excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeTop).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
	
	excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeBottom).LineStyle = xlContinuous
	excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeBottom).Weight = xlThin
	excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeBottom).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
	
	excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeRight).LineStyle = xlContinuous
	excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeRight).Weight = xlThin
	excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeRight).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
	
	excel.Selection.Borders(xlInsideVertical).LineStyle = xlNone
End Sub

	excel.Selection.IndentLevel = 0

End Sub
