Ekran Formu
Uygulamalar Ýçin Baþlangýç Ekran Formu Ayarlarý
Baþlangýç Ekran Formu Bu Uygulama için : 
Bu Uygulama için Baþlangýç Ekran Formu : 
Baþlangýçta Bu Makro Çalýþacak : 
Alt Ekranlar Ýçin Ekran Formu
Alt Ekran Form Kodu : 
Yeni Alt Ekra Form Kodu: 
Alt Ekran Açýlmadan Önce Çalýþtýrýlacak Makro : 


----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : SNK10 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : *1
Hangi Tablo için : SFDC32
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : ENERJÝ RAPOR MAKRO **YENÝ
Kod : 
DIM S70T, SF, d(), SH2, WB, EXCEL, yenitarih, eskitarih
dim cc, excelkolon
dim htotal(), gtotal(), c()
Const xlAutomatic = -4105
Const xlNone = -4142
Const xlContinuous = 1
Const xlThin = 2
Const xlDiagonalDown = 5
Const xlDiagonalUp = 6
Const xlEdgeLeft = 7
Const xlEdgeTop = 8
Const xlEdgeBottom = 9
Const xlEdgeRight = 10
Const xlInsideVertical = 11
Const xlInsideHorizontal = 12
Const xlGeneral = 1
Const xlBottom = -4107 
Const xlContext = -5002

Sub makro1()
	excelkolon = 1
	cc = 0
	c2 = 0
	redim c(35)
	redim d(35)
	redim htotal(100)
	redim gtotal(100)
Set Excel = CreateObject("excel.Application")
	Set Wb = Excel.Workbooks.Add	
	Set Sh2 = Wb.Sheets.Add
	Sh2.Name = REPLACE(LISTEE.RECTARIH11, "/", "")
	for x=1 to 35
		c(x) = 0
		d(x) = 0
	Next

	Call data_yarat()
	
	sf.sort("RECTARIH1+MPSNO")
	yenitarih=LISTEE.RECTARIH11
	call gecmis_hesapla()
	call hafta_hesapla()
	For g=1 to 33
		Sh2.Cells(g,1).Select
		excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	Next
	For g1=1 to 13
		Sh2.Cells(1,g1).Select
		excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	Next
	Call CizgiCiz("A1:L84")
	Sh2.Range("A1:L75").Select
	Sh2.Range("A1:L75").columnwidth=12.45
	Sh2.Range("A:A").Select
	Sh2.Range("A:A").columnwidth=28.43

	Call belirle("A1:L84")
	call Font_belirle()
	Call exceleyazdir_toplam(excelkolon)
	Excel.Visible = True

End Sub
Sub exceleyazdir(eskitarih, excelkolon)
	ff=excelkolon
	Sh2.Cells(1,ff)= eskitarih

	Sh2.Cells(2,1)= "TOTAL FINISHED PRODUCTIONS (KGS)"
	Sh2.Cells(2,ff)= Doc.numformat(c(2),12,2)

	Sh2.Cells(4,1)= "REWORK (KGS)"
	Sh2.Cells(4,ff)= Doc.numformat(c(4),12,2)

	Sh2.Cells(7,1)= "OPERATING HOURS (HRS)-LEISTRITZ"
	Sh2.Cells(7,ff)= Doc.numformat(c(7),12,2)

	Sh2.Cells(8,1)= "OPERATING HOURS (HRS)-ZSK32-K"
	Sh2.Cells(8,ff)= Doc.numformat(c(8),12,2)

	Sh2.Cells(9,1)= "OPERATING HOURS (HRS)-ZSK32-S"
	Sh2.Cells(9,ff)= Doc.numformat(c(9),12,2)

	Sh2.Cells(10,1)= "OPERATING HOURS (HRS)-MARIS"
	Sh2.Cells(10,ff)= Doc.numformat(c(10),12,2)

	Sh2.Cells(12,1)= "WORKING HOURS (HRS)-TOTAL"
	Sh2.Cells(12,ff)= Doc.numformat(c(12),12,2)

	Sh2.Cells(14,1)= "TOTAL NUMBER OF LAB BATCHES"
	Sh2.Cells(15,1)= "TOTAL QUANTITY OF LAB BATCHES (kg)"
	Sh2.Cells(16,1)= "TOTAL EXTRUSION QUANTITY (2,5 TIME PASSING)-(kg)"
	Sh2.Cells(17,1)= "TOTAL CLEANING MATERIAL QUANTITY FOR LAB BATCHES (kg)"

	Sh2.Cells(18,1)= "CLEANING MATERIALS (KGS)"
	Sh2.Cells(18,ff)= Doc.numformat(c(18),12,2)

	Sh2.Cells(19,1)= "NO OF LOTS (FINAL PRODUCTIONS ONLY) "
	Sh2.Cells(19,ff)= Doc.numformat(c(19),12,2)

	Sh2.Cells(21,1)= "NO OF LOTS (INCLUDING PRE-MIX)"
	Sh2.Cells(21,ff)= Doc.numformat(c(21),12,2)

	Sh2.Cells(23,1)= "POWER COMPANY ENERGY COUNTER IND.(KWH)(MAIN PANEL)"
	Sh2.Cells(23,ff)= Doc.numformat(c(23),12,2)

	Sh2.Cells(24,1)= "TOTAL ENERGY CONSUMPTION FROM POWER COMPANY(KWh)"
	Sh2.Cells(24,ff)= Doc.numformat(c(24),12,2)

	

	Sh2.Cells(3,1)= "TOTAL FINISHED PRODUCTIONS AND CLEANING (KGS)"
	Sh2.Cells(3,ff)= doc.numformat(Sh2.Cells(2,ff) + Sh2.Cells(18,ff) + Sh2.Cells(16,ff) + Sh2.Cells(17,ff),12,2)

	Sh2.Cells(5,1)= "REWORK (%)"
	Sh2.Cells(5,ff)= doc.numformat(doc.div(Sh2.Cells(4,ff), Sh2.Cells(2,ff))*100,12,2)

	
	Sh2.Cells(6,1)= "MONO MATERIALS USED IN  PRODUCTIONS (KGS)"
	Sh2.Cells(6,ff)= doc.numformat(Sh2.Cells(2,ff) - Sh2.Cells(4,ff),12,2)


	Sh2.Cells(11,1)= "OPERATING HOURS (HRS)-TOTAL"
	Sh2.Cells(11,ff)= doc.numformat(Sh2.Cells(7,ff) + Sh2.Cells(8,ff) + Sh2.Cells(9,ff) + Sh2.Cells(10,ff),12,2)

	Sh2.Cells(13,1)= "USING CAPACITY OF LINES  (%)"
	Sh2.Cells(13,ff)= doc.numformat(doc.div(Sh2.Cells(11,ff), Sh2.Cells(12,ff))*100,12,2)

	Sh2.Cells(20,1)= "CLEANING MATERIALS (FOR FINAL PRODUCTS) (KGS/LOT)"
	Sh2.Cells(20,ff)= doc.numformat(doc.div(Sh2.Cells(18,ff), Sh2.Cells(19,ff)),12,2)

	Sh2.Cells(22,1)= "CLEANING MATERIALS (INCLUDING PRE-MIX) (KGS/LOT)"
	Sh2.Cells(22,ff)= doc.numformat(doc.div(Sh2.Cells(18,ff), Sh2.Cells(21,ff)),12,2)

	Sh2.Cells(25,1)= "ENERGY CONSUMPTION (KWh/kg)"
	Sh2.Cells(25,ff)= doc.numformat(doc.div(Sh2.Cells(24,ff), Sh2.Cells(3,ff)),12,2)


	if ff=2 then
		Call kur_yazdir1(ff)
		cc=0
	else
		Call kur_yazdir(ff, eskitarih)
		ss=ss+1
	end if
	

End Sub
Sub Exceleyazdir_toplam(excelkolon)
	excelkolon = excelkolon + 1
	Sh2.Cells(1, excelkolon) = "CUMMULATIVE OF THIS WEEK"
	Sh2.Cells(1, excelkolon+1) = "GENERAL CUMMULATIVE"

	for c1=2 to 90
		for c2=2 to excelkolon
			if trim(Sh2.cells(c1, c2)) <> "" then
				GTotal(c1) = GTotal(c1) + Sh2.cells(c1, c2) 
			end if
		Next
		for c2=3 to excelkolon
			if trim(Sh2.cells(c1, c2)) <> "" then
				hTotal(c1) = hTotal(c1) + Sh2.cells(c1, c2) 
			end if
		Next
		
		Sh2.Cells(c1,excelkolon)= doc.numformat(hTotal(c1),12,2)
		Sh2.Cells(c1,excelkolon+1)= doc.numformat(gTotal(c1),12,2)
	Next

			Sh2.Cells(5,excelkolon)= "%"&doc.numformat(doc.div(Sh2.Cells(4,excelkolon),Sh2.Cells(2,excelkolon) )*100,12,2)
			Sh2.Cells(5,excelkolon+1)= "%"&doc.numformat(doc.div(Sh2.Cells(4,excelkolon+1),Sh2.Cells(2,excelkolon+1) )*100,12,2)

			Sh2.Cells(13,excelkolon)= "%"&doc.numformat(doc.div(Sh2.Cells(11,excelkolon),Sh2.Cells(12,excelkolon) )*100,12,2)
			Sh2.Cells(13,excelkolon+1)= "%"&doc.numformat(doc.div(Sh2.Cells(11,excelkolon+1),Sh2.Cells(12,excelkolon+1))*100,12,2)

			Sh2.Cells(20,excelkolon)= doc.numformat(doc.div(Sh2.Cells(18,excelkolon),Sh2.Cells(19,excelkolon) ),12,2)
			Sh2.Cells(20,excelkolon+1)= doc.numformat(doc.div(Sh2.Cells(18,excelkolon+1),Sh2.Cells(19,excelkolon+1) ),12,2)

			Sh2.Cells(22,excelkolon)= doc.numformat(doc.div(Sh2.Cells(18,excelkolon),Sh2.Cells(21,excelkolon) ),12,2)
			Sh2.Cells(22,excelkolon+1)= doc.numformat(doc.div(Sh2.Cells(18,excelkolon+1),Sh2.Cells(21,excelkolon+1) ),12,2)

			Sh2.Cells(25,excelkolon)= doc.numformat(doc.div(Sh2.Cells(24,excelkolon),Sh2.Cells(3,excelkolon) ),12,2)
			Sh2.Cells(25,excelkolon+1)= doc.numformat(doc.div(Sh2.Cells(24,excelkolon+1),Sh2.Cells(3,excelkolon+1) ),12,2)
End Sub
Sub CizgiCiz(aralik)
	Sh2.Range(aralik).Select
    excel.Selection.Borders(xlDiagonalDown).LineStyle = xlNone
    excel.Selection.Borders(xlDiagonalUp).LineStyle = xlNone
        excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeLeft).LineStyle = xlContinuous
        excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeLeft).Weight = xlThin
        excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeLeft).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic

        excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeTop).LineStyle = xlContinuous
        excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeTop).Weight = xlThin
        excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeTop).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic

        excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeBottom).LineStyle = xlContinuous
        excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeBottom).Weight = xlThin
        excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeBottom).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic

        excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeRight).LineStyle = xlContinuous
        excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeRight).Weight = xlThin
        excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeRight).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic

        excel.Selection.Borders(xlInsideVertical).LineStyle = xlContinuous
        excel.Selection.Borders(xlInsideVertical).Weight = xlThin
        excel.Selection.Borders(xlInsideVertical).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic

        excel.Selection.Borders(xlInsideHorizontal).LineStyle = xlContinuous
        excel.Selection.Borders(xlInsideHorizontal).Weight = xlThin
        excel.Selection.Borders(xlInsideHorizontal).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
End Sub
Sub Font_belirle()
	excel.Selection.Font.Name = "Arial"
  	excel.Selection.Font.Size = 9	
End Sub
Sub Belirle(aralik)
	Sh2.Range(aralik).Select

        excel.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = xlGeneral
        excel.Selection.VerticalAlignment = xlBottom
        excel.Selection.WrapText = True
        excel.Selection.Orientation = 0
        excel.Selection.AddIndent = False
        excel.Selection.IndentLevel = 0
        excel.Selection.ShrinkToFit = False
        excel.Selection.ReadingOrder = xlContext
        excel.Selection.MergeCells = False

End Sub
Sub Kur_yazdir(ff,Eskitarih)
	Set grup1 = Doc.Select1("_gecust","GECOUST","EVRAKNO='"+"*PUNIT"+"'","",-1)
	for h1=1 to grup1.getreccount
		kur = grup1.KOD(h1)
		ad = grup1.AD(h1)
		if trim(ad)="X" then
				n=n+1
				sh2.cells(26+(n-1)*2, 1)=kur&"- COUNTER INDICATION "
				Dim Kriter
				Kriter=""
				Kriter=Kriter&"CODEFROM='"&kur &"'"
				Kriter=Kriter&" & "
				Kriter=Kriter&"EVRAKNOTARIH='"&eskitarih &"'"

				Set EXC1=Doc.Select1("___EXCRATTccc","EXCRATT",Kriter,"",-1)
				For h2=1 to exc1.getreccount
			
					sh2.cells(26+(n-1)*2, ff)=exc1.kurs_1(h2)
			
					
					tt=Doc.Date_NumToStr(doc.CDateToInt(eskitarih) + 1)
					tt=mid(tt, 7, 4)&"/"&mid(tt,4,2)&"/"&mid(tt,1,2)

				Dim Kriter1
				Kriter1=""
				Kriter1=Kriter1&"CODEFROM='"&kur &"'"
				Kriter1=Kriter1&" & "
				Kriter1=Kriter1&"EVRAKNOTARIH='"&tt &"'"

				Set EXC11=Doc.Select1("___EXCRATTnnnn1","EXCRATT",Kriter1,"",-1)
				For h21=1 to exc11.getreccount
					sh2.cells(27+(n-1)*2, ff)=exc11.kurs_1(h21)-exc1.kurs_1(h2)
				Next
				Next
				

		End If			
	Next

End Sub
Sub Kur_yazdir1(ff)
	
	Set grup1 = Doc.Select1("_gecust","GECOUST","EVRAKNO='"+"*PUNIT"+"'","",-1)
	for h1=1 to grup1.getreccount
		kur = grup1.KOD(h1)
		ad = grup1.AD(h1)
		if trim(ad)="X" then
			cc=cc+1
			Dim Kriter
				Kriter=""
				Kriter=Kriter&"CODEFROM='"&kur &"'"
				Kriter=Kriter&" & "
				Kriter=Kriter&"EVRAKNOTARIH>='"&"2010/01/01" &"'"
				Kriter=Kriter&" & "
				Kriter=Kriter&"EVRAKNOTARIH<'"&LISTEE.RECTARIH11 &"'"

				Set EXC12=Doc.Select1("___EXCRATT2","EXCRATT",Kriter,"",-1)
				For h2=1 to exc12.getreccount
					
					t22=Doc.Date_NumToStr(doc.CDateToInt(exc12.EVRAKNOTARIH(h2)) + 1)
					t22=mid(t22, 7, 4)&"/"&mid(t22,4,2)&"/"&mid(t22,1,2)

					Dim Kriter1
					Kriter1=""
					Kriter1=Kriter1&"CODEFROM='"&kur &"'"
					Kriter1=Kriter1&" & "
					Kriter1=Kriter1&"EVRAKNOTARIH='"&t22 &"'"

					Set EXC123=Doc.Select1("___EXCRATT23","EXCRATT",Kriter1,"",-1)
					for h3 = 1 to exc123.getreccount
						topla = topla + (exc123.KURS_1(h3) - exc12.KURS_1(h2))
					next
				Next
					sh2.cells(27+(cc-1)*2, 1)=kur&"- ENERGY (KWh) "
					sh2.cells(27+(cc-1)*2, ff)=topla
		topla = 0

		End If			
	Next
End Sub
Sub Data_yarat()
	Doc.CreateTableObject "ABCXYZ=SFDC32E","TMPCOPY"
	Set SF = Doc.GetTableObject("ABCXYZ") 
	yenitarih=LISTEE.RECTARIH11

	Set LISTEG=Doc.Select1("___tABLE10XXX","SFDC32E","","",-1)

	for TT=1 to LISTEG.GETRECCOUNT
		t=0
		sorgu = 0
		EVRAKNO = LISTEG.evrakno(tT)
		Set s70t=Doc.Select1("___tABLE10","SFDC32E",""&"EVRAKNO='"&evrakno &"'","",-1)
		t0=Doc.Date_NumToStr(doc.CDateToInt(S70T.RECTARIH1(1)) - 1)
		t1=Doc.Date_NumToStr(doc.CDateToInt(S70T.RECTARIH1(1)) + 1)
		t2=Doc.Date_NumToStr(doc.CDateToInt(S70T.RECTARIH1(1)) + 2)
		t3=Doc.Date_NumToStr(doc.CDateToInt(S70T.RECTARIH1(1)) + 3)
		if S70T.RECTIME1(1) < "09:00" then
			SF.ADDROW
			S=sf.getreccount
			sf.evrakno(s) = S70T.evrakno(1)
			sf.MPSNO(s) = S70T.MPSNO(1)
			sf.RECTYPE(s) = S70T.RECTYPE(1)
			sf.X_T_ISMERKEZI(s) = S70T.X_T_ISMERKEZI(1)
			sf.rectarih1(s) = t0
			sf.rectime1(s) = S70T.rectime1(1)
			if S70T.RECTIME2< "09:00" and s70t.rectarih2 = s70t.rectarih1 then
				sorgu=sorgu+1
				sf.rectarih2(s) = t0
				sf.rectime2(s) = S70T.RECTIME2
			else
				sf.rectarih2(s) = t0
				sf.rectime2(s) = "09:00"
				S70T.rectime1(1) = "09:00"
			end if
			sf.sure(s) = S70T.sure(1)
			T=t+doc.DTDTDif1(S70T.RECTARIH1,S70T.RECTIME1,S70T.RECTARIH1,"09:00")
		end if
		if sorgu =0 then
			fark1=doc.DTDTDif1(S70T.RECTARIH1,S70T.RECTIME1,t1,"09:00")
			t=t+fark1
			IF t >= s70t.sure THEN
				SF.ADDROW
				S=sf.getreccount
				sf.evrakno(s) = S70T.evrakno(1)
				sf.MPSNO(s) = S70T.MPSNO(1)
				sf.RECTYPE(s) = S70T.RECTYPE(1)
				sf.X_T_ISMERKEZI(s) = S70T.X_T_ISMERKEZI(1)
				sf.rectarih1(s) = S70T.RECTARIH1(1)
				sf.rectime1(s) = S70T.rectime1(1)
				sf.rectarih2(s) = S70T.RECTARIH2(1)
				sf.rectime2(s) = S70T.RECTIME2(1)
				sf.sure(s) = S70T.sure(1)
				sorgu=sorgu+1
			ELSE
				SF.ADDROW
				S=sf.getreccount
				sf.evrakno(s) = S70T.evrakno(1)
				sf.MPSNO(s) = S70T.MPSNO(1)
				sf.RECTYPE(s) = S70T.RECTYPE(1)
				sf.X_T_ISMERKEZI(s) = S70T.X_T_ISMERKEZI(1)
				sf.rectarih1(s) = S70T.RECTARIH1(1)
				sf.rectime1(s) = S70T.rectime1(1)
				sf.rectarih2(s) = t1
				sf.rectime2(s) = "09:00"
				sf.sure(s) = S70T.sure(1)
				S70T.rectarih1 = t1
				S70T.rectime1 = "09:00"
			END IF
		end if

		fark1=doc.DTDTDif1(S70T.RECTARIH1,S70T.RECTIME1,t2,"09:00")
		t=t+fark1
		if sorgu = 0 then
			IF t >= s70t.sure THEN
				SF.ADDROW
				S=sf.getreccount
				sf.evrakno(s) = S70T.evrakno(1)
				sf.MPSNO(s) = S70T.MPSNO(1)
				sf.RECTYPE(s) = S70T.RECTYPE(1)
				sf.X_T_ISMERKEZI(s) = S70T.X_T_ISMERKEZI(1)
				sf.rectarih1(s) = S70T.RECTARIH1(1)
				sf.rectime1(s) = S70T.rectime1(1)
				sf.rectarih2(s) = S70T.RECTARIH2(1)
				sf.rectime2(s) = S70T.RECTIME2(1)
				sf.sure(s) = S70T.sure(1)
				sorgu=sorgu+1
			ELSE
				SF.ADDROW
				S=sf.getreccount
				sf.evrakno(s) = S70T.evrakno(1)
				sf.MPSNO(s) = S70T.MPSNO(1)
				sf.RECTYPE(s) = S70T.RECTYPE(1)
				sf.X_T_ISMERKEZI(s) = S70T.X_T_ISMERKEZI(1)
				sf.rectarih1(s) = S70T.RECTARIH1(1)
				sf.rectime1(s) = S70T.rectime1(1)
				sf.rectarih2(s) = t2
				sf.rectime2(s) = "09:00"
				sf.sure(s) = S70T.sure(1)
				S70T.rectarih1 = t2
				S70T.rectime1 = "09:00"
			END IF
		end if

		fark1=doc.DTDTDif1(S70T.RECTARIH1,S70T.RECTIME1,t3,"09:00")
		t=t+fark1
	
		if sorgu = 0 then
			IF t >= s70t.sure THEN
				SF.ADDROW
				S=sf.getreccount
				sf.evrakno(s) = S70T.evrakno(1)
				sf.MPSNO(s) = S70T.MPSNO(1)
				sf.RECTYPE(s) = S70T.RECTYPE(1)
				sf.RECTYPE(s) = S70T.RECTYPE(1)
				sf.X_T_ISMERKEZI(s) = S70T.X_T_ISMERKEZI(1)
				sf.rectarih1(s) = S70T.RECTARIH1(1)
				sf.rectime1(s) = S70T.rectime1(1)
				sf.rectarih2(s) = S70T.RECTARIH2(1)
				sf.rectime2(s) = S70T.RECTIME2(1)
				sf.sure(s) = S70T.sure(1)
			ELSE
				SF.ADDROW
				S=sf.getreccount
				sf.evrakno(s) = S70T.evrakno(1)
				sf.MPSNO(s) = S70T.MPSNO(1)
				sf.RECTYPE(s) = S70T.RECTYPE(1)
				sf.X_T_ISMERKEZI(s) = S70T.X_T_ISMERKEZI(1)
				sf.rectarih1(s) = S70T.RECTARIH1(1)
				sf.rectime1(s) = S70T.rectime1(1)
				sf.rectarih2(s) = t3
				sf.rectime2(s) = "09:00"
				sf.sure(s) = S70T.sure(1)
				S70T.rectarih1 = t3
				S70T.rectime1 = "09:00"
			END IF
			end if
	next

End Sub
Sub gecmis_hesapla()
	mpsno1=""
	eskit = ""
			Dim Kriter
				Kriter=""
				Kriter=Kriter&"CODEFROM='"&"KAAN" &"'"
				Kriter=Kriter&" & "
				Kriter=Kriter&"EVRAKNOTARIH>='"&"2010/01/01" &"'"
				Kriter=Kriter&" & "
				Kriter=Kriter&"EVRAKNOTARIH<'"&LISTEE.RECTARIH11 &"'"

				Set EXC12=Doc.Select1("___EXCRAXTT2","EXCRATT",Kriter,"",-1)
				For h2=1 to exc12.getreccount
					
					t22=Doc.Date_NumToStr(doc.CDateToInt(exc12.EVRAKNOTARIH(h2)) + 1)
					t22=mid(t22, 7, 4)&"/"&mid(t22,4,2)&"/"&mid(t22,1,2)

					Dim Kriter1
					Kriter1=""
					Kriter1=Kriter1&"CODEFROM='"&"KAAN" &"'"
					Kriter1=Kriter1&" & "
					Kriter1=Kriter1&"EVRAKNOTARIH='"&t22 &"'"

					Set EXC123=Doc.Select1("___EXCXRATT23","EXCRATT",Kriter1,"",-1)
					for h3 = 1 to exc123.getreccount
						topla1 = topla1 + ((exc123.KURS_1(h3) - exc12.KURS_1(h2))*120)
					next
				Next
					C(24)=TOPLA1
	For v=1 to sf.getreccount
		if  sf.RECTARIH1(v) >= "2010/01/01" and SF.RECTARIH1(v) < LISTEE.RECTARIH11 then
			if TRim(eskit) <> trim(SF.RECTARIH1(v)) then
				c(12) = c(12) + 96
				eskit= SF.RECTARIH1(v)
				
			end if


			eskitarih="GENERAL CUMMULATIVE OF LAST WEEK"
			input=Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(v),"INPUT",0)-Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(v),"IADE",0)
			farkg=doc.DTDTDif1(SF.RECTARIH1(v),SF.RECTIME1(v), SF.RECTARIH2(v),SF.RECTIME2(v))
			sure = sf.SURE(v)
			C(2)=C(2)+doc.div(input, sure)*farkg
			if trim(SF.X_T_ISMERKEZI(v))<>"MARIS" and ( trim(SF.RECTYPE(v))="DS" or trim(SF.RECTYPE(v))="R" ) then
				C(4)=C(4)+doc.div(input, SF.SURE(v))*farkg
			end if
			mb1=doc.div(Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(v),"MB",0), sure) * farkg
			me1=doc.div(Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(v),"ME",0), sure) * farkg

			tezgah1=SF.X_T_ISMERKEZI(v)
			temizlik = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(v),"PP",0)+Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(v),"PE",0)_
						+Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(v),"HDPE",0)+Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(v),"KIRMA",0)
			C(18)=C(18)+( doc.div(temizlik, sure)* farkg )
			select case trim(tezgah1)
						case "LEISTRITZ"
								C(7) = C(7) + mE1 - mB1
						case "ZSK32K"
								C(8) = C(8) + mE1 - mB1
						case "ZSK32S"
								C(9) = C(9) + mE1 - mB1
						case "MARIS"
								C(10) = C(10) + mE1 - mB1
			end select
			mpsno2=SF.MPSNO(v)
			if trim(mpsno2)<>trim(mpsno1) then
				mpsno1=mpsno2
				C(19)=C(19)+1			
				C(21)=C(21) + 1
				if trim(sf.RECTYPE(v))="P" Then
					C(21)=C(21) + 1
				end if
			end if
			
			
		end if
	next
	c(23) = 0
	excelkolon = excelkolon + 1
	call exceleyazdir(eskitarih, excelkolon)

End Sub
Sub hafta_hesapla()
	mpsno1=""
	for x=1 to 35
		c(x) = 0
		d(x) = 0
	Next
	For v=1 to sf.getreccount
		if sf.RECTARIH1(v) <= LISTEE.RECTARIH12 and sf.RECTARIH1(v) >= "2010/01/01" and SF.RECTARIH1(v) >= LISTEE.RECTARIH11 then
			if trim(yenitarih)<>trim(SF.RECTARIH1(v)) then
				mpsno1=""
				excelkolon=excelkolon +1
				eskitarih=yenitarih
				YENitarih=SF.RECTARIH1(v)
				call exceleyazdir(eskitarih, excelkolon)
				for x=1 to 35
					c(x) = 0
					d(x) = 0
				Next
			end if
			input=Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(v),"INPUT",0)-Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(v),"IADE",0)
			farkg=doc.DTDTDif1(SF.RECTARIH1(v),SF.RECTIME1(v), SF.RECTARIH2(v),SF.RECTIME2(v))
			sure = sf.SURE(v)
			C(2)=C(2)+doc.div(input, sure)*farkg

			if trim(SF.X_T_ISMERKEZI(v))<>"MARIS" and ( trim(SF.RECTYPE(v))="DS" or trim(SF.RECTYPE(v))="R" ) then
				C(4)=C(4)+doc.div(input, sure)*farkg
			end if

			mb1=doc.div(Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(v),"MB",0), sure) * farkg
			me1=doc.div(Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(v),"ME",0), sure) * farkg

			tezgah1=SF.X_T_ISMERKEZI(v)
			temizlik = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(v),"PP",0)+Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(v),"PE",0)_
						+Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(v),"HDPE",0)+Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(v),"KIRMA",0)
			C(18)=C(18)+ doc.div(temizlik, sure)* farkg
			select case trim(tezgah1)
						case "LEISTRITZ"
								C(7) = C(7) + mE1 - mB1
						case "ZSK32K"
								C(8) = C(8) + mE1 - mB1
						case "ZSK32S"
								C(9) = C(9) + mE1 - mB1
						case "MARIS"
								C(10) = C(10) + mE1 - mB1
			end select
			mpsno2=SF.MPSNO(v)
				C(12) = 96

			if trim(mpsno2)<>trim(mpsno1) then
				mpsno1=mpsno2
				C(19)=C(19)+1			
				C(21)=C(21) + 1
				if trim(sf.RECTYPE(v))="P" Then
					C(21)=C(21) + 1
				end if
			end if
		
			Set EXCT = Doc.Select1("EXCFTT","EXCRATT","EVRAKNOTARIH='"+sf.rectarih1(v)+"'","",-1)
			For zz=1 to EXCT.getreccount
				select case TRIM(EXCT.CODEFROM(zz))
							case "KAAN"
								C(23)=EXCT.KURS_1(zz)
				end select

			t22=Doc.Date_NumToStr(doc.CDateToInt(sf.RECTARIH1(v)) + 1)
			t22=mid(t22, 7, 4)&"/"&mid(t22,4,2)&"/"&mid(t22,1,2)

			Set EXCs = Doc.Select1("EXCFTTs","EXCRATT","EVRAKNOTARIH='"+t22+"'","",-1)

			for d1=1 to excs.getreccount
				select case TRIM(EXCs.CODEFROM(d1))
						case "KAAN"
							D(23) = EXCs.kurs_1(d1)
				end select
			Next
			if d(23)> 0  and c(23) >0  then
				C(24) = (D(23) - C(23)) * 120
			end if
			Next

		end if
	next

	excelkolon = excelkolon + 1
	call exceleyazdir(yenitarih, excelkolon)

End Sub

Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	EXCRATT.SafePack()
	Set PB = Doc.Select1("___tABLEGEC","GECOUST","EVRAKNO='*PUNIT'","",-1)	
	For i = 1 to PB.getreccount
		if trim(PB.AD(i)) = "X" or trim(PB.KOD(i)) = "KAAN" Then
			satirno = EXCRATT.addrow()
			EXCRATT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			EXCRATT.CODEFROM(satirno) = PB.KOD(i)
			EXCRATT.CODETO(satirno) = "SAAT"
		End if
	Next
	EXCRATT.SetCurrentRow(1)
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Dim MTable
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=MMPS10E(NOFILE)","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "BARKOD","CHAR",16,0,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "LOTNUMBER","CHAR",12,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SF_MIKTAR","DOUBLE",16,3,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "ELEKALTI","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TRNUM","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
'	MTable.Addfield2 "GRADE","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Setd7FieldEvent "BARKOD","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "BARKOD_AV"

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadMainForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU1",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
End Sub
Sub BARKOD_AV()
	Call FillMTable()
End Sub
Sub FillMTable()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	if trim(MTable.BARKOD) = "" Then Exit Sub

'	MTable.GRADE = ""
	MTable.ELEKALTI = "H"
	MTable.LOTNUMBER = ""
	
	MPSNo = Left(MTable.BARKOD, 10)
	MPSTrNum = Right(MTable.BARKOD, 6)
	Set M10E = Doc.Select1("de2221", "MMPS10E", "EVRAKNO='" & Doc.RightJustify(trim(MPSNo), 10) & "'", "", -1)
	Set M10TM = Doc.Select1("dce2221", "MMPS10TM", "EVRAKNO='" & Doc.RightJustify(trim(MPSNo), 10) & "'&TRNUM='" & MPSTrNum & "'", "", -1)

	Alanlar = M10E.GetFieldNames("")
	if M10E.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		For each Alan in Alanlar
			AlanDegeri = M10E.GetFieldValue(Alan, 1)
			MTable.SetFieldValue Alan, -1, AlanDegeri
		Next
		MTable.MAMULSTOKKODU_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", MTable.MAMULSTOKKODU, "")
		MTable.HAVUZKODU_ACIKLAMA = Doc.GetGecoValue("MPSNO1", "AD", MTable.HAVUZKODU, -1)
		MTable.STATUS_ACIKLAMA = Doc.GetGecoValue("|MPSST", "AD", MTable.STATUS, -1)
		MTable.MUSTERIKODU = M10TM.MUSTERIKODU
		MTable.MUSTERIKODU_CARI00_AD = GetMusteriAdi(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", M10TM.MUSTERIKODU, ""))
		MTable.TRNUM = MPSTrNum
	End if

	MTable.BARKOD = ""
End Sub
Sub ElekAltiEtiketiBas()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	MTable.ELEKALTI = "E"
	
	Doc.RunMacro0 "PFRM7S.ETK-MAMUL", "PrintLabel1"
End Sub
Sub PosetEtiketiBas()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	MTable.ELEKALTI = "H"
	
	Doc.RunMacro0 "PFRM7S.ETK-MAMUL", "PrintLabel1"
End Sub
Sub PaletEtiketiBas()
	Call PaletYarat()
End Sub
Function GetMusteriAdi(MusteriAdi)
	GetMusteriAdi = MusteriAdi
	Parantez = InStr(1, GetMusteriAdi, "(")
	if Parantez <= 1 Then Exit Function
	GetMusteriAdi = Left(MusteriAdi, Parantez - 1)
End Function
Sub PaletYarat()
	Barkod1 = InputBox("Paletteki Ýlk Barkodu Okutunuz")
	Barkod2 = InputBox("Paletteki Son Barkodu Okutunuz")

	if trim(Barkod1) = "" or trim(Barkod2) = "" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Baþlangýç ve Bitiþ barkodlarýný okutmalýsýnýz", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Set d7LB = Doc.Select1("we23fsdfsdf", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD>='" & Barkod1 & "'&KOD<='" & Barkod2 & "'", "", -1)

	if d7LB.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub

	if d7LB.GetRowCount() > 50 Then
		k = Doc.d7MsgBox("Verilen aralýkta " & d7LB.GetRowCount() & " adet torba var. Palet etiketi basmayý onaylýyor musunuz", "Uyarý", MB_YESNO)
		if k <> 6 Then Exit Sub
	End if

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSPL", "")
	Set d7LB1 = Doc1.GetTableObject("D7KIDSLB")
	Set d7PL1 = Doc1.GetTableObject("D7KIDSPL")

	Doc1.New_Voucher

	d7LB1.NOSUBLEVEL = "1"

	For i = 1 to d7LB.GetRowCount()
		if trim(d7LB.REFTEXT01(i)) <> "ELEKALTI" Then
			satirNo = d7PL1.AddRow()
			d7PL1.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			d7PL1.D_ID = D7LB.KOD(i)
		End if
	Next

	nret = Doc1.Save_Voucher()
	if nret = 0 Then
		Doc1.RunMacro0 "PFRM7S.ETK-MAMUL", "PrintLabel"
	End if
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
End Sub
Sub PosetEtiketiIptal()
	BarkodNo = InputBox("Barkodu Okutunuz")
	Set d7PL = Doc.Select1("qw2wersdf", "D7KIDSPL", "KOD='" & BarkodNo & "'", "", -1)
	if d7PL.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Bu programla palet etiketi iptal edilemez. Poþet barkodu okutmalýsýnýz", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Set d7LB = Doc.Select1("qw2wersdf", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & BarkodNo & "'", "", -1)
	if d7LB.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox BarkodNo & " no.lu barkod bulunamadý", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")
	Doc1.Load_Voucher BarkodNo
	nret = Doc1.Delete_Voucher
	if nret = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Barkod iptal edildi", "", MB_OK
	End if
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : SNK10 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : BARKOD-2

Sub Makro1()
	Dim MTable
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=MMPS10E(NOFILE)","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "AMBCODE","CHAR",6,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('GDEF00')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TARGETAMBCODE","CHAR",6,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('GDEF00')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "BARKOD","CHAR",30,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "HAMMADDE_BARKOD","CHAR",30,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "JOBNO","CHAR",16,0,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "KOD","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "KOD_AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "LOTNUMBER","CHAR",12,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SF_MIKTAR","DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "HMD_UNIT","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""

	MTable.Setd7FieldEvent "BARKOD","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "BARKOD_AV"

	MTable.AMBCODE= "HM"
	MTable.TARGETAMBCODE= "IML"

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadMainForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU1",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
End Sub
Sub Makro2()
	Dim MTable2
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE2=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "SF_MIKTAR","DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "HMD_UNIT","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadMainForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU2",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
End Sub
Sub TransferveEtiket()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set MTable2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE2")
	ret = Transfer()
	if ret <> 0 Then Exit Sub
	if ret = 0 Then ret = EtiketBolEtiketYazdir()

	MTable.Empty
	MTable2.Empty
	Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL
End Sub
Function EtiketBolEtiketYazdir()
	EtiketBolEtiketYazdir = -1

	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set MTable2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE2")

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("TRACK25", "")
	Set S01 = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK01")

	S01.D_ID2 = MTable.HAMMADDE_BARKOD
	S01.SF_PAKETMIKTARI = 1
	S01.SF_PAKETICERIGI = MTable2.SF_MIKTAR

	Doc1.RunCommand "CMD_KAYDET"
	Doc1.RunMacro0 "PFRM7S.ETK-KB", "PrintLabel" 
	EtiketBolEtiketYazdir = 0
End Function
Function Transfer()
	Transfer = -1
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set MTable2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE2")

	QStr = "AMBCODE='" & MTable.AMBCODE & "'&KOD='" & MTable.KOD & "'&LOTNUMBER='" & MTable.LOTNUMBER & "'&ALLOC_MPSARTNO='" & MTable.JOBNO & _
						"'&SF_MIKTAR>=" & MTable2.SF_MIKTAR
	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("we2fsdfsdfsd", "STOK01", QStr, "", -1)
	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox trim(MTable.AMBCODE) & " deposunda bu miktarda rezerve hammadde bulunamadý", "", MB_OK
		Exit Function
	End if

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK26", "")
	Set S26E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK26E")
	Set S26T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK26T")

	Doc1.New_Voucher
	S26E.AMBCODE = MTable.AMBCODE
	S26E.TARGETAMBCODE = MTable.TARGETAMBCODE

	satirNo = S26T.AddRow
	S26T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

'	S26T.D_ID = MTable.HAMMADDE_BARKOD
	S26T.KOD = S01.KOD
	S26T.LOTNUMBER = S01.LOTNUMBER
	S26T.SERINO = S01.SERINO
	S26T.SF_MIKTAR = MTable2.SF_MIKTAR
	S26T.SF_SF_UNIT = S01.SF_UNIT
	S26T.FOKOD = S01.FOKOD
	S26T.SF_VRI_VR_C = S01.VRI_VR_C
	S26T.SF_VRI_VR_R1 = S01.VRI_VR_R1
	S26T.SF_VRI_VR_R2 = S01.VRI_VR_R2
	S26T.SF_VRI_VR_R3 = S01.VRI_VR_R3
	S26T.SF_VRI_NUM1 = S01.VRI_NUM1
	S26T.SF_VRI_NUM2 = S01.VRI_NUM2
	S26T.LOCATION1 = S01.LOCATION1
	S26T.LOCATION2 = S01.LOCATION2
	S26T.LOCATION3 = S01.LOCATION3
	S26T.LOCATION4 = S01.LOCATION4
	S26T.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS = S01.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS
	S26T.ALLOC_NONMOVABL = S01.ALLOC_NONMOVABL
	S26T.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE
	S26T.ALLOC_EVRAKNO = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKNO
	S26T.ALLOC_SIPTYPE = S01.ALLOC_SIPTYPE
	S26T.ALLOC_SIPNO = S01.ALLOC_SIPNO
	S26T.ALLOC_SIPARTNO = S01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO
	S26T.ALLOC_MPSEVNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSEVNO
	S26T.ALLOC_MPSARTNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSARTNO

	Transfer = Doc1.Save_Voucher
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Function
Sub Temizle()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	MTable.Empty
	MTable.AMBCODE= "HM"
	MTable.TARGETAMBCODE= "IML"
End Sub
Sub BARKOD_AV()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	if trim(MTable.AMBCODE) = "" or trim(MTable.TARGETAMBCODE) = "" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Veren Depo ve Alan Depo Girilmiþ olmalýdýr", "Dikkat", MB_OK
		MTable.BARKOD = ""
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Len1 = Len("%JB%")
	if Left(trim(MTable.BARKOD), Len1) = "%JB%" Then
		Call MPSBilgileriniDoldur()
	Else
		Call HammaddeBarkodBilgileriniDoldur()
	End if

	if trim(MTable.JOBNO) <> "" and trim(MTable.HAMMADDE_BARKOD) <> "" Then
		Call Makro2()
	End if
End Sub
Sub HammaddeBarkodBilgileriniDoldur()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	if trim(MTable.BARKOD) = "" Then Exit Sub

	Set D7LB = Doc.Select1("swd324fedfs", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & trim(MTable.BARKOD) & "'", "", -1)
	if D7LB.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub

	if trim(MTable.LOTNUMBER) <> "" and trim(MTable.LOTNUMBER) <> trim(D7LB.LOTNUMBER) Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Okutulan hammadde barkodunun lotu transfer edilen lottan farklý", "", MB_OK
	Elseif trim(MTable.KOD) <> "" and trim(MTable.KOD) <> trim(D7LB.SKOD) Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Okutulan hammadde barkodunun stok kodu transfer edilen stok kodundan farklý", "", MB_OK
	Else
		MTable.HAMMADDE_BARKOD = trim(MTable.BARKOD)
		MTable.KOD = D7LB.SKOD
		MTable.KOD_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", D7LB.SKOD, "")
		MTable.LOTNUMBER = D7LB.LOTNUMBER
	End if
	MTable.BARKOD = ""
End Sub
Sub MPSBilgileriniDoldur()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	if trim(MTable.BARKOD) = "" Then Exit Sub
	
	Barkod = Trim(MTable.BARKOD)
	Len1 = Len("%JB%")
	if Len(Barkod) <= Len1 Then Exit Sub

	Barkod = Mid(Barkod, Len1 + 1)
	MPSNo = Left(Barkod, Len(Barkod) - 6)
	JobNo = Doc.RightJustify(trim(Barkod), 16)
	Set M10E = Doc.Select1("de2221", "MMPS10E", "EVRAKNO='" & Doc.RightJustify(trim(MPSNo), 10) & "'", "", -1)
	Set M10TM = Doc.Select1("dce2221", "MMPS10TM", "EVRAKNO='" & Doc.RightJustify(trim(MPSNo), 10) & "'", "", -1)
	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("we23fsdfsd", "MMPS10T", "JOBNO='" & JobNo & "'", "", -1)

	Alanlar = M10E.GetFieldNames("")
	if M10E.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		Set S01 = Doc.Select1("we2fsdfsdfsd", "STOK01", "AMBCODE='" & MTable.AMBCODE & "'&ALLOC_MPSARTNO='" & JobNo & "'", "", -1)
		if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Okutulan barkoda ait hammadde " & trim(MTable.AMBCODE) & " deposunda bulunamadý", "", MB_OK
		Else
			if trim(MTable.LOTNUMBER) <> "" and trim(MTable.LOTNUMBER) <> trim(S01.LOTNUMBER) Then
				Doc.d7MsgBox "Okutulan hammadde barkodunun lotu transfer edilen lottan farklý", "", MB_OK
			Elseif trim(MTable.KOD) <> "" and trim(MTable.KOD) <> trim(S01.KOD) Then
				Doc.d7MsgBox "Okutulan hammadde barkodunun stok kodu transfer edilen stok kodundan farklý", "", MB_OK
			Else
				For each Alan in Alanlar
					AlanDegeri = M10E.GetFieldValue(Alan, 1)
					MTable.SetFieldValue Alan, -1, AlanDegeri
				Next
				MTable.MAMULSTOKKODU_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", MTable.MAMULSTOKKODU, "")
				MTable.HAVUZKODU_ACIKLAMA = Doc.GetGecoValue("MPSNO1", "AD", MTable.HAVUZKODU, -1)
				MTable.STATUS_ACIKLAMA = Doc.GetGecoValue("|MPSST", "AD", MTable.STATUS, -1)
				MTable.MUSTERIKODU = M10TM.MUSTERIKODU
				MTable.MUSTERIKODU_CARI00_AD = GetMusteriAdi(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", M10TM.MUSTERIKODU, ""))
				MTable.JOBNO = JobNo	
				MTable.KOD = S01.KOD
				MTable.KOD_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", S01.KOD, "")
				MTable.LOTNUMBER = S01.LOTNUMBER
				MTable.SF_MIKTAR = S01.SF_MIKTAR
				MTable.HMD_UNIT = S01.SF_UNIT
			End if
		End if
	End if

	MTable.BARKOD = ""
End Sub
Function GetMusteriAdi(MusteriAdi)
	GetMusteriAdi = MusteriAdi
	Parantez = InStr(1, GetMusteriAdi, "(")
	if Parantez <= 1 Then Exit Function
	GetMusteriAdi = Left(MusteriAdi, Parantez - 1)
End Function

Makro Kaynak Kodu

Sub Makro1()
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "HURDA_TURU","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SON_EVRAKTYPE","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SON_EVRAKNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","",""

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadMainForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU1",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK25", "")
	Set Doc2 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK24", "")
End Sub
Sub Wizard01()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	MainOp = MTable.HURDA_TURU
	if MainOp = "K" Then
		Call LabHurdaKirilacakMain()
	Elseif MainOp = "S" Then
		Call LabHurdaSatisMain()
	Elseif MainOp = "T" Then
		Call LabHurdaUretimTemizlikMain()
	Else
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Bir seçenek iþaretlemelisiniz", "Uyarý", MB_OK
	End if
End Sub
Sub LabHurdaKirilacakMain()
	Set MTableT=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("HURDA_TABLE1=STACK","IO")
	MTableT.Addfield2 "HURDA_KODU","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "HURDA_ADI","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "SF_SF_UNIT","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""

	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("HURDA_TABLE2=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "HURDA_MIKTARI","DOUBLE",10,3,"R","",""

	Call HurdaKodlariniYukle()

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadMainForm_Dyn "LABHURDA_KIRMA",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
End Sub
Sub HurdaKodlariniYukle()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("HURDA_TABLE1")
	Set S00 = Doc.Select1("sdf23fsdf", "STOK00", "KOD>='999999 01 01 '&KOD<'999999 01 01zzz'&CODETYPE='D'", "", -1)
	MTableT.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	MTableT.Empty
	For i = 1 to S00.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MTableT.AddRow
		MTableT.HURDA_KODU(satirNo) = S00.KOD(i)
		MTableT.HURDA_ADI(satirNo) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", S00.KOD(i), "")
		MTableT.SF_SF_UNIT(satirNo) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", S00.KOD(i), "")
	Next
	MTableT.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub
Sub LabHurdaKirilacakGiris()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("HURDA_TABLE2")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("HURDA_TABLE1")
	Set MTableM = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")

	if MTable.HURDA_MIKTARI = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Miktar girmelisiniz", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	VerenDepo = "LBA"
	EskiKod = "99999"

	Set S01 = Doc.Select1G("sd34fdfgdfg", "STOK01", "AMBCODE='" & VerenDepo & "'&KOD='" & EskiKod & "'", "", "KOD", -1)
	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox VerenDepo & " deposunda " & trim(EskiKod) & " kodlu malzemeden bu miktarda bulunamadý", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	Elseif S01.SF_MIKTAR < MTable.HURDA_MIKTARI Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox VerenDepo & " deposunda " & trim(EskiKod) & " kodlu malzemeden bu miktarda bulunamadý", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK25", "")
	Set S25E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25E")
	Set S25T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25T")

	Doc1.New_Voucher
	S25E.AMBCODE = VerenDepo
'	S25E.TARGETAMBCODE = "LBA"
	S25E.TARGETAMBCODE = "GAD" '14/09/2010

	satirNo = S25T.AddRow
	S25T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

	S25T.KOD = S01.KOD
	S25T.SF_MIKTAR = MTable.HURDA_MIKTARI
	
	Doc1.RunCommand "HESAPLANMIS_RECETENIN_LOT_SERI_BUL"
	Doc1.RunCommand "SATIR_ESKIBILGILERI_YENIYEKOPYALA_ALL"
	For i = 1 to S25T.GetRowCount()
		S25T.NEWCODE(i) = MTableT.HURDA_KODU
	Next

	ret = Doc1.Save_Voucher
	if ret = 0 Then
		MTableM.SON_EVRAKTYPE = "KD"
		MTableM.SON_EVRAKNO = S25E.EVRAKNO
		Doc1.RunMacro0 "PFRM7S.ETK-HURDA", "PrintLabel"
		Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL
	End if
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub
Sub LabHurdaSatisMain()
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("HURDA_TABLELS=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "HURDA_TURU","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "HURDA_MIKTARI","DOUBLE",10,3,"R","",""

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadMainForm_Dyn "LABHURDA_SATILACAK",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
End Sub
Sub LabHurdaSatilacakGiris()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("HURDA_TABLELS")
	if MTable.HURDA_MIKTARI = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Miktar girmelisiniz", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	if trim(MTable.HURDA_TURU) = "" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Hurda Türü Seçmelisiniz", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	Elseif MTable.HURDA_TURU = "R" Then
		Call LabHurdaSatilacakMaliyetliGiris()
	Elseif MTable.HURDA_TURU = "M" Then
		Call LabHurdaSatilacakMunchyGirisMain()
	Else
		Call LabHurdaSatilacakTakozGiris()
	End if
End Sub
Sub LabHurdaSatilacakMaliyetliGiris()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("HURDA_TABLELS")
	Set MTableM = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	HurdaKodu = "999999 01 02 004"
	HurdaKodu = Doc.LeftJustify(HurdaKodu, 24)

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK24", "")
	Set S24E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK24E")
	Set S24T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK24T")

	Doc1.New_Voucher
	S24E.AMBCODE = "HR"

	satirNo = S24T.AddRow
	S24T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

	S24T.KOD = HurdaKodu
	S24T.LOTNUMBER = ""
	S24T.GIREN_MIKTAR = MTable.HURDA_MIKTARI
	S24T.SF_SF_UNIT = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", HurdaKodu, "")
	
	ret = Doc1.Save_Voucher
	if ret = 0 Then
		MTableM.SON_EVRAKTYPE = "DT"
		MTableM.SON_EVRAKNO = S24E.EVRAKNO
		Doc1.RunMacro0 "PFRM7S.ETK-HURDA", "PrintLabel"
		Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL
	End if
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher

End Sub
Sub LabHurdaSatilacakTakozGiris()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("HURDA_TABLELS")
	Set MTableM = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")

	if trim(MTable.HURDA_TURU) = "E" Then
		HurdaKodu = "999999 01 02 002"
	Elseif trim(MTable.HURDA_TURU) = "G" Then
		HurdaKodu = "999999 01 02 003"
	Elseif trim(MTable.HURDA_TURU) = "T" Then
		HurdaKodu = "999999 01 02 001"
	End if

	VerenDepo = "LBA"
	EskiKod = "99999"

	Set S01 = Doc.Select1G("sd34fdfgdfg", "STOK01", "AMBCODE='" & VerenDepo & "'&KOD='" & EskiKod & "'", "", "KOD", -1)
	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox VerenDepo & " deposunda " & trim(EskiKod) & " kodlu malzemeden bu miktarda bulunamadý", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	Elseif S01.SF_MIKTAR < MTable.HURDA_MIKTARI Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox VerenDepo & " deposunda " & trim(EskiKod) & " kodlu malzemeden bu miktarda bulunamadý", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK25", "")
	Set S25E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25E")
	Set S25T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25T")

	Doc1.New_Voucher
	S25E.AMBCODE = VerenDepo
	S25E.TARGETAMBCODE = "HR"

	satirNo = S25T.AddRow
	S25T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

	S25T.KOD = S01.KOD
	S25T.SF_MIKTAR = MTable.HURDA_MIKTARI
	
	Doc1.RunCommand "HESAPLANMIS_RECETENIN_LOT_SERI_BUL"
	Doc1.RunCommand "SATIR_ESKIBILGILERI_YENIYEKOPYALA_ALL"
	For i = 1 to S25T.GetRowCount()
		S25T.NEWCODE(i) = HurdaKodu
	Next

	ret = Doc1.Save_Voucher
	if ret = 0 Then
		MTableM.SON_EVRAKTYPE = "KD"
		MTableM.SON_EVRAKNO = S25E.EVRAKNO
		Doc1.RunMacro0 "PFRM7S.ETK-HURDA", "PrintLabel"
		Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL
	End if
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub

	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE_LAB_UT=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "HURDA_MIKTARI","DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""

End Sub
Sub UretimTemizlikOK()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_LAB_UT")
	Set MTableM = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	HurdaKodu = "999999 01 03 001"

	VerenDepo = "LBA"
	EskiKod = "99999"
	Set S01 = Doc.Select1G("sd34fdfgdfg", "STOK01", "AMBCODE='" & VerenDepo & "'&KOD='" & EskiKod & "'", "", "KOD", -1)
	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox VerenDepo & " deposunda " & trim(EskiKod) & " kodlu malzemeden bu miktarda bulunamadý", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	Elseif S01.SF_MIKTAR < MTable.HURDA_MIKTARI Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox VerenDepo & " deposunda " & trim(EskiKod) & " kodlu malzemeden bu miktarda bulunamadý", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK25", "")
	Set S25E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25E")
	Set S25T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25T")

	Doc1.New_Voucher
	S25E.AMBCODE = VerenDepo
	S25E.TARGETAMBCODE = "UTE"

	satirNo = S25T.AddRow
	S25T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

	S25T.KOD = S01.KOD
	S25T.SF_MIKTAR = MTable.HURDA_MIKTARI
	
	Doc1.RunCommand "HESAPLANMIS_RECETENIN_LOT_SERI_BUL"
	Doc1.RunCommand "SATIR_ESKIBILGILERI_YENIYEKOPYALA_ALL"
	For i = 1 to S25T.GetRowCount()
		S25T.NEWCODE(i) = HurdaKodu
	Next

	ret = Doc1.Save_Voucher
	if ret = 0 Then
		MTableM.SON_EVRAKTYPE = "KD"
		MTableM.SON_EVRAKNO = S25E.EVRAKNO
		Doc1.RunMacro0 "PFRM7S.ETK-HURDA", "PrintLabel"
		Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL
	End if
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub
Sub LabHurdaSatilacakMunchyGirisMain()
	Set MTableT=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("HURDA_TABLEM1=STACK","IO")
	MTableT.Addfield2 "HURDA_KODU","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "HURDA_ADI","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "SF_SF_UNIT","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""

	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("HURDA_TABLEM2=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "HURDA_MIKTARI","DOUBLE",10,3,"R","",""

	Call MunchyGirisKodlariniYukle()

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadMainForm_Dyn "LABHURDA_MUNCHY",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
End Sub
Sub MunchyGirisKodlariniYukle()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("HURDA_TABLEM1")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("HURDA_TABLEM2")
	Set MTableS = Doc.GetTableObject("HURDA_TABLELS")
	MTable.HURDA_MIKTARI = MTableS.HURDA_MIKTARI

	Set S00 = Doc.Select1("sdf23fsdf", "STOK00", "KOD>='999999 01 04'&KOD<'999999 01 04zzz'&CODETYPE='D'", "", -1)
	MTableT.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	MTableT.Empty
	For i = 1 to S00.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MTableT.AddRow
		MTableT.HURDA_KODU(satirNo) = S00.KOD(i)
		MTableT.HURDA_ADI(satirNo) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", S00.KOD(i), "")
		MTableT.SF_SF_UNIT(satirNo) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", S00.KOD(i), "")
	Next
	MTableT.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub
Sub LabHurdaMunchyGiris()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("HURDA_TABLEM2")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("HURDA_TABLEM1")
	Set MTableM = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")

	if MTable.HURDA_MIKTARI = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Miktar girmelisiniz", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	EskiKod = MTableT.HURDA_KODU
	YeniKod = "999999 01 02 005"
	VerenDepo = "MNCHY"

	Set S01 = Doc.Select1G("sd34fdfgdfg", "STOK01", "AMBCODE='" & VerenDepo & "'&KOD='" & EskiKod & "'", "", "KOD", -1)
	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox VerenDepo & " deposunda " & trim(EskiKod) & " kodlu malzemeden bu miktarda bulunamadý", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	Elseif S01.SF_MIKTAR < MTable.HURDA_MIKTARI Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox VerenDepo & " deposunda " & trim(EskiKod) & " kodlu malzemeden bu miktarda bulunamadý", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK25", "")
	Set S25E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25E")
	Set S25T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25T")

	Doc1.New_Voucher
	S25E.AMBCODE = VerenDepo
	S25E.TARGETAMBCODE = "HR"

	satirNo = S25T.AddRow
	S25T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

	S25T.KOD = S01.KOD
	S25T.SF_MIKTAR = MTable.HURDA_MIKTARI
	
	Doc1.RunCommand "HESAPLANMIS_RECETENIN_LOT_SERI_BUL"
	Doc1.RunCommand "SATIR_ESKIBILGILERI_YENIYEKOPYALA_ALL"
	For i = 1 to S25T.GetRowCount()
		S25T.NEWCODE(i) = YeniKod
	Next

	ret = Doc1.Save_Voucher
	if ret = 0 Then
		MTableM.SON_EVRAKTYPE = "KD"
		MTableM.SON_EVRAKNO = S25E.EVRAKNO
		Doc1.RunMacro0 "PFRM7S.ETK-HURDA", "PrintLabel"
		Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL
	End if
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub

ENDFORM

	LTEXT LEFT=E+3P TOP=+0 D=HURDA_TABLEM1.SF_SF_UNIT BORDER=ETCHED BCKCOL=RGB_232_232_232

	RESTORELOCATION XXX
	BUTTON LEFT=+0 TOP=E+3p WIDTH=14 HEIGHT=2 CAPTION="Tamam" ID="MT_RUNMACRO_AFTERCALC('MAKR7S.@@@BUMAKRO@@@','LabHurdaMunchyGiris')"
	BUTTON LEFT=E+5 TOP=+0 WIDTH=10 HEIGHT=2 CAPTION="Ýptal" ID=ID_APP_OK
ENDFORM

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : SNK10 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : BARKOD-4
Hangi Tablo için : 
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Üretim Hurda
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "HURDA_TURU","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SON_EVRAKTYPE","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SON_EVRAKNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","",""

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadMainForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU1",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK25", "")
	Set Doc2 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK24", "")
End Sub
Sub Wizard01()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	MainOp = MTable.HURDA_TURU
	if MainOp = "K" Then
		Call UretimHurdaKirilacakMain()
	Elseif MainOp = "S" Then
		Call UretimHurdaSatisMain()
	Else
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Bir seçenek iþaretlemelisiniz", "Uyarý", MB_OK
	End if
End Sub
Sub UretimHurdaKirilacakMain()
	Set MTableT=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("HURDA_TABLE1=STACK","IO")
	MTableT.Addfield2 "HURDA_KODU","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "HURDA_ADI","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "SF_SF_UNIT","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""

	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("HURDA_TABLE2=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "HURDA_MIKTARI","DOUBLE",10,3,"R","",""

	Call KirilacakHurdaKodlariniYukle()

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "URETIMHURDA_KIRMA",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
End Sub
Sub KirilacakHurdaKodlariniYukle()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("HURDA_TABLE1")
	Set S00 = Doc.Select1("sdf23fsdf", "STOK00", "KOD>='999999 02 01'&KOD<'999999 02 01zzz'&CODETYPE='D'", "", -1)
	MTableT.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	MTableT.Empty
	For i = 1 to S00.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MTableT.AddRow
		MTableT.HURDA_KODU(satirNo) = S00.KOD(i)
		MTableT.HURDA_ADI(satirNo) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", S00.KOD(i), "")
		MTableT.SF_SF_UNIT(satirNo) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", S00.KOD(i), "")
	Next
	MTableT.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub
Sub UretimHurdaKirilacakGiris()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("HURDA_TABLE2")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("HURDA_TABLE1")
	Set MTableM = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")

	if MTable.HURDA_MIKTARI = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Miktar girmelisiniz", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	VerenDepo = "UTE"
	EskiKod = "99999"
	Set S01 = Doc.Select1G("sd34fdfgdfg", "STOK01", "AMBCODE='" & VerenDepo & "'&KOD='" & EskiKod & "'", "", "KOD", -1)
	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox VerenDepo & " deposunda " & trim(EskiKod) & " kodlu malzemeden bu miktarda bulunamadý", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	Elseif S01.SF_MIKTAR < MTable.HURDA_MIKTARI Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox VerenDepo & " deposunda " & trim(EskiKod) & " kodlu malzemeden bu miktarda bulunamadý", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK25", "")
	Set S25E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25E")
	Set S25T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25T")

	Doc1.New_Voucher
	S25E.AMBCODE = VerenDepo
	S25E.TARGETAMBCODE = "UTE"

	satirNo = S25T.AddRow
	S25T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

	S25T.KOD = S01.KOD
	S25T.SF_MIKTAR = MTable.HURDA_MIKTARI
	
	Doc1.RunCommand "HESAPLANMIS_RECETENIN_LOT_SERI_BUL"
	Doc1.RunCommand "SATIR_ESKIBILGILERI_YENIYEKOPYALA_ALL"
	For i = 1 to S25T.GetRowCount()
		S25T.NEWCODE(i) = MTableT.HURDA_KODU
	Next

	ret = Doc1.Save_Voucher
	if ret = 0 Then
		MTableM.SON_EVRAKTYPE = "KD"
		MTableM.SON_EVRAKNO = S25E.EVRAKNO
		Doc1.RunMacro0 "PFRM7S.ETK-HURDA", "PrintLabel"
		Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL
	End if
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub
Sub UretimHurdaSatisMain()
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("HURDA_TABLELS=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "HURDA_TURU","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "HURDA_MIKTARI","DOUBLE",10,3,"R","",""

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "URETIMHURDA_SATILACAK",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
End Sub
Sub UretimHurdaSatilacakGiris()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("HURDA_TABLELS")
	if MTable.HURDA_MIKTARI = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Miktar girmelisiniz", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	if trim(MTable.HURDA_TURU) = "" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Hurda Türü Seçmelisiniz", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	Elseif MTable.HURDA_TURU = "T" Then
		Call UretimHurdaSatilacakTakozGiris()
	Else
		Call UretimHurdaSatilacakMaliyetliGiris()
	End if
End Sub
Sub UretimHurdaSatilacakMaliyetliGiris()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("HURDA_TABLELS")
	Set MTableM = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")

	if trim(MTable.HURDA_TURU) = "U" Then 'Üretim Firesi
		HurdaKodu = "999999 02 02 002"
	Elseif trim(MTable.HURDA_TURU) = "P" Then 'premix
		HurdaKodu = "999999 02 02 003"
	Elseif trim(MTable.HURDA_TURU) = "R" Then 'naylon torba
		HurdaKodu = "999999 02 02 004"
	Elseif trim(MTable.HURDA_TURU) = "G" Then 'Granül
		HurdaKodu = "999999 02 02 005"
	End if	
	HurdaKodu = Doc.LeftJustify(HurdaKodu, 24)

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK24", "")
	Set S24E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK24E")
	Set S24T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK24T")

	Doc1.New_Voucher
	S24E.AMBCODE = "HR"

	satirNo = S24T.AddRow
	S24T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

	S24T.KOD = HurdaKodu
	S24T.LOTNUMBER = ""
	S24T.GIREN_MIKTAR = MTable.HURDA_MIKTARI
	S24T.SF_SF_UNIT = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", HurdaKodu, "")
	
	ret = Doc1.Save_Voucher
	if ret = 0 Then
		MTableM.SON_EVRAKTYPE = "DT"
		MTableM.SON_EVRAKNO = S24E.EVRAKNO
		Doc1.RunMacro0 "PFRM7S.ETK-HURDA", "PrintLabel"
		Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL
	End if
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher

End Sub
Sub UretimHurdaSatilacakTakozGiris()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("HURDA_TABLELS")
	Set MTableM = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")

	HurdaKodu = "999999 02 02 001"

	VerenDepo = "UTE"
	EskiKod = "99999"
	Set S01 = Doc.Select1G("sd34fdfgdfg", "STOK01", "AMBCODE='" & VerenDepo & "'&KOD='" & EskiKod & "'", "", "KOD", -1)
	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox VerenDepo & " deposunda " & trim(EskiKod) & " kodlu malzemeden bu miktarda bulunamadý", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	Elseif S01.SF_MIKTAR < MTable.HURDA_MIKTARI Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox VerenDepo & " deposunda " & trim(EskiKod) & " kodlu malzemeden bu miktarda bulunamadý", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK25", "")
	Set S25E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25E")
	Set S25T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25T")

	Doc1.New_Voucher
	S25E.AMBCODE = VerenDepo
	S25E.TARGETAMBCODE = "HR"

	satirNo = S25T.AddRow
	S25T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

	S25T.SF_MIKTAR = MTable.HURDA_MIKTARI
	
	Doc1.RunCommand "HESAPLANMIS_RECETENIN_LOT_SERI_BUL"
	Doc1.RunCommand "SATIR_ESKIBILGILERI_YENIYEKOPYALA_ALL"
	For i = 1 to S25T.GetRowCount()
		S25T.NEWCODE(i) = HurdaKodu
	Next

	ret = Doc1.Save_Voucher
	if ret = 0 Then
		MTableM.SON_EVRAKTYPE = "KD"
		MTableM.SON_EVRAKNO = S25E.EVRAKNO
		Doc1.RunMacro0 "PFRM7S.ETK-HURDA", "PrintLabel"
		Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL
	End if
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher

Sub Wizard01()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	MainOp = MTable.HURDA_TURU
	if MainOp = "" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Bir seçenek iþaretlemelisiniz", "Uyarý", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	Call LabKirma()
End Sub
Sub LabKirma()
	Set MTableT=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("HURDA_TABLE1=STACK","IO")
	MTableT.Addfield2 "HURDA_KODU","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "HURDA_ADI","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "SF_SF_UNIT","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""

	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("HURDA_TABLE2=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "HURDA_MIKTARI","DOUBLE",10,3,"R","",""

	Call KodlariYukle()

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "LAB_KIRMA",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
End Sub
Sub KodlariYukle()
	Set MTableM = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("HURDA_TABLE1")
	if trim(MTableM.HURDA_TURU) = "K" Then
		Set S00 = Doc.Select1("sdf23fsdf", "STOK00", "KOD>='999999 01 04 '&KOD<'999999 01 04zzz'&CODETYPE='D'", "", -1)
	Elseif trim(MTableM.HURDA_TURU) = "M" Then
		Set S00 = Doc.Select1("sdf23fsdf", "STOK00", "KOD>='999999 01 05'&KOD<'999999 01 05zzzz'&CODETYPE='D'", "", -1)
	End if

	MTableT.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	MTableT.Empty
	For i = 1 to S00.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MTableT.AddRow
		MTableT.HURDA_KODU(satirNo) = S00.KOD(i)
		MTableT.HURDA_ADI(satirNo) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", S00.KOD(i), "")
		MTableT.SF_SF_UNIT(satirNo) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", S00.KOD(i), "")
	Next
	MTableT.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub
Sub KirmaGirisi()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("HURDA_TABLE2")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("HURDA_TABLE1")
	Set MTableM = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")

	if MTable.HURDA_MIKTARI = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Miktar girmelisiniz", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	HedefDepo = "GAD"
	YeniKod = MTableT.HURDA_KODU
	if trim(MTableM.HURDA_TURU) = "K" Then
		EskiKod = Left(YeniKod, 11) & "1" & Mid(YeniKod, 13)
'		VerenDepo = "LBA"  '14/09/2010
		VerenDepo = "GAD"  '14/09/2010
	Else
		VerenDepo = "MNCHY"  '14/09/2010
		EskiKod = Left(YeniKod, 11) & "4" & Mid(YeniKod, 13)
	End if

	
	Set S01 = Doc.Select1G("sd34fdfgdfg", "STOK01", "AMBCODE='" & VerenDepo & "'&KOD='" & EskiKod & "'", "", "KOD", -1)
	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox VerenDepo & " deposunda " & trim(EskiKod) & " kodlu malzemeden bu miktarda bulunamadý", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	Elseif S01.SF_MIKTAR < MTable.HURDA_MIKTARI Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox VerenDepo & " deposunda " & trim(EskiKod) & " kodlu malzemeden bu miktarda bulunamadý", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK25", "")
	Set S25E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25E")
	Set S25T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25T")

	Doc1.New_Voucher

	S25E.AMBCODE = VerenDepo
	S25E.TARGETAMBCODE = HedefDepo

	satirNo = S25T.AddRow
	S25T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

	S25T.KOD = S01.KOD
	S25T.SF_MIKTAR = MTable.HURDA_MIKTARI
	
	Doc1.RunCommand "HESAPLANMIS_RECETENIN_LOT_SERI_BUL"
	Doc1.RunCommand "SATIR_ESKIBILGILERI_YENIYEKOPYALA_ALL"
	For i = 1 to S25T.GetRowCount()
		S25T.NEWCODE(i) = MTableT.HURDA_KODU
	Next
	ret = Doc1.Save_Voucher
	if ret = 0 Then
		MTableM.SON_EVRAKTYPE = "KD"
		MTableM.SON_EVRAKNO = S25E.EVRAKNO
		Doc1.RunMacro0 "PFRM7S.ETK-HURDA", "PrintLabel"
		Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL
	End if
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub

SET AUTOFORMSIZE_RIGHTMARGIN=1

SET FIXEDPITCH_EDIT=1

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : SNK10 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : BARKOD-6

Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Set MTableT=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("HURDA_TABLE1=STACK","IO")
	MTableT.Addfield2 "HURDA_KODU","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "HURDA_ADI","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "SF_SF_UNIT","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""

	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("HURDA_TABLE2=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "HURDA_MIKTARI","DOUBLE",10,3,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SON_EVRAKTYPE","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SON_EVRAKNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","",""

	Call KodlariYukle()

'	Dim Par
'	Set Par=Nothing
'	Doc.LoadMainForm_Dyn "URETIM_KIRMA",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
End Sub
Sub KodlariYukle()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("HURDA_TABLE1")
	Set S00 = Doc.Select1("sdf23fsdf", "STOK00", "KOD>='999999 02 03'&KOD<'999999 02 03ZZZZ'&CODETYPE='D'", "", -1)

	MTableT.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	MTableT.Empty
	For i = 1 to S00.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MTableT.AddRow
		MTableT.HURDA_KODU(satirNo) = S00.KOD(i)
		MTableT.HURDA_ADI(satirNo) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", S00.KOD(i), "")
		MTableT.SF_SF_UNIT(satirNo) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", S00.KOD(i), "")
	Next
	MTableT.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub
Sub KirimaGirisi()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("HURDA_TABLE2")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("HURDA_TABLE1")
	Set MTableM = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")

	if MTable.HURDA_MIKTARI = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Miktar girmelisiniz", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	YeniKod = MTableT.HURDA_KODU
	if Mid(YeniKod, 12, 1) = "3" Then
		EskiKod = Left(YeniKod, 11) & "1" & Mid(YeniKod, 13)
	End if
	VerenDepo = "UTE"

	Set S01 = Doc.Select1G("sd34fdfgdfg", "STOK01", "AMBCODE='" & VerenDepo & "'&KOD='" & EskiKod & "'", "", "KOD", -1)
	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox VerenDepo & " deposunda " & trim(EskiKod) & " kodlu malzemeden bu miktarda bulunamadý", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	Elseif S01.SF_MIKTAR < MTable.HURDA_MIKTARI Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox VerenDepo & " deposunda " & trim(EskiKod) & " kodlu malzemeden bu miktarda bulunamadý", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK25", "")
	Set S25E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25E")
	Set S25T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25T")

	Doc1.New_Voucher
	S25E.AMBCODE = VerenDepo
	S25E.TARGETAMBCODE = "UTE"

	satirNo = S25T.AddRow
	S25T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

	S25T.KOD = S01.KOD
	S25T.SF_MIKTAR = MTable.HURDA_MIKTARI
	
	Doc1.RunCommand "HESAPLANMIS_RECETENIN_LOT_SERI_BUL"
	Doc1.RunCommand "SATIR_ESKIBILGILERI_YENIYEKOPYALA_ALL"
	For i = 1 to S25T.GetRowCount()
		S25T.NEWCODE(i) = MTableT.HURDA_KODU
	Next

	ret = Doc1.Save_Voucher
	if ret = 0 Then
		MTableM.SON_EVRAKTYPE = "KD"
		MTableM.SON_EVRAKNO = S25E.EVRAKNO
		Doc1.RunMacro0 "PFRM7S.ETK-HURDA", "PrintLabel"
'		Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL
	End if
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "PREMIX_ISLEM","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "MPSNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","F2=DO_BRW('MMPS10E')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "LOTNUMBER","CHAR",12,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "MAMULSTOKKODU","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "MAMULSTOKKODU_AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "PREMIX_KODU","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "UAGAC_FOY_NO","CHAR",10,0,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR","DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SF_SF_UNIT","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "PREMIX_ORANI","DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""

	MTable.Setd7FieldEvent "MPSNO","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "MPSNO_AV"

'	Dim Par
'	Set Par=Nothing
'	Doc.LoadMainForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU1",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK00", "")
	Set Doc2 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("BOMU01", "")
End Sub
Sub Wizard01()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	MainOp = MTable.PREMIX_ISLEM
	if trim(MainOp) = "" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Bir seçenek iþaretlemelisiniz", "Uyarý", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	if MainOp = "G" Then
		Call PremixGirisMain()
	Else
		Call PremixCikisMain()
	End if
End Sub
Sub PremixGirisMain()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set MTableT = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET=MMPS10T(NOFILE)","IO")
	MTableT.Addfield2 "PREMIX_MIKTARI","DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
'	MTableT.Addfield2 "HURDA_ADI","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
'	MTableT.Addfield2 "SF_SF_UNIT","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Setd7FieldEvent "PREMIX_MIKTARI","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "PREMIX_MIKTARI_AV"
	MTable.Empty

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "PREMIX_GIRIS",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
End Sub
Sub MPSNO_AV()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")

	MpsNo = MTable.MPSNO
	MTableT.Empty
	MTable.MAMULSTOKKODU = ""
	MTable.MAMULSTOKKODU_AD = ""
	MTable.LOTNUMBER = ""
	MTable.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR = 0
	MTable.SF_SF_UNIT = ""

	if trim(MTable.MPSNO) = "" Then Exit Sub
	Set M10T	= Doc.Select1("sdf23ffdf34f", "MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO='" & MTable.MPSNO & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='H'", "", -1)
	if M10T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	LotNo = Doc.GetUDFValue("MMPS10E", MTable.MPSNO, "PARTI", 0)
	if trim(LotNo) = "" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "MPS KTA 'sýna Parti No bilgisi girilmemiþ", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	MTable.LOTNUMBER = LotNo
	
	MTable.MAMULSTOKKODU = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10E", "MAMULSTOKKODU", MTable.MPSNO, "")
	MTable.MAMULSTOKKODU_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", MTable.MAMULSTOKKODU, "")
	MTable.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10E", "SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR", MTable.MPSNO, "")
	MTable.SF_SF_UNIT = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10E", "SF_SF_UNIT", MTable.MPSNO, "")

	MTableT.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	For i = 1 to M10T.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MTableT.AddRow
		MTableT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		MTableT.R_KAYNAKKODU = M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i)
		MTableT.R_KAYNAKKODU_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i), "")
		MTableT.KAYNAK_BIRIM = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i), "")
		MTableT.R_MIKTAR0 = M10T.R_MIKTAR0(i)
	Next
	MTableT.SetCurrentRow(1)
	MTableT.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub
Sub PremixGirisi()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")

	if trim(MTable.MPSNO) = "" or trim(MTable.MAMULSTOKKODU) = "" Then Exit Sub
	For i = 1 to MTableT.GetRowCount()
		ToplamPremixMiktari = ToplamPremixMiktari + MTableT.PREMIX_MIKTARI(i)
		if MTableT.PREMIX_MIKTARI(i) > MTableT.R_MIKTAR0(i) Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox i & ". satýr, Premix miktarý Ýþ Emrinde Kullanýlacak miktardan Fazla olamaz", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		End if
	Next
	if ToplamPremixMiktari = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Premix miktarlarýný girmelisiniz", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	StokKodu = "P" & trim(MTable.MAMULSTOKKODU) & "-" & trim(MTable.MPSNO)
	Set S00 = Doc.Select1("sf34fsdf", "STOK00", "KOD>='" & StokKodu & "'&KOD<='" & StokKodu  & "zzzz'", "<KOD", 1)
	if S00.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		StokKodu = StokKodu & "-0"
	Else
		if PremixCikisGirilmemis(S00.KOD) Then Exit Sub
		StokKodu = Doc.StrIncrement(trim(S00.KOD))
	End if

	if Len(trim(StokKodu)) > 24 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox StokKodu & " stok kodu yaratýlamýyor", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	ret = StokKartiniYarat(StokKodu)
	if ret <> 0 Then Exit Sub

	ret = UrunAgaciYarat(StokKodu)
	if ret <> 0 Then Exit Sub
	Doc.d7MsgBox "Ýþlem Tamamlandý", "", MB_OK
	'MTable.Empty
	'Doc.Run_App_Cancel
End Sub
Function StokKartiniYarat(StokKodu)
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK00", "")
	Set S00 = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK00")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	
	Doc1.Load_Voucher StokKodu
	S00.AD = "Premix - " & trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", MTable.MAMULSTOKKODU, ""))
	S00.CODETYPE = "D"
	S00.IUNIT = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", MTable.MAMULSTOKKODU, "")
	StokKartiniYarat = Doc1.Save_Voucher
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Function
Function UrunAgaciYarat(StokKodu)
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("BOMU01", "")
	Set B01E = Doc1.GetTableObject("BOMU01E")
	Set B01T = Doc1.GetTableObject("BOMU01T")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")

	Set B1E = Doc.Select1("sd23fsdfsdf", "BOMU01E", "MAMULCODE='" & StokKodu & "'&AKTIF_PASIF='*'", "", -1)
	if B1E.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		Doc1.Load_Voucher B1E.EVRAKNO
		B01E.AKTIF_PASIF=" "
		Doc1.Save_Voucher
		Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	End if

	Doc1.New_Voucher
	B01E.AKTIF_PASIF = "*"
	B01E.MAMULCODE = StokKodu
	For i = 1 to MTableT.GetRowCount()
		ToplamPremixMiktari = ToplamPremixMiktari + MTableT.PREMIX_MIKTARI(i)

		satirNo = B01T.AddRow()
		B01T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		B01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE = "H"
		B01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE = MTableT.R_KAYNAKKODU(i)
		B01T.BOMREC_KAYNAK0 = MTableT.PREMIX_MIKTARI(i)
	Next
	B01E.MAMULMIKTAR = ToplamPremixMiktari
	UrunAgaciYarat = Doc1.Save_Voucher
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Function
Sub PremixCikisMain()
	Set MTable1=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE1=STACK","IO")
	MTable1.Addfield2 "MPSNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","F2=DO_BRW('MMPS10E')",""
	MTable1.Addfield2 "MPSNO2","CHAR",10,0,"R","F2=DO_BRW('MMPS10E')",""
	MTable1.Addfield2 "LOTNUMBER","CHAR",12,0,"L","",""
	MTable1.Addfield2 "MAMULSTOKKODU","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTable1.Addfield2 "MAMULSTOKKODU_AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable1.Addfield2 "PREMIX_KODU","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTable1.Addfield2 "UAGAC_FOY_NO","CHAR",10,0,"R","",""
	MTable1.Addfield2 "UAGAC_MIKTARI","DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTable1.Addfield2 "PREMIX_MIKTARI","DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTable1.Addfield2 "SF_SF_UNIT","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
	
	MTable1.Setd7FieldEvent "MPSNO","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "MPSNO_AV1"

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadMainForm_Dyn "PREMIX_CIKIS",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
	Set Doc2 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("BOMU01", "")
End Sub
Sub MPSNO_AV1()
	Set MTable1 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE1")

	MpsNo = MTable1.MPSNO
	MTable1.Empty
	MTable1.MPSNO2 = MpsNo

	if trim(MTable1.MPSNO2) = "" Then Exit Sub
	LotNo = Doc.GetUDFValue("MMPS10E", MTable1.MPSNO2, "PARTI", 0)
	if trim(LotNo) = "" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "MPS KTA 'sýna Parti No bilgisi girilmemiþ", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	MTable1.LOTNUMBER = LotNo
	
	MTable1.MAMULSTOKKODU = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10E", "MAMULSTOKKODU", MTable1.MPSNO2, "")
	MTable1.MAMULSTOKKODU_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", MTable1.MAMULSTOKKODU, "")
	Call GetLastPremixStokKodu()
End Sub

Function PremixCikisGirilmemis(StokKodu)
	PremixCikisGirilmemis = False
	Set B1E = Doc.Select1("sd23fsdfsdf", "BOMU01E", "MAMULCODE='" & StokKodu & "'&AKTIF_PASIF='*'", "", -1)
	if B1E.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Call UrunAgaciYarat(StokKodu)
		PremixCikisGirilmemis = True
	Else
		PremixCikisMiktari = Doc.GetUDFValue("BOMU01E", B1E.EVRAKNO, "PREMIX_CKS", 0)
		if Doc.DRound(PremixCikisMiktari) = 0 Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Bu MPS için daha önce yaratýlan " & trim(StokKodu) & " kodlu premixin çýkýþ aðýrlýðý girilmemiþ", _
								"Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
			PremixCikisGirilmemis = True			
		End if
	End if
End Function
Sub GetLastPremixStokKodu()
	Set MTable1 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE1")

	StokKodu = "P" & trim(MTable1.MAMULSTOKKODU) & "-" & trim(MTable1.MPSNO2) & "-"
	Set S00 = Doc.Select1("sf34fsdf", "STOK00", "KOD>='" & StokKodu & "'&KOD<='" & StokKodu  & "zzzz'", "<KOD", 1)
	if S00.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Girilen MPS 'e ait premix giriþi bulunamadý", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		MTable1.Empty
	Else
		Set B1E = Doc.Select1("sd23fsdfsdf", "BOMU01E", "MAMULCODE='" & S00.KOD & "'&AKTIF_PASIF='*'", "", -1)
		if B1E.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Girilen MPS 'e ait açýk premix giriþi bulunamadý", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
			MTable1.Empty
		Else
			PremixCikisMiktari = Doc.GetUDFValue("BOMU01E", B1E.EVRAKNO, "PREMIX_CKS", 0)
			if Doc.DRound(PremixCikisMiktari) > 0 Then
				ret = Doc.d7MsgBox("Girilen MPS 'e ait açýk premix giriþi bulunamadý. Son premix bilgilerini getirmek ister misiniz?", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_YESNO)
				if ret <> 6 Then
					MTable1.Empty
				Else
					MTable1.PREMIX_KODU = S00.KOD
					MTable1.UAGAC_FOY_NO = B1E.EVRAKNO
					MTable1.UAGAC_MIKTARI = B1E.MAMULMIKTAR
					MTable1.SF_SF_UNIT = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", S00.KOD, "")
				End if
			Else
				MTable1.PREMIX_KODU = S00.KOD
				MTable1.UAGAC_FOY_NO = B1E.EVRAKNO
				MTable1.UAGAC_MIKTARI = B1E.MAMULMIKTAR
				MTable1.SF_SF_UNIT = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", S00.KOD, "")
			End if
		End if
	End if
End Sub
Sub PremixCikisi()
	Set MTable1 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE1")
	if trim(MTable1.UAGAC_FOY_NO) = "" or trim(MTable1.PREMIX_KODU) = "" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Premix kodu ve Ürün Aðacý Föy No eksik", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	if MTable1.PREMIX_MIKTARI = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Premix çýkýþ miktarý girilmemiþ", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub		
	End if

	Set Doc2 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("BOMU01", "")

	ret = Doc2.UFLD00_CreateEditTable("xxx","BOMU01E", MTable1.UAGAC_FOY_NO)
	Set KTA = Doc2.GetTableObject("xxx")
	KTA.PREMIX_CKS = MTable1.PREMIX_MIKTARI
	KTA.Exec "SAVE"
	Doc.d7MsgBox "Ýþlem Tamamlandý", "", MB_OK
	'MTable1.Empty
	'Doc.Run_App_Cancel
End Sub
Sub PREMIX_MIKTARI_AV()
	Set MAKROTABLE = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set MTableT = Doc.Select2G("sdf3fsdf", "MAKROTABLET", "PREMIX_MIKTARI>0", "", "KAYNAK_BIRIM",-1)
	if MTableT.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		PremixMiktari = 0
	Else
		For i = 1 to MTableT.GetRowCount()
			PremixMiktari = PremixMiktari + MTableT.PREMIX_MIKTARI(i)
		Next
	End if
	MAKROTABLE.PREMIX_ORANI = Doc.Round(Doc.Div(PremixMiktari, MAKROTABLE.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR) * 100, 3)
End Sub
