Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : SNK10 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : BARKOD-8
Hangi Tablo için : 
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Üretime Ýlave Hammadde Çýkýþý (MPS Ýçin Transfer Fiþi)
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "BARKOD","CHAR",30,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "JOBNO","CHAR",16,0,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "MPS_ICIN_TRANSFER","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Setd7FieldEvent "BARKOD","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "BARKOD_AV"

	Set MSTCK = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK")
	if Doc.IsBlank(MSTCK) Then
		Set MSTCK=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK=STACK","IO")
		MSTCK.Addfield2 "STOK83E_EVRAKNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","",""
		MSTCK.Addfield2 "STOK83T_TRNUM","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
		MSTCK.Addfield2 "JOBNO","CHAR",16,0,"R","",""		
	End if
	
	Set STACK_PDA=Doc.CreateTableObject("STACK_PDA=STACK","IO")
	STACK_PDA.Addfield2 "PROPT","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	STACK_PDA.Addfield2 "AUTO_ADD","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	STACK_PDA.PROPT = "1"
	
	Set STOK01_PDA=Doc.CreateTableObject("STOK01_PDA=STOK01(NOFILE)","IO")
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION1","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION2","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION3","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION4","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SF_PAKETICERIGI","DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SF_MIKTAR_KALAN","DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SAYAC","DOUBLE",12,0,"R","",""

	Set STOK83T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83T")
	STOK83T.Addfield2 "USR_D_ID","CHAR",30,0,"L","",""
End Sub
Sub TransfereBasla()
	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadMainForm_Dyn "STOK83_M",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
End Sub
Sub BARKOD_AV()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set STACK_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STACK_PDA")
	Set STOK83T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83T")	
	if STACK_PDA.PROPT = "2" Then
		Call Cikart()
		MTable.BARKOD = ""
	Else
		Call EkleOp()
	End if
End Sub
Sub Cikart()
	Set STOK83T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83T")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")

	Set S83T = Doc.Select2("sdfw4fsdfsdfwqsd", "STOK83T", "USR_D_ID='" & MTable.BARKOD & "'", "", -1)
	if S83T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Barkod daha önce okutulmamýþ", "", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	satirNo = Doc.CDbl(S83T.SRNUM)
	STOK83T.DeleteRow_NOUI(satirNo)	
	STOK83T.SafePack()
	STOK01_PDA.Empty
	STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = STOK83T.GetRowCount()
End Sub
Sub EkleOp()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set STOK83T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83T")

	if Left(MTable.BARKOD, 4) = "%JB%" Then
		MTable.JOBNO = Mid(MTable.BARKOD, 5)
		MTable.BARKOD = ""
		STOK01_PDA.Empty
		STOK83T.SafePack()
		STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = STOK83T.GetRowCount()
		STOK01_PDA.KOD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "R_KAYNAKKODU", MTable.JOBNO, "JOBNO")
		STOK01_PDA.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", STOK01_PDA.KOD, "")
	Else
		if trim(MTable.JOBNO) = "" Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Önce Ýþ No barkodunu okutmalýsýnýz", "", MB_OK
			MTable.BARKOD = ""
			Exit Sub
		End if
		StokKodu = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("D7KIDSLB", "SKOD", MTable.BARKOD, "")
		if trim(StokKodu) <> trim(STOK01_PDA.KOD) Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Okutulan malzeme, okutulan Ýþ No 'ya " & vbcrlf & "baðlý malzemeden farklý", "Malzeme Hatalý", MB_OK
			MTable.BARKOD = ""
			Exit Sub			
		End if
		Call Ekle()

		'STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI = GetTRMiktar()
		
		MTable.BARKOD = ""
	End if
End Sub
Sub Ekle()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set STOK83T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83T")

	Set STACK_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STACK_PDA")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set d7LB = Doc.Select1("sefw4fdsf", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & MTable.BARKOD & "'", "", -1)

	STOK01_PDA.D_ID = MTable.BARKOD
	STOK01_PDA.KOD= D7LB.SKOD
	STOK01_PDA.KOD_STOK00_AD= Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", D7LB.SKOD, "")
	STOK01_PDA.LOTNUMBER = D7LB.LOTNUMBER
	STOK01_PDA.SERINO = D7LB.SERINO
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R1 = D7LB.VRI_VR_R1
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R2 = D7LB.VRI_VR_R2
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R3 = D7LB.VRI_VR_R3
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_NUM1 = D7LB.VRI_NUM1
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_NUM2 = D7LB.VRI_NUM2
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_C = D7LB.VRI_VR_C
	STOK01_PDA.SF_UNIT = D7LB.SF_UNIT
	STOK01_PDA.FOKOD = D7LB.FOKOD
	STOK01_PDA.SF_MIKTAR = D7LB.SF_BAKIYE

	if STACK_PDA.AUTO_ADD = "E" Then
		Call cmdEkle()
	End if
End Sub
Sub cmdEkle()
	Set MAKROTABLE = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set STOK83T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83T")

	STOK83T.SafePack()

	Set S83T2 = Doc.Select2("dsf34fdfgdfg345", "STOK83T", "USR_D_ID='" & MAKROTABLE.BARKOD & "'", "", -1)
	if S83T2.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Bu barkod daha önce okutulmuþ", "Ýþlem Ýptal Edildi", MB_OK
		STOK01_PDA.Empty
		STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = STOK83T.GetRowCount()
		Exit Sub
	End if

	satirNo = STOK83T.AddRow()
	STOK83T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

	STOK83T.USR_D_ID = STOK01_PDA.D_ID

	STOK83T.JOBNO = MAKROTABLE.JOBNO
	STOK83T.ALANAMBARDASREZ = "E"
	STOK83T.SF_MIKTAR = STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI
	STOK83T.SF_SF_UNIT = STOK01_PDA.SF_UNIT

	STOK83T.KOD = STOK01_PDA.KOD
	STOK83T.LOTNUMBER = STOK01_PDA.LOTNUMBER
	STOK83T.SERINO = STOK01_PDA.SERINO
	STOK83T.FOKOD = STOK01_PDA.FOKOD
	STOK83T.SF_VRI_VR_C = STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_C
	STOK83T.SF_VRI_VR_R1 = STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R1
	STOK83T.SF_VRI_VR_R2 = STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R2
	STOK83T.SF_VRI_VR_R3 = STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R3
	STOK83T.SF_VRI_NUM1 = STOK01_PDA.VRI_NUM1
	STOK83T.SF_VRI_NUM2 = STOK01_PDA.VRI_NUM2
	STOK83T.LOCATION1 = STOK01_PDA.LOCATION1
	STOK83T.LOCATION2 = STOK01_PDA.LOCATION2
	STOK83T.LOCATION3 = STOK01_PDA.LOCATION3
	STOK83T.LOCATION4 = STOK01_PDA.LOCATION4
	STOK83T.LOCATION_NEW1 = STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION1
	STOK83T.LOCATION_NEW2 = STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION2
	STOK83T.LOCATION_NEW3 = STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION3
	STOK83T.LOCATION_NEW4 = STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION4
	STOK83T.OALLOC_SYSSTATUS = STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS
	STOK83T.OALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE
	STOK83T.OALLOC_EVRAKNO = STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_EVRAKNO
	STOK83T.OALLOC_SIPTYPE = STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_SIPTYPE
	STOK83T.OALLOC_SIPNO = STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_SIPNO
	STOK83T.OALLOC_SIPARTNO = STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_SIPARTNO
	STOK83T.OALLOC_MPSEVNO = STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_MPSEVNO
	STOK83T.OALLOC_MPSARTNO = STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_MPSARTNO

	STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = STOK83T.GetRowCount()
End Sub
Sub cmdKaydet()
	Doc.Save_Voucher
	Set MAKROTABLE = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	MAKROTABLE.Empty
	STOK01_PDA.Empty
End Sub
Sub EvrakSil()
	Set STOK83E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83E")
	k = Doc.d7MsgBox(trim(STOK83E.EVRAKNO) & " no.lu MPS Ýçin Hammadde Transfer/Ýade Fiþi Silinsin mi?", "", MB_YESNO) 
	if k <> 6 Then Exit Sub
	Doc.Delete_Voucher
	Set MAKROTABLE = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	MAKROTABLE.Empty
	STOK01_PDA.Empty
End Sub
Function GetTRMiktar()
	GetTRMiktar = 0
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set STOK83T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83T")

	if trim(MTable.JOBNO) = "" Then Exit Function

	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("sf3fsdef3f", "MMPS10T", "JOBNO='" & MTable.JOBNO & "'", "", -1)
	if M10T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function

	TransferBakiyesi = M10T.R_MIKTART - M10T.R_HM_TRANSFER_EDILEN + R_HM_IADE_EDILEN - R_HM_ODONEMNET
	TransferBakiyesi = TransferBakiyesi - GetBuEvrakSecilenMik()
	if TransferBakiyesi < 0 Then TransferBakiyesi = 0

	if STOK01_PDA.SF_MIKTAR < TransferBakiyesi Then
		GetTRMiktar = STOK01_PDA.SF_MIKTAR
	Else
		GetTRMiktar = TransferBakiyesi
	End if
End Function
Function GetBuEvrakSecilenMik()
	GetBuEvrakSecilenMik = 0
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set STOK83E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83E")
	Set STOK83T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83T")
	Set MSTCK = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK")

	if Doc.IsBlank(MSTCK) Then Exit Function
	if MSTCK.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function

	For i = 1 to STOK83T.GetRowCount()
		QStr = "STOK83E_EVRAKNO='" & STOK83E.EVRAKNO & "'&STOK83T_TRNUM='" & STOK83T.TRNUM(i) & "'"
		Set MST = Doc.Select2("sdfw4ffg", "MAKROTABLET_STACK", QStr, "", -1)
		if MST.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			GetBuEvrakSecilenMik = GetBuEvrakSecilenMik + STOK83T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		End if
	Next
End Function
Sub Nerede()
	Set MSTCK=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("STOK01_PDA_LIST=STOK01(NOFILE)","IO")

	Call StokGoster()

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadMainForm_Dyn "NEREDE",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par	
End Sub
Sub StokGoster()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set STOK01_PDA_LIST = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA_LIST")
	Set STOK83E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83E")

	STOK01_PDA_LIST.Empty

	if trim(STOK01_PDA.KOD) = "" Then Exit Sub

	QStr = "KOD='" & STOK01_PDA.KOD & "'"
	if trim(STOK83E.AMBCODE) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & "AMBCODE='" & STOK83E.AMBCODE & "'"
	End if
	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("sdfw3ff4f4", "STOK01", QStr, "", -1)

	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	
	For i = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = STOK01_PDA_LIST.AddRow()
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

		STOK01_PDA_LIST.AMBCODE = S01.AMBCODE(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.LOCATION1 = S01.LOCATION1(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.LOCATION2 = S01.LOCATION2(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.LOCATION3 = S01.LOCATION3(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.LOCATION4 = S01.LOCATION4(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.LOTNUMBER = S01.LOTNUMBER(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.SERINO = S01.SERINO(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.VRI_VR_R1 = S01.VRI_VR_R1(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.VRI_VR_R2 = S01.VRI_VR_R2(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.VRI_VR_R3 = S01.VRI_VR_R3(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.VRI_NUM1 = S01.VRI_NUM1(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.VRI_NUM2 = S01.VRI_NUM2(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.VRI_VR_C = S01.VRI_VR_C(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.SF_MIKTAR = S01.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.SF_UNIT = S01.SF_UNIT(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.FOKOD = S01.FOKOD(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS = S01.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_EVRAKNO = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_SIPTYPE = S01.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_SIPNO = S01.ALLOC_SIPNO(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_SIPARTNO = S01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_MPSEVNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_MPSARTNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(i)
	Next
	STOK01_PDA_LIST.SetCurrentRow(1)
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : SNK10 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : BARKOD-9
Hangi Tablo için : 
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Üretimden Hammadde Ýadesi (MPS Ýçin Transfer Fiþi)
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")
	Set Doc2 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK25", "")

	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "BARKOD","CHAR",30,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "JOBNO","CHAR",16,0,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "MPS_ICIN_TRANSFER","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "STOK25_EVRAKNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","",""

	MTable.Setd7FieldEvent "BARKOD","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "BARKOD_AV"

	Set MSTCK = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK")
	if Doc.IsBlank(MSTCK) Then
		Set MSTCK=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK=STACK","IO")
		MSTCK.Addfield2 "STOK83E_EVRAKNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","",""
		MSTCK.Addfield2 "STOK83T_TRNUM","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
		MSTCK.Addfield2 "JOBNO","CHAR",16,0,"R","",""		
	End if
	
	Set STACK_PDA=Doc.CreateTableObject("STACK_PDA=STACK","IO")
	STACK_PDA.Addfield2 "PROPT","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	STACK_PDA.Addfield2 "AUTO_ADD","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	STACK_PDA.PROPT = "1"
	
	Set STOK01_PDA=Doc.CreateTableObject("STOK01_PDA=STOK01(NOFILE)","IO")
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION1","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION2","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION3","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION4","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SF_PAKETICERIGI","DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SF_MIKTAR_KALAN","DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SAYAC","DOUBLE",12,0,"R","",""

	Set STOK83T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83T")
	STOK83T.Addfield2 "USR_D_ID","CHAR",30,0,"L","",""
	STOK83T.Addfield2 "YENI_LOTNUMBER","CHAR",12,0,"L","",""
End Sub
Sub TransfereBasla()
	Set STOK83E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83E")	
	STOK83E.MPSGIRISCIKISTYPE = "-"

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadMainForm_Dyn "STOK83_M",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
End Sub
Sub BARKOD_AV()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set STACK_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STACK_PDA")
	Set STOK83T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83T")	
	if STACK_PDA.PROPT = "2" Then
		Call Cikart()
		MTable.BARKOD = ""
	Else
		Call EkleOp()
	End if
End Sub
Sub Cikart()
	Set STOK83T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83T")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")

	Set S83T = Doc.Select2("sdfw4fsdfsdfwqsd", "STOK83T", "USR_D_ID='" & MTable.BARKOD & "'", "", -1)
	if S83T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Barkod daha önce okutulmamýþ", "", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	satirNo = Doc.CDbl(S83T.SRNUM)
	STOK83T.DeleteRow_NOUI(satirNo)	
	STOK83T.SafePack()
	STOK01_PDA.Empty
	STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = STOK83T.GetRowCount()
End Sub
Sub EkleOp()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set STOK83T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83T")

	if Left(MTable.BARKOD, 4) = "%JB%" Then
		MTable.JOBNO = Mid(MTable.BARKOD, 5)
		MTable.BARKOD = ""
		STOK01_PDA.Empty
		STOK83T.SafePack()
		STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = STOK83T.GetRowCount()
		STOK01_PDA.KOD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "R_KAYNAKKODU", MTable.JOBNO, "JOBNO")
		STOK01_PDA.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", STOK01_PDA.KOD, "")
		Call LotSeriBul()
	Else
		if trim(MTable.JOBNO) = "" Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Önce Ýþ No barkodunu okutmalýsýnýz", "", MB_OK
			MTable.BARKOD = ""
			Exit Sub
		End if
		StokKodu = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("D7KIDSLB", "SKOD", MTable.BARKOD, "")
		if trim(StokKodu) <> trim(STOK01_PDA.KOD) Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Okutulan malzeme, okutulan Ýþ No 'ya " & vbcrlf & "baðlý malzemeden farklý", "Malzeme Hatalý", MB_OK
			MTable.BARKOD = ""
			Exit Sub			
		End if
		Call Ekle()

		'STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI = GetTRMiktar()
		
		MTable.BARKOD = ""
	End if
End Sub
Sub LotSeriBul()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set STOK83E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83E")

	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("sdf342fsdfs4gg", "STOK01", "AMBCODE='" & STOK83E.AMBCODE & "'&ALLOC_MPSARTNO='" & MTable.JOBNO & "'", "", -1)
	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox trim(STOK83E.AMBCODE) & " deposunda bu iþ için rezerve edilmiþ hammadde bulunamadý", "", MB_OK
	Elseif S01.GetRowCount() = 1 Then
		STOK01_PDA.AMBCODE = S01.AMBCODE
		STOK01_PDA.LOCATION1 = S01.LOCATION1
		STOK01_PDA.LOCATION2 = S01.LOCATION2
		STOK01_PDA.LOCATION3 = S01.LOCATION3
		STOK01_PDA.LOCATION4 = S01.LOCATION4
		STOK01_PDA.LOTNUMBER = S01.LOTNUMBER
		STOK01_PDA.SERINO = S01.SERINO
		STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R1 = S01.VRI_VR_R1
		STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R2 = S01.VRI_VR_R2
		STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R3 = S01.VRI_VR_R3
		STOK01_PDA.VRI_NUM1 = S01.VRI_NUM1
		STOK01_PDA.VRI_NUM2 = S01.VRI_NUM2
		STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_C = S01.VRI_VR_C
		STOK01_PDA.SF_MIKTAR = S01.SF_MIKTAR
		STOK01_PDA.SF_UNIT = S01.SF_UNIT
		STOK01_PDA.FOKOD = S01.FOKOD
		STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS = S01.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS
		STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE
		STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_EVRAKNO = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKNO
		STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_SIPTYPE = S01.ALLOC_SIPTYPE
		STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_SIPNO = S01.ALLOC_SIPNO
		STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_SIPARTNO = S01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO
		STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_MPSEVNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSEVNO
		STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_MPSARTNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSARTNO
	Else
		Call LotSecimEkraniGetir(S01)
	End If
End Sub
Sub LotSecimEkraniGetir(S01)
	Set STOK01_LIST = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("STOK01_LIST=STOK01(NOFILE)","IO")
	STOK01_LIST.Empty
	S01.Sort("SF_MIKTAR")
	For i = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = STOK01_LIST.AddRow()
		STOK01_LIST.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

		STOK01_LIST.AMBCODE = S01.AMBCODE(i)
		STOK01_LIST.LOCATION1 = S01.LOCATION1(i)
		STOK01_LIST.LOCATION2 = S01.LOCATION2(i)
		STOK01_LIST.LOCATION3 = S01.LOCATION3(i)
		STOK01_LIST.LOCATION4 = S01.LOCATION4(i)
		STOK01_LIST.LOTNUMBER = S01.LOTNUMBER(i)
		STOK01_LIST.SERINO = S01.SERINO(i)
		STOK01_LIST.VRI_VR_R1 = S01.VRI_VR_R1(i)
		STOK01_LIST.VRI_VR_R2 = S01.VRI_VR_R2(i)
		STOK01_LIST.VRI_VR_R3 = S01.VRI_VR_R3(i)
		STOK01_LIST.VRI_NUM1 = S01.VRI_NUM1(i)
		STOK01_LIST.VRI_NUM2 = S01.VRI_NUM2(i)
		STOK01_LIST.VRI_VR_C = S01.VRI_VR_C(i)
		STOK01_LIST.SF_MIKTAR = S01.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		STOK01_LIST.SF_UNIT = S01.SF_UNIT(i)
		STOK01_LIST.FOKOD = S01.FOKOD(i)
		STOK01_LIST.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS = S01.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(i)
		STOK01_LIST.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(i)
		STOK01_LIST.ALLOC_EVRAKNO = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(i)
		STOK01_LIST.ALLOC_SIPTYPE = S01.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(i)
		STOK01_LIST.ALLOC_SIPNO = S01.ALLOC_SIPNO(i)
		STOK01_LIST.ALLOC_SIPARTNO = S01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i)
		STOK01_LIST.ALLOC_MPSEVNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(i)
		STOK01_LIST.ALLOC_MPSARTNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(i)
	Next
	STOK01_LIST.SetCurrentRow(1)
	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadMainForm_Dyn "LOT_LIST",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
End Sub
Sub cmdListedenEkle()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set MAKROTABLE = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")

	Set S01_LIST = Doc.Select2("wef342fsdfs", "STOK01_LIST", "SELECTED='E'", "", -1)
	if S01_LIST.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Ýade alýnacak lotlarý seçmelisiniz", "", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	if STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Ýade alýnacak miktar girilmemiþ", "", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End If
	Set STOK83T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83T")

	SatirBasinaMiktar = Doc.DRound(Doc.Div(STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI, S01_LIST.GetRowCount))
	
	Kalan = STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI
	For i = 1 to S01_LIST.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = STOK83T.AddRow()
		STOK83T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

		STOK83T.JOBNO = MAKROTABLE.JOBNO
		STOK83T.ALANAMBARDASREZ = "H"
		STOK83T.SF_SF_UNIT = S01_LIST.SF_UNIT(i)
		if i = S01_LIST.GetRowCount() Then
			EklenecekMiktar = Kalan
		Else
			if S01_LIST.SF_MIKTAR(i) < SatirBasinaMiktar Then
				EklenecekMiktar = S01_LIST.SF_MIKTAR(i)
				SatirBasinaMiktar = Doc.DRound(Doc.Div(Kalan - EklenecekMiktar, S01_LIST.GetRowCount() - i))
			Else
				EklenecekMiktar = SatirBasinaMiktar
			End if
		End if
		Kalan = Kalan - EklenecekMiktar

		STOK83T.SF_MIKTAR = EklenecekMiktar
		STOK83T.KOD = S01_LIST.KOD(i)
		STOK83T.LOTNUMBER = S01_LIST.LOTNUMBER(i)
		STOK83T.YENI_LOTNUMBER = "KARISIM"
		STOK83T.SERINO = S01_LIST.SERINO(i)
		STOK83T.FOKOD = S01_LIST.FOKOD(i)
		STOK83T.SF_VRI_VR_C = S01_LIST.VRI_VR_C(i)
		STOK83T.SF_VRI_VR_R1 = S01_LIST.VRI_VR_R1(i)
		STOK83T.SF_VRI_VR_R2 = S01_LIST.VRI_VR_R2(i)
		STOK83T.SF_VRI_VR_R3 = S01_LIST.VRI_VR_R3(i)
		STOK83T.SF_VRI_NUM1 = S01_LIST.VRI_NUM1(i)
		STOK83T.SF_VRI_NUM2 = S01_LIST.VRI_NUM2(i)
		STOK83T.LOCATION1 = S01_LIST.LOCATION1(i)
		STOK83T.LOCATION2 = S01_LIST.LOCATION2(i)
		STOK83T.LOCATION3 = S01_LIST.LOCATION3(i)
		STOK83T.LOCATION4 = S01_LIST.LOCATION4(i)

		STOK83T.LOCATION_NEW1 = STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION1
		STOK83T.LOCATION_NEW2 = STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION2
		STOK83T.LOCATION_NEW3 = STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION3
		STOK83T.LOCATION_NEW4 = STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION4

		STOK83T.OALLOC_SYSSTATUS = S01_LIST.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(i)
		STOK83T.OALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = S01_LIST.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(i)
		STOK83T.OALLOC_EVRAKNO = S01_LIST.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(i)
		STOK83T.OALLOC_SIPTYPE = S01_LIST.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(i)
		STOK83T.OALLOC_SIPNO = S01_LIST.ALLOC_SIPNO(i)
		STOK83T.OALLOC_SIPARTNO = S01_LIST.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i)
		STOK83T.OALLOC_MPSEVNO = S01_LIST.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(i)
		STOK83T.OALLOC_MPSARTNO = S01_LIST.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(i)

	Next

	Doc.RUN_APP_OK
	MAKROTABLE.Empty
	STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = STOK83T.GetRowCount()
End Sub
Sub Ekle()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set STOK83T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83T")

	Set STACK_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STACK_PDA")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set d7LB = Doc.Select1("sefw4fdsf", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & MTable.BARKOD & "'", "", -1)

	STOK01_PDA.D_ID = MTable.BARKOD
	STOK01_PDA.KOD= D7LB.SKOD
	STOK01_PDA.KOD_STOK00_AD= Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", D7LB.SKOD, "")
	STOK01_PDA.LOTNUMBER = D7LB.LOTNUMBER
	STOK01_PDA.SERINO = D7LB.SERINO
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R1 = D7LB.VRI_VR_R1
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R2 = D7LB.VRI_VR_R2
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R3 = D7LB.VRI_VR_R3
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_NUM1 = D7LB.VRI_NUM1
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_NUM2 = D7LB.VRI_NUM2
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_C = D7LB.VRI_VR_C
	STOK01_PDA.SF_UNIT = D7LB.SF_UNIT
	STOK01_PDA.FOKOD = D7LB.FOKOD
	STOK01_PDA.SF_MIKTAR = D7LB.SF_BAKIYE

	if STACK_PDA.AUTO_ADD = "E" Then
		Call cmdEkle()
	End if
End Sub
Sub cmdEkle()
	Set MAKROTABLE = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set STOK83T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83T")

	STOK83T.SafePack()

	Set S83T2 = Doc.Select2("dsf34fdfgdfg345", "STOK83T", "USR_D_ID='" & MAKROTABLE.BARKOD & "'", "", -1)
	if S83T2.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Bu barkod daha önce okutulmuþ", "Ýþlem Ýptal Edildi", MB_OK
		STOK01_PDA.Empty
		STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = STOK83T.GetRowCount()
		Exit Sub
	End if

	satirNo = STOK83T.AddRow()
	STOK83T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

	STOK83T.USR_D_ID = STOK01_PDA.D_ID

	STOK83T.JOBNO = MAKROTABLE.JOBNO
	STOK83T.ALANAMBARDASREZ = "H"
	STOK83T.SF_MIKTAR = STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI
	STOK83T.SF_SF_UNIT = STOK01_PDA.SF_UNIT

	STOK83T.KOD = STOK01_PDA.KOD
	STOK83T.LOTNUMBER = STOK01_PDA.LOTNUMBER
	STOK83T.SERINO = STOK01_PDA.SERINO
	STOK83T.FOKOD = STOK01_PDA.FOKOD
	STOK83T.SF_VRI_VR_C = STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_C
	STOK83T.SF_VRI_VR_R1 = STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R1
	STOK83T.SF_VRI_VR_R2 = STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R2
	STOK83T.SF_VRI_VR_R3 = STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R3
	STOK83T.SF_VRI_NUM1 = STOK01_PDA.VRI_NUM1
	STOK83T.SF_VRI_NUM2 = STOK01_PDA.VRI_NUM2
	STOK83T.LOCATION1 = STOK01_PDA.LOCATION1
	STOK83T.LOCATION2 = STOK01_PDA.LOCATION2
	STOK83T.LOCATION3 = STOK01_PDA.LOCATION3
	STOK83T.LOCATION4 = STOK01_PDA.LOCATION4

	STOK83T.LOCATION_NEW1 = STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION1
	STOK83T.LOCATION_NEW2 = STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION2
	STOK83T.LOCATION_NEW3 = STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION3
	STOK83T.LOCATION_NEW4 = STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION4

	STOK83T.OALLOC_SYSSTATUS = STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS
	STOK83T.OALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE
	STOK83T.OALLOC_EVRAKNO = STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_EVRAKNO
	STOK83T.OALLOC_SIPTYPE = STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_SIPTYPE
	STOK83T.OALLOC_SIPNO = STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_SIPNO
	STOK83T.OALLOC_SIPARTNO = STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_SIPARTNO
	STOK83T.OALLOC_MPSEVNO = STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_MPSEVNO
	STOK83T.OALLOC_MPSARTNO = STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_MPSARTNO

	STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = STOK83T.GetRowCount()
End Sub
Sub cmdKaydet()
	Doc.Save_Voucher
	Call KodLotSeriTransferFisiYarat()
	Set MAKROTABLE = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	TrFisNo = MAKROTABLE.STOK25_EVRAKNO
	MAKROTABLE.Empty
	MAKROTABLE.STOK25_EVRAKNO = TrFisNo
	STOK01_PDA.Empty
End Sub
Sub EvrakSil()
	Set STOK83E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83E")
	k = Doc.d7MsgBox(trim(STOK83E.EVRAKNO) & " no.lu MPS Ýçin Hammadde Transfer/Ýade Fiþi Silinsin mi?", "", MB_YESNO) 
	if k <> 6 Then Exit Sub

	Set MAKROTABLE = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	if trim(MAKROTABLE.STOK25_EVRAKNO) <> "" Then
		Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK25", "")
		Doc1.Load_Voucher MAKROTABLE.STOK25_EVRAKNO
		Doc1.Delete_Voucher
	End if
	Doc.Delete_Voucher
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	MAKROTABLE.Empty
	STOK01_PDA.Empty
End Sub
Function GetTRMiktar()
	GetTRMiktar = 0
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set STOK83T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83T")

	if trim(MTable.JOBNO) = "" Then Exit Function

	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("sf3fsdef3f", "MMPS10T", "JOBNO='" & MTable.JOBNO & "'", "", -1)
	if M10T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function

	TransferBakiyesi = M10T.R_MIKTART - M10T.R_HM_TRANSFER_EDILEN + R_HM_IADE_EDILEN - R_HM_ODONEMNET
	TransferBakiyesi = TransferBakiyesi - GetBuEvrakSecilenMik()
	if TransferBakiyesi < 0 Then TransferBakiyesi = 0

	if STOK01_PDA.SF_MIKTAR < TransferBakiyesi Then
		GetTRMiktar = STOK01_PDA.SF_MIKTAR
	Else
		GetTRMiktar = TransferBakiyesi
	End if
End Function
Function GetBuEvrakSecilenMik()
	GetBuEvrakSecilenMik = 0
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set STOK83E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83E")
	Set STOK83T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83T")
	Set MSTCK = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK")

	if Doc.IsBlank(MSTCK) Then Exit Function
	if MSTCK.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function

	For i = 1 to STOK83T.GetRowCount()
		QStr = "STOK83E_EVRAKNO='" & STOK83E.EVRAKNO & "'&STOK83T_TRNUM='" & STOK83T.TRNUM(i) & "'"
		Set MST = Doc.Select2("sdfw4ffg", "MAKROTABLET_STACK", QStr, "", -1)
		if MST.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			GetBuEvrakSecilenMik = GetBuEvrakSecilenMik + STOK83T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		End if
	Next
End Function
Sub Nerede()
	Set MSTCK=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("STOK01_PDA_LIST=STOK01(NOFILE)","IO")

	Call StokGoster()

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadMainForm_Dyn "NEREDE",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par	
End Sub
Sub StokGoster()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set STOK01_PDA_LIST = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA_LIST")
	Set STOK83E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83E")

	STOK01_PDA_LIST.Empty

	if trim(STOK01_PDA.KOD) = "" Then Exit Sub

	QStr = "KOD='" & STOK01_PDA.KOD & "'"
	if trim(STOK83E.AMBCODE) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & "AMBCODE='" & STOK83E.AMBCODE & "'"
	End if
	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("sdfw3ff4f4", "STOK01", QStr, "", -1)

	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	
	For i = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = STOK01_PDA_LIST.AddRow()
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

		STOK01_PDA_LIST.AMBCODE = S01.AMBCODE(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.LOCATION1 = S01.LOCATION1(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.LOCATION2 = S01.LOCATION2(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.LOCATION3 = S01.LOCATION3(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.LOCATION4 = S01.LOCATION4(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.LOTNUMBER = S01.LOTNUMBER(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.SERINO = S01.SERINO(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.VRI_VR_R1 = S01.VRI_VR_R1(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.VRI_VR_R2 = S01.VRI_VR_R2(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.VRI_VR_R3 = S01.VRI_VR_R3(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.VRI_NUM1 = S01.VRI_NUM1(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.VRI_NUM2 = S01.VRI_NUM2(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.VRI_VR_C = S01.VRI_VR_C(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.SF_MIKTAR = S01.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.SF_UNIT = S01.SF_UNIT(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.FOKOD = S01.FOKOD(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS = S01.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_EVRAKNO = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_SIPTYPE = S01.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_SIPNO = S01.ALLOC_SIPNO(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_SIPARTNO = S01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_MPSEVNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_MPSARTNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(i)
	Next
	STOK01_PDA_LIST.SetCurrentRow(1)
End Sub
Sub KodLotSeriTransferFisiYarat()
	Set S83T = Doc.Select2("afd23fsdfgsweg", "STOK83T", "YENI_LOTNUMBER<>'" & Space(12) & "'", "", -1)
	if S83T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK25", "")
	Set S25E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25E")
	Set S25T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25T")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set STOK83E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83E")

	MTable.STOK25_EVRAKNO = ""

	Doc1.New_Voucher()
	S25E.AMBCODE = STOK83E.TARGETAMBCODE
	S25E.TARGETAMBCODE = STOK83E.TARGETAMBCODE

	For i = 1 to S83T.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = S25T.AddRow()
		S25T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

		S25T.KOD = S83T.KOD(i)
		S25T.LOTNUMBER = S83T.LOTNUMBER(i)
		S25T.SERINO = S83T.SERINO(i)
		S25T.SF_MIKTAR = S83T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		S25T.OSF_SF_UNIT = S83T.SF_SF_UNIT(i)
		S25T.FOKOD = S83T.FOKOD(i)
		S25T.OSF_VRI_VR_C = S83T.SF_VRI_VR_C(i)
		S25T.OSF_VRI_VR_R1 = S83T.SF_VRI_VR_R1(i)
		S25T.OSF_VRI_VR_R2 = S83T.SF_VRI_VR_R2(i)
		S25T.OSF_VRI_VR_R3 = S83T.SF_VRI_VR_R3(i)
		S25T.OSF_VRI_NUM1 = S83T.SF_VRI_NUM1(i)
		S25T.OSF_VRI_NUM2 = S83T.SF_VRI_NUM2(i)
		S25T.LOCATION1 = S83T.LOCATION_NEW1(i)
		S25T.LOCATION2 = S83T.LOCATION_NEW2(i)
		S25T.LOCATION3 = S83T.LOCATION_NEW3(i)
		S25T.LOCATION4 = S83T.LOCATION_NEW4(i)
		S25T.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS = S83T.NALLOC_SYSSTATUS(i)
		S25T.ALLOC_NONMOVABL = S83T.NALLOC_NONMOVABL(i)
		S25T.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = S83T.NALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(i)
		S25T.ALLOC_EVRAKNO = S83T.NALLOC_EVRAKNO(i)
		S25T.ALLOC_SIPTYPE = S83T.NALLOC_SIPTYPE(i)
		S25T.ALLOC_SIPNO = S83T.NALLOC_SIPNO(i)
		S25T.ALLOC_SIPARTNO = S83T.NALLOC_SIPARTNO(i)
		S25T.ALLOC_MPSEVNO = S83T.NALLOC_MPSEVNO(i)
		S25T.ALLOC_MPSARTNO = S83T.NALLOC_MPSARTNO(i)

		Doc1.RunCommand "SATIR_ESKIBILGILERI_YENIYEKOPYALA"
		S25T.NEWLOTNUMBER = S83T.YENI_LOTNUMBER(i)
	Next
	ret = Doc1.Save_Voucher
	if ret = 0 Then
		MTable.STOK25_EVRAKNO = S25E.EVRAKNO
	End if
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : SNK10 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : BATCH
Hangi Tablo için : STOK00
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Batch
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Dim MTable
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "KOD","CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "URETIM_TARIHI","DATE",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "LOTNO","CHAR",16,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "B_BATCH_D","DATE",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "E_BATCH_D","DATE",10,0,"L","",""

	Dim TEKL20E
	Set TEKL20E=Doc.CreateTableObject("TEKL20E=STACK","IO")
	TEKL20E.Addfield2 "MAL_HESAPTARIH","DATE",10,0,"L","",""
	TEKL20E.Addfield2 "MAL_STDM_STH","CHAR",2,0,"L","","STMTYP"
	TEKL20E.Addfield2 "MAL_STDM_STI","CHAR",2,0,"L","","STMTYP"
	TEKL20E.Addfield2 "BATCH_COST","DOUBLE",20,2,"R","",""
	TEKL20E.Addfield2 "LOSS","DOUBLE",20,2,"R","",""
	TEKL20E.Addfield2 "PRODUCTION_COST","DOUBLE",20,2,"R","",""
	TEKL20E.Addfield2 "SALES_COST","DOUBLE",20,2,"R","",""
	TEKL20E.Addfield2 "ADMIN_COST","DOUBLE",20,2,"R","",""
	TEKL20E.Addfield2 "LAB_COST","DOUBLE",20,2,"R","",""
	TEKL20E.Addfield2 "TOTAL_COST","DOUBLE",20,2,"R","",""

	Dim MTableT
	Set MTableT=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET=BOMU01T(NOFILE)","IO")

	Dim MTableT2
	Set MTableT2=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET2=STACK","IO")
	MTableT2.Addfield2 "BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE","CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')",""
	MTableT2.Addfield2 "KAYNAK_AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTableT2.Addfield2 "LIGHT_FASTNESS","CHAR",20,0,"L","",""
	MTableT2.Addfield2 "HEAT_RESISTANCE","CHAR",20,0,"L","",""
	MTableT2.Addfield2 "FOOD_GRADE","CHAR",20,0,"L","",""
	MTableT2.Addfield2 "COLOR_INDEX","CHAR",20,0,"L","",""

	Dim MTableT3
	Set MTableT3=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET3=TEKL20TI(NOFILE)","IO")


	Set GRP=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("GUNLUKRAPOR=STACK","IO")
	GRP.Addfield2 "BATCH_D", "DATE",10,0,"L","",""
	GRP.Addfield2 "KOD", "CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	GRP.Addfield2 "CUST_NAM", "CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	GRP.Addfield2 "CUST_CO", "CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	GRP.Addfield2 "NOTE", "CHAR",70,0,"L","",""


	Call AddUDEFFields(MTable)

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Set S00 = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK00")
	EkranFormu = GetEkranFormu("STOK00", S00)
'	Call MsgBoxToTxt("", EkranFormu)
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU1",EkranFormu, Par

	Set S00 = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK00")
	MTable.KOD = S00.KOD

	Call GetKartInfo()
End Sub
Function GetEkranFormu(AnaTabloStr, AnaTablo)
'	EnBoy = GetScreenWidthAndHeight()
'	En = EnBoy(0)
'	Boy = EnBoy(1)
	En = 0
	if En = 0 or Boy = 0 Then
		En = 1024
		Boy = 690
	Else
		En = En - 25
		Boy = Boy - 60
	End if 
	TabEn = En - 5
	TabBoy = Boy - 80

	GridEn = En - 5
	GridBoy = TabBoy - 65

	FormStr = "FORM MAKROFORMU1 HEIGHT=" & Boy & "P WIDTH=" & En & "P SCROLLBARS=NO CAPTIONTYPE=SYS" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "SET CAPTION= " & Chr(34) & "Edit Batch" & Chr(34) & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "MENUDEF" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "   POPUP TEXT=" & Chr(34) & "Dosya" & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "   MENUITEM TEXT=" & Chr(34) & "Ýþlem Yapmadan Çý&kýþ" & Chr(34) & " ID=ID_APP_CANCEL KEY=ESCAPE" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "   ENDPOPUP" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDMENUDEF" & vbCrLf

	UDFStr2 = GetUDFStr2()
	FormStr = FormStr & UDFStr2 & vbCrLf


	FormStr = FormStr & "TABDEF X=0p Y=120p WIDTH=" & TabEn & "p H=" & TabBoy & "p AUTOSIZE=N" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & 	"TAB Name=""Batch Information"" CAPTION="" Batch Information """ & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & 	"TAB Name=""Ürün Aðacý"" CAPTION="" Ürün Aðacý """ & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & 	"TAB Name=""Component Properties"" CAPTION="" Component Properties """ & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & 	"TAB Name=""Cost"" CAPTION="" Cost """ & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & 	"TAB Name=""Batch Test"" CAPTION="" Batch Test """ & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & 	"TAB Name=""Günlük Rapor"" CAPTION="" Günlük Rapor """ & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDTABDEF" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "LOCATE TOP=10p" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "LTEXT LEFT=1 TOP=+0 T='Stok Kodu : '" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "LOCATE LEFT=E+2" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "EDITTEXT LEFT=+0 TOP=+0 D=MAKROTABLE.KOD BROWSEBUTTON=1" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "BUTTON LEFT=+0 TOP=E+8p WIDTH=20 HEIGHT=30p CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & "Bilgileri Getir" & Chr(34) & " ID=" & Chr(34) & "MT_RUNMACRO_AFTERCALC('MAKR7S.@@@BUMAKRO@@@','GetKartInfo')" & chr(34) & vbcrlf
	FormStr = FormStr & "BUTTON LEFT=+0 TOP=E+8p WIDTH=20 HEIGHT=30p CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & "KTA 'larý Kaydet" & Chr(34) & " ID=" & Chr(34) & "MT_RUNMACRO_AFTERCALC('MAKR7S.@@@BUMAKRO@@@','KTA_Yaz')" & chr(34) & vbcrlf
	FormStr = FormStr & "BUTTON LEFT=E+10P TOP=+0P WIDTH=20 HEIGHT=30p CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & "Listele" & Chr(34) & " ID=" & Chr(34) & "MT_RUNMACRO_AFTERCALC('MAKR7S.@@@BUMAKRO@@@','Listele')" & chr(34) & vbcrlf
	

'**
	FormStr = FormStr & "TAB ""Batch Information""" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "LOCATE TOP=50p" & vbCrLf

	UDFStr = GetUDFStr()
	FormStr = FormStr & UDFStr & vbCrLf
	'FormStr = FormStr & "BUTTON LEFT=E+2 TOP=+0 WIDTH=20 HEIGHT=2 CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & "Çýkýþ" & Chr(34) & " ID=ID_APP_CANCEL" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDTAB" & vbCrLf
'**

'**
	FormStr = FormStr & "TAB ""Ürün Aðacý""" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "LOCATE TOP=50p" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & GetBomTableStr & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDTAB" & vbCrLf
'**

'**
	FormStr = FormStr & "TAB ""Component Properties""" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "LOCATE TOP=50p" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & GetComPropTableStr() & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDTAB" & vbCrLf
'**

'**
	FormStr = FormStr & "TAB ""Cost""" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "LOCATE TOP=20p" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & GetKriterStr()
	FormStr = FormStr & GetCostTableStr() & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDTAB" & vbCrLf
'**

'**
	FormStr = FormStr & "TAB ""Batch Test""" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "LOCATE TOP=30p" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & GetBatchTestStr() & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDTAB" & vbCrLf
'**

'**
	FormStr = FormStr & "TAB ""Günlük Rapor""" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "LOCATE TOP=30p" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & GetGunlukRaporStr() & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDTAB" & vbCrLf
'**

	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDFORM" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = Replace(FormStr, "@@@BUMAKRO@@@", SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME)

	GetEkranFormu = FormStr
End Function
Function GetUDFStr()
	Ust = 2
	IkinciBlok = 11
	UDFStr = "LOCATE TOP=" & Ust & vbCrLf
'	FormStr = FormStr & "LTEXT LEFT=E+10 TOP=+0 D=MAKROTABLE.KOD" & vbCrLf

	Set U10T = Doc.Select1("dd333", "UFLD10T", "EVRAKNO='STOK00'&VARCODE>='BI_'&VARCODE<='BI_zzz'", "", -1)
	For i = 1 to U10T.GetRowCount()
		AlanAdi = "UDEFF__" & trim(U10T.VARCODE(i))
		AlanIndex = U10T.VARINDEX(i)
		if trim(AlanIndex) <> "" Then
			AlanAdi = AlanAdi & "_" & trim(AlanIndex)
		End if
		Aciklama = trim(U10T.VARASPNAME(i))
		AlanTuru = trim(U10T.VARTYPE(i))
		AlanGenisligi = U10T.VARLEN(i)
		Grupkodu = trim(U10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT3(i))

		if i >= IkinciBlok Then Left1 = 68 Else Left1 = 1
		if i = IkinciBlok Then UDFStr = UDFStr & "LOCATE TOP=" & Ust & vbCrLf
		UDFStr = UDFStr & "LTEXT LEFT=" & Left1 & " TOP=E+0 T='" & Aciklama & "'" & vbCrLf
		if AlanTuru = "D" Then
			UDFStr = UDFStr & "COMBOBOX "
		Elseif AlanTuru = "T" and trim(Grupkodu) <> "" Then
			UDFStr = UDFStr & "COMBOBOX "
		Else
			UDFStr = UDFStr & "EDITTEXT "
		End if
		UDFStr = UDFStr & "LEFT=+16 TOP=+0 "
		if AlanGenisligi > 50 Then UDFStr = UDFStr & "WIDTH=50 "
		UDFStr = UDFStr & "D=MAKROTABLE." & AlanAdi & vbCrLf
	Next
	GetUDFStr = UDFStr
End Function
Function GetUDFStr2()
	UDFStr = "LOCATE TOP=10p" & vbCrLf

	QStr = "EVRAKNO='STOK00'&VARCODE>='B_'&VARCODE<='B_zzz'"
	QStr = QStr & "&VARCODE<>'B_BOMEVRAK'&VARCODE<>'B_TKEVRAK'&VARCODE<>'B_TKPUNIT'&VARCODE<>'B_ONAY'"
	Set U10T = Doc.Select1("dd333", "UFLD10T", QStr, "", -1)
	For i = 1 to U10T.GetRowCount()
		AlanAdi = "UDEFF__" & trim(U10T.VARCODE(i))
		AlanIndex = U10T.VARINDEX(i)
		if trim(AlanIndex) <> "" Then
			AlanAdi = AlanAdi & "_" & trim(AlanIndex)
		End if
		Aciklama = trim(U10T.VARASPNAME(i))
		AlanTuru = trim(U10T.VARTYPE(i))
		AlanGenisligi = U10T.VARLEN(i)
		Grupkodu = trim(U10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT3(i))

		Left1 = 50
		UDFStr = UDFStr & "LTEXT LEFT=" & Left1 & " TOP=E+0 T='" & Aciklama & "'" & vbCrLf
		if AlanTuru = "D" Then
			UDFStr = UDFStr & "COMBOBOX "
		Elseif AlanTuru = "T" and trim(Grupkodu) <> "" Then
			UDFStr = UDFStr & "COMBOBOX "
		Else
			UDFStr = UDFStr & "EDITTEXT "
		End if
		UDFStr = UDFStr & "LEFT=+16 TOP=+0 "
		if AlanGenisligi > 50 Then UDFStr = UDFStr & "WIDTH=50 "
		UDFStr = UDFStr & "D=MAKROTABLE." & AlanAdi & vbCrLf
	Next
	UDFStr = UDFStr & "BUTTON LEFT=+0 TOP=E+8p WIDTH=20 HEIGHT=30p CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & "Statü Deðistir" & Chr(34) & " ID=" & Chr(34) & "MT_RUNMACRO_AFTERCALC('MAKR7S.@@@BUMAKRO@@@','StatuDegistir')" & chr(34) & vbcrlf
	UDFStr = UDFStr & "LTEXT LEFT=E+3P TOP=+5p W=160P D=""MAKROTABLE.UDEFF__B_ONAY"" BORDER=ETCHED  BCKCOL=RGB_232_232_232" & vbcrlf

	GetUDFStr2 = UDFStr
End Function
Sub AddUDEFFields(MTable)
	Set U10T = Doc.Select1("dd333", "UFLD10T", "EVRAKNO='STOK00'&VARCODE>='BI_'&VARCODE<='BI_zzz'", "", -1)
	For i = 1 to U10T.GetRowCount()
		AlanAdi = "UDEFF__" & trim(U10T.VARCODE(i))
		AlanIndex = U10T.VARINDEX(i)
		if trim(AlanIndex) <> "" Then
			AlanAdi = AlanAdi & "_" & trim(AlanIndex)
		End if
		Aciklama = trim(U10T.VARASPNAME(i))
		AlanTuru = trim(U10T.VARTYPE(i))
		Grupkodu = trim(U10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT3(i))
		Tablo = trim(U10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT4(i))
		Uzunluk = U10T.VARLEN(i)
		Ondalik = U10T.VARSIG(i)
		if AlanTuru = "D" Then
			MTable.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "DATE",10,0,"L","",""
		Elseif AlanTuru = "T" and trim(Grupkodu) <> "" Then
			MTable.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "CHAR",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"L","",Grupkodu
		Elseif AlanTuru = "T" and trim(Tablo) <> "" Then
			MTable.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "CHAR",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"L","F2=DO_BRW('" & Tablo & "')",""
		Elseif AlanTuru = "T" Then
			MTable.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "CHAR",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"L","",""
		Elseif AlanTuru = "N" Then
			MTable.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "DOUBLE",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"R","",""
		End if
	Next
	Call AddUDEFFields2(MTable)
End Sub

Sub AddBOMFields(MTableT)
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("BOMU01", "")
	Grid = Doc1.GetGridColInfo("", "BOMU01T")
	For i = 0 to uBound(Grid, 1)
		AlanAdi = Grid(i,0)
		Aciklama = Grid(i,2)
		AlanTuru = Grid(i,4)
		Grupkodu = ""
		Tablo = ""
		Uzunluk = Grid(i,5)
		Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
		if AlanTuru = "D" Then
			MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "DATE",10,0,"L","",""
		Elseif AlanTuru = "C" and trim(Grupkodu) <> "" Then
			MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "CHAR",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"L","",Grupkodu
		Elseif AlanTuru = "C" and trim(Tablo) <> "" Then
			MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "CHAR",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"L","F2=DO_BRW('" & Tablo & "')",""
		Elseif AlanTuru = "C" Then
			MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "CHAR",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"L","",""
		Elseif AlanTuru = "N" Then
			MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "DOUBLE",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"R","",""
		End if
	Next
End Sub

Function GetMainTableStr()
	MainTableStr = ""
	Grid = Doc.GetGridColInfo("", "MAKROTABLET")
	For i = 0 to uBound(Grid, 1)
		AlanAdi = Grid(i,0)
		Aciklama = Replace(Grid(i,2), "\n", " ")
		Aciklama = Replace(Aciklama, chr(13), " ")
		Aciklama = Replace(Aciklama, chr(10), " ")
		AlanTuru = Grid(i,4)
		Grupkodu = ""
		Tablo = ""
		if AlanTuru = "D" Then
			MainTableStr = MainTableStr & "COMBOBOX D=" & AlanAdi & " CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & Aciklama & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
		Elseif AlanTuru = "C" and trim(Grupkodu) <> "" Then
			MainTableStr = MainTableStr & "COMBOBOX D=" & AlanAdi & " CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & Aciklama & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
		Else
			MainTableStr = MainTableStr & "EDITTEXT D=" & AlanAdi & " CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & Aciklama & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
		End if
	Next
	GetMainTableStr = MainTableStr
End Function

Sub MsgBoxToTxt(MesajBaslik, Mesaj)
	Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
	tmpFolder = fso.GetSpecialFolder(2)
	dosyaAdi = tmpFolder & "\Hata.txt"
	Set HataFile = Fso.OpenTextFile(dosyaAdi, 2, True)
	HataFile.WriteLine(MesajBaslik & " : " & vbcrlf & Mesaj)
	HataFile.Close
	Set wshShell = CreateObject("WSCript.shell")
	wshshell.run "notepad " & dosyaAdi, 1, True
	set wshshell = nothing
End Sub
Sub AlanlariDoldur()

	Set AnaTablo = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK00")
	Set MainTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	StokKodu = MainTable.KOD
	MainTable.Empty

	Alanlar = MainTable.GetFieldNames("")
	For each Alan in Alanlar
		if InStr(1,Alan, "UDEFF__") > 0 Then
			AlanAdiIndex = GetAlanAdiIndex(Mid(Alan, 8), "STOK00")
			KTA_Alan = AlanAdiIndex(0)
			KTA_Index = AlanAdiIndex(1)
			AlanDegeri = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK00", StokKodu, KTA_Alan, KTA_Index)
			MainTable.SetFieldValue Alan, -1, AlanDegeri
		End if
	Next
	MainTable.KOD = StokKodu
	if trim(StokKodu) = "" Then
		StokKodu = AnaTablo.KOD
		BatchDate = AnaTablo.CREA_TARIH
	Else
		BatchDate = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "CREA_TARIH", StokKodu, "")
	End if
	if trim(MainTable.UDEFF__B_BATCH_D) = "" Then MainTable.UDEFF__B_BATCH_D = BatchDate
End Sub
Sub KTA_Yaz()
	Set S00 = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK00")
	if Onayli() Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "ONAYLI statüsündeki kartlarýn KTA 'larýný kaydedemezsiniz", "Hata", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set AnaTablo = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK00")
	Alanlar = MTable.GetFieldNames("")
	nret = Doc.UFLD00_CREATEEDITTABLE("T_U0001", "STOK00", MTable.KOD)
	Set T_U1 = Doc.GetTableObject("T_U0001")   
	For each Alan in Alanlar
		if InStr(1,Alan, "UDEFF__") > 0 Then
			KTA_Alan = Mid(Alan, 8)
			AlanDegeri = MTable.GetFieldValue(Alan, -1)
			K = T_U1.SetFieldValue(KTA_Alan,-1,AlanDegeri)
		End if
	Next 
	T_U1.Exec "SAVE" 

	Doc.Load_Voucher MTable.KOD
	S00.TEXT_TECH_4 = MTable.UDEFF__B_BATCH_D
	Doc.Save_Voucher
End Sub
Function GetAlanAdiIndex(AlanAdi, KTA_Tablo)
	AlanAdi = trim(AlanAdi)
	Dim AlanArr(1)
	AlanArr(0) = AlanAdi
	AlanArr(1) = 0

	GetAlanAdiIndex = AlanArr
	tire = Instr(1,AlanAdi, "_")
	If tire = 0 Then Exit Function

	If Right(AlanAdi,1) =  "_" Then Exit Function
	If Right(AlanAdi,1) < "0" or Right(AlanAdi,1) > "9" Then Exit Function

	Set U10T = Doc.Select1("dd3333", "UFLD10T", "EVRAKNO='STOK00'&VARCODE='" & AlanAdi & "'", "", -1)
	if U10T.GetRowCount() > 0 and Len(AlanAdi) <= Len(U10T.VARCODE) Then Exit Function

	Uzunluk = Len(AlanAdi)
	if Uzunluk < 3 Then Exit Function
	For i = Uzunluk-1 to Uzunluk-3 step -1
		chr1 = Mid(AlanAdi, i, 1)
		if chr1 = "_" Then
			AlanArr(0) = Left(AlanAdi, i-1)
			AlanArr(1) = Doc.CDbl(Mid(AlanAdi, i+1))
		End if
	Next
	GetAlanAdiIndex = AlanArr
End Function
Function GetScreenWidthAndHeight()
	Dim ScrArr(1)
	ScrArr(0) = 0
	ScrArr(1) = 0

	Set IE = CreateObject("InternetExplorer.Application")
	IE.Visible = False
	IE.Navigate ("about:blank")
	Set Scr = IE.Document.parentWindow.screen
	Width = Scr.Width
	Height = Scr.Height
	AvailableWidth = Scr.availwidth
	AvailableHeight = Scr.availheight
	BitsPerPixel = Scr.colordepth

	ScrArr(0) = AvailableWidth
	ScrArr(1) = AvailableHeight

	Set Scr = Nothing
	IE.Quit
	Set IE = Nothing
	GetScreenWidthAndHeight = ScrArr
End Function

	BomStr = BomStr & "LTEXT LEFT=1 T

Dim S32E_REP, d(), SH2, WB, EXCEL, yenitarih, eskitarih, MaliYilbasi
Dim cc, excelkolon

Sub makro1()
	Set s = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	excelkolon = 1
	cc = 0
	c2 = 0
	redim c(35)
	redim d(35)
	redim htotal(100)
	redim gtotal(100)
	Set Excel = CreateObject("excel.Application")
	Set Wb = Excel.Workbooks.Add
	Set Sh2 = Wb.Sheets.Add
	Sh2.Name = Replace(LISTEE.RECTARIH11, "/", "")
	for x = 1 to 35
		c(x) = 0
		d(x) = 0
	Next
	MaliYilbasi = GetMaliYilbasi()
	
	Call data_yarat()
	
	S32E_REP.sort("RECTARIH1 + MPSNO")
'	Call SendTableToExcel(S32E_REP)
'	exit sub
	yenitarih = LISTEE.RECTARIH11
	
	call gecmis_hesapla()
	
	call hafta_hesapla()
	
	For g = 1 to 33
		Sh2.Cells(g,1).Select
		excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	Next
	For g1 = 1 to 13
		Sh2.Cells(1,g1).Select
		excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	Next
	Call CizgiCiz("A1:L84")
	Sh2.Range("A1:L75").Select
	Sh2.Range("A1:L75").columnwidth = 12.45
	Sh2.Range("A:A").Select
	Sh2.Range("A:A").columnwidth = 28.43
	
	Call belirle("A1:L84")
	call Font_belirle()
	Call exceleyazdir_toplam(excelkolon)
	Excel.Visible = True
	
End Sub
Sub exceleyazdir(eskitarih, excelkolon)
	ff = excelkolon
	Sh2.Cells(1,ff) = eskitarih
	
	Sh2.Cells(2,1) = "TOTAL FINISHED PRODUCTIONS (KGS)"
	Sh2.Cells(2,ff) = Doc.NumFormat(c(2),12,2)
	
	Sh2.Cells(3,1) = "TOTAL FINISHED PRODUCTIONS AND CLEANING (KGS)"
	ToplamBitmisUrunveTemizlik = C(2) + c(18) + c(16) + c(17)
	Sh2.Cells(3,ff) = Doc.NumFormat(ToplamBitmisUrunveTemizlik,12,2)
	
	Sh2.Cells(4,1) = "REWORK (KGS)"
	Sh2.Cells(4,ff) = Doc.NumFormat(c(4),12,2)
	
	Sh2.Cells(5,1) = "REWORK (%)"
	Sh2.Cells(5,ff) = Doc.NumFormat(Doc.Div(Sh2.Cells(4,ff), Sh2.Cells(2,ff))*100,12,2)
	
	Sh2.Cells(6,1) = "MONO MATERIALS USED IN PRODUCTIONS (KGS)"
	Sh2.Cells(6,ff) = Doc.NumFormat(Sh2.Cells(2,ff) - Sh2.Cells(4,ff),12,2)
	
	Sh2.Cells(7,1) = "OPERATING HOURS (HRS) - LEISTRITZ"
	Sh2.Cells(7,ff) = Doc.NumFormat(c(7),12,2)
	
	Sh2.Cells(8,1) = "OPERATING HOURS (HRS) - ZSK32 - K"
	Sh2.Cells(8,ff) = Doc.NumFormat(c(8),12,2)
	
	Sh2.Cells(9,1) = "OPERATING HOURS (HRS) - ZSK32 - S"
	Sh2.Cells(9,ff) = Doc.NumFormat(c(9),12,2)
	
	Sh2.Cells(10,1) = "OPERATING HOURS (HRS) - MARIS"
	Sh2.Cells(10,ff) = Doc.NumFormat(c(10),12,2)
	
	Sh2.Cells(11,1) = "OPERATING HOURS (HRS) - LAB-EX"
	Sh2.Cells(11,ff) = Doc.NumFormat(c(11),12,2)
	ToplamCalismaSuresi = C(7) + c(8) + c(9) + c(10) + c(11)

	ExcelsatirNo = 12
	Sh2.Cells(ExcelsatirNo,1) = "OPERATING HOURS (HRS) - TOTAL"
	Sh2.Cells(ExcelsatirNo,ff) = Doc.NumFormat(ToplamCalismaSuresi,12,2)
	
	ExcelsatirNo = ExcelsatirNo + 1
	Sh2.Cells(ExcelsatirNo,1) = "WORKING HOURS (HRS) - TOTAL"
	Sh2.Cells(ExcelsatirNo,ff) = Doc.NumFormat(c(12),12,2)
	
	ExcelsatirNo = ExcelsatirNo + 1
	Sh2.Cells(ExcelsatirNo,1) = "USING CAPACITY OF LINES (%)"
	Sh2.Cells(ExcelsatirNo,ff) = Doc.NumFormat(Doc.Div(ToplamCalismaSuresi, c(12))*100,12,2)
	
	ExcelsatirNo = ExcelsatirNo + 1
	Sh2.Cells(ExcelsatirNo,1) = "TOTAL NUMBER OF LAB BATCHES"
	ExcelsatirNo = ExcelsatirNo + 1
	Sh2.Cells(ExcelsatirNo,1) = "TOTAL QUANTITY OF LAB BATCHES (kg)"
	ExcelsatirNo = ExcelsatirNo + 1
	Sh2.Cells(ExcelsatirNo,1) = "TOTAL EXTRUSION QUANTITY (2,5 TIME PASSING) - (kg)"
	ExcelsatirNo = ExcelsatirNo + 1
	Sh2.Cells(ExcelsatirNo,1) = "TOTAL CLEANING MATERIAL QUANTITY FOR LAB BATCHES (kg)"
	
	ExcelsatirNo = ExcelsatirNo + 1
	Sh2.Cells(ExcelsatirNo,1) = "CLEANING MATERIALS (KGS)"
	Sh2.Cells(ExcelsatirNo,ff) = Doc.NumFormat(c(18),12,2)
	
	ExcelsatirNo = ExcelsatirNo + 1
	Sh2.Cells(ExcelsatirNo,1) = "NO OF LOTS (FINAL PRODUCTIONS ONLY) "
	Sh2.Cells(ExcelsatirNo,ff) = Doc.NumFormat(c(19),12,2)
	
	ExcelsatirNo = ExcelsatirNo + 1
	Sh2.Cells(ExcelsatirNo,1) = "CLEANING MATERIALS (FOR FINAL PRODUCTS) (KGS/LOT)"
'	Sh2.Cells(ExcelsatirNo,ff) = Doc.NumFormat(Doc.Div(Sh2.Cells(18,ff), Sh2.Cells(19,ff)),12,2)
	Sh2.Cells(ExcelsatirNo,ff) = Doc.NumFormat(Doc.Div(c(18), c(19)),12,2)

	ExcelsatirNo = ExcelsatirNo + 1
	Sh2.Cells(ExcelsatirNo,1) = "NO OF LOTS (INCLUDING PRE - MIX)"
	Sh2.Cells(ExcelsatirNo,ff) = Doc.NumFormat(c(21),12,2)
	
	ExcelsatirNo = ExcelsatirNo + 1
	Sh2.Cells(ExcelsatirNo,1) = "CLEANING MATERIALS (INCLUDING PRE - MIX) (KGS/LOT)"
	Sh2.Cells(ExcelsatirNo,ff) = Doc.NumFormat(Doc.Div(c(18), c(21)),12,2)
	
	ExcelsatirNo = ExcelsatirNo + 1
	Sh2.Cells(ExcelsatirNo,1) = "POWER COMPANY ENERGY COUNTER IND.(KWH)(MAIN PANEL)"
	Sh2.Cells(ExcelsatirNo,ff) = Doc.NumFormat(c(23),12,2)
	
	ExcelsatirNo = ExcelsatirNo + 1
	Sh2.Cells(ExcelsatirNo,1) = "TOTAL ENERGY CONSUMPTION FROM POWER COMPANY(KWh)"
	Sh2.Cells(ExcelsatirNo,ff) = Doc.NumFormat(c(24),12,2)
	
	ExcelsatirNo = ExcelsatirNo + 1
	Sh2.Cells(ExcelsatirNo,1) = "ENERGY CONSUMPTION (KWh/kg)"
	Sh2.Cells(ExcelsatirNo,ff) = Doc.NumFormat(Doc.Div(c(24), ToplamBitmisUrunveTemizlik),12,2)
	
	if ff = 2 Then
		Call kur_yazdir1(ff, ExcelsatirNo + 2)
		cc = 0
	else
		Call kur_yazdir(ff, eskitarih, ExcelsatirNo + 1)
		ss = ss + 1
	End If
End Sub
Sub Exceleyazdir_toplam(excelkolon)
	excelkolon = excelkolon + 1
	Sh2.Cells(1, excelkolon) = "CUMMULATIVE OF THIS WEEK"
	Sh2.Cells(1, excelkolon + 1) = "GENERAL CUMMULATIVE"
	
	for c1 = 2 to 90
		for c2 = 2 to excelkolon
			if trim(Sh2.cells(c1, c2)) <> "" Then
				GTotal(c1) = GTotal(c1) + Sh2.cells(c1, c2)
			End If
		Next
		for c2 = 3 to excelkolon
			if trim(Sh2.cells(c1, c2)) <> "" Then
				hTotal(c1) = hTotal(c1) + Sh2.cells(c1, c2)
			End If
		Next
		
		Sh2.Cells(c1,excelkolon) = Doc.NumFormat(hTotal(c1),12,2)
		Sh2.Cells(c1,excelkolon + 1) = Doc.NumFormat(gTotal(c1),12,2)
	Next
	
	Sh2.Cells(5,excelkolon)= "%"&Doc.NumFormat(doc.div(Sh2.Cells(4,excelkolon),Sh2.Cells(2,excelkolon) )*100,12,2)
	Sh2.Cells(5,excelkolon+1)= "%"&Doc.NumFormat(doc.div(Sh2.Cells(4,excelkolon+1),Sh2.Cells(2,excelkolon+1) )*100,12,2)
	Sh2.Cells(14,excelkolon) = "%" & Doc.NumFormat(Doc.Div(Sh2.Cells(12,excelkolon),Sh2.Cells(13,excelkolon) )*100,12,2)
	Sh2.Cells(14,excelkolon + 1) = "%" & Doc.NumFormat(Doc.Div(Sh2.Cells(12,excelkolon + 1),Sh2.Cells(13,excelkolon + 1))*100,12,2)
	
	Sh2.Cells(21,excelkolon) = Doc.NumFormat(Doc.Div(Sh2.Cells(19,excelkolon),Sh2.Cells(20,excelkolon) ),12,2)
	Sh2.Cells(21,excelkolon + 1) = Doc.NumFormat(Doc.Div(Sh2.Cells(19,excelkolon + 1),Sh2.Cells(20,excelkolon + 1) ),12,2)
	
	Sh2.Cells(23,excelkolon) = Doc.NumFormat(Doc.Div(Sh2.Cells(19,excelkolon),Sh2.Cells(22,excelkolon) ),12,2)
	Sh2.Cells(23,excelkolon + 1) = Doc.NumFormat(Doc.Div(Sh2.Cells(19,excelkolon + 1),Sh2.Cells(22,excelkolon + 1) ),12,2)
	
	Sh2.Cells(26,excelkolon) = Doc.NumFormat(Doc.Div(Sh2.Cells(25,excelkolon),Sh2.Cells(3,excelkolon) ),12,2)
	Sh2.Cells(26,excelkolon + 1) = Doc.NumFormat(Doc.Div(Sh2.Cells(25,excelkolon + 1),Sh2.Cells(3,excelkolon + 1) ),12,2)
End Sub
Sub CizgiCiz(aralik)
	Sh2.Range(aralik).Select
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlDiagonalDown).LineStyle = xlNone
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlDiagonalUp).LineStyle = xlNone
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeLeft).LineStyle = xlContinuous
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeLeft).Weight = xlThin
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeLeft).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
	
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeTop).LineStyle = xlContinuous
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeTop).Weight = xlThin
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeTop).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
	
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeBottom).LineStyle = xlContinuous
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeBottom).Weight = xlThin
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeBottom).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
	
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeRight).LineStyle = xlContinuous
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeRight).Weight = xlThin
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeRight).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
	
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlInsideVertical).LineStyle = xlContinuous
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlInsideVertical).Weight = xlThin
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlInsideVertical).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
	
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlInsideHorizontal).LineStyle = xlContinuous
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlInsideHorizontal).Weight = xlThin
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlInsideHorizontal).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
End Sub
Sub Font_belirle()
	excel.Selection.Font.Name = "Arial"
	excel.Selection.Font.Size = 9
End Sub
Sub Belirle(aralik)
	Sh2.Range(aralik).Select
	
	Excel.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = xlGeneral
	Excel.Selection.VerticalAlignment = xlBottom
	Excel.Selection.WrapText = True
	Excel.Selection.Orientation = 0
	Excel.Selection.AddIndent = False
	Excel.Selection.IndentLevel = 0
	Excel.Selection.ShrinkToFit = False
	Excel.Selection.ReadingOrder = xlContext
	Excel.Selection.MergeCells = False
End Sub
Sub Kur_yazdir(xCol,Eskitarih, BaslangicSatir)
	Set grup1 = Doc.Select1("_gecust","GECOUST","EVRAKNO = '*PUNIT'&AD = 'X'","SRNUM", -1)
	For i = 1 to grup1.GetRowCount()
		kur = grup1.KOD(i)
		
		sh2.cells(BaslangicSatir + (i - 1)*2, 1) = kur &" - COUNTER INDICATION "
		Kriter = "CODEFROM = '"& kur & "'&EVRAKNOTARIH = '" & eskitarih & "'"
		Set EXC1 = Doc.Select1("___EXCRATTccc","EXCRATT",Kriter,"", -1)
		For h2 = 1 to exc1.GetRecCount()
			Sh2.Cells(BaslangicSatir + (i - 1)*2, xCol) = exc1.kurs_1(h2)
			tt = Doc.StrDate_Increment(eskitarih, 1)
			Kriter1 = "CODEFROM = '" & kur & "'&EVRAKNOTARIH = '" & tt &"'"
			Set EXC11 = Doc.Select1("___EXCRATTnnnn1","EXCRATT",Kriter1,"", -1)
			
			For h21 = 1 to exc11.getreccount
				sh2.cells(BaslangicSatir + 1 + (i - 1)*2, xCol) = exc11.kurs_1(h21) - exc1.kurs_1(h2)
			Next
		Next
	Next
End Sub
Sub Kur_yazdir1(ff, BaslangicSatir)
	BaslangicTarihi = "2009/10/01"
	if MaliYilBasi > BaslangicTarihi Then BaslangicTarihi = MaliYilBasi
	
	BaslangicTarihi = Doc.StrDate_Increment(BaslangicTarihi, -1)
	BitisTarihi = Doc.StrDate_Increment(LISTEE.RECTARIH11, -1)
	
	Set Grup1 = Doc.Select1("_gecust","GECOUST","EVRAKNO = '*PUNIT'&AD = 'X'","SRNUM", -1)
	For h1 = 1 to Grup1.getreccount
		kur = Grup1.KOD(h1)
		cc = cc + 1
		if BitisTarihi <= BaslangicTarihi Then
			ToplamSarfiyat = 0
		Else
			BaslangicSayac = Doc.GetKur(Kur, "SAAT", BaslangicTarihi, "", -1)
			BitisSayac = Doc.GetKur(Kur, "SAAT", BitisTarihi, "", -1)
			if BaslangicSayac = 0 or BitisSayac = 0 or BitisSayac < BaslangicSayac Then
				Doc.d7MsgBox trim(Kur) & " tezgahý " & Doc.StrToNorDate(BaslangicTarihi) & " tarihli sayaç : " & _
				BaslangicSayac &  ", " & Doc.StrToNorDate(BitisTarihi) & " tarihli sayaç : " & BitisSayac, "", MB_OK
			End If
			ToplamSarfiyat = BitisSayac - BaslangicSayac
		End If
		Sh2.cells(BaslangicSatir + (cc - 1) * 2, 1) = kur & " - ENERGY (kWh) "
		Sh2.cells(BaslangicSatir + (cc - 1) * 2, ff) = ToplamSarfiyat
	Next
End Sub
Sub Data_yarat()
	Doc.CreateTableObject "ABCXYZ=SFDC32E","TMPCOPY"
	Set S32E_REP = Doc.GetTableObject("ABCXYZ")
	yenitarih = LISTEE.RECTARIH11
	
	Set S32E = Doc.Select1("___tABLE10XXX","SFDC32E","RECTARIH1>= '" & MaliYilbasi & "'","", -1)
	
	for i = 1 to S32E.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Data_yarat : " & i & " / " & S32E.GetRowCount()
		t = 0
		sorgu = 0
		
		t0 = Doc.StrDate_Increment(S32E.RECTARIH1(i), -1)
		t1 = Doc.StrDate_Increment(S32E.RECTARIH1(i), 1)
		t2 = Doc.StrDate_Increment(S32E.RECTARIH1(i), 2)
		t3 = Doc.StrDate_Increment(S32E.RECTARIH1(i),3)
		if S32E.RECTIME1(i) < "09:00" Then
			satirNo = S32E_REP.AddRow()
			S32E_REP.EVRAKNO(satirNo) = S32E.EVRAKNO(i)
			S32E_REP.MPSNO(satirNo) = S32E.MPSNO(i)
			S32E_REP.RECTYPE(satirNo) = S32E.RECTYPE(i)
			S32E_REP.X_T_ISMERKEZI(satirNo) = S32E.X_T_ISMERKEZI(i)
			S32E_REP.RECTARIH1(satirNo) = t0
			S32E_REP.RECTIME1(satirNo) = S32E.RECTIME1(i)
			if S32E.RECTIME2(i) < "09:00" and S32E.RECTARIH2(i) = S32E.RECTARIH1(i) Then
				sorgu = sorgu + 1
				S32E_REP.RECTARIH2(satirNo) = t0
				S32E_REP.RECTIME2(satirNo) = S32E.RECTIME2(i)
			Else
				S32E_REP.RECTARIH2(satirNo) = t0
				S32E_REP.RECTIME2(satirNo) = "09:00"
				S32E.RECTIME1(i) = "09:00"
			End If
			S32E_REP.SURE(satirNo) = S32E.SURE(i)
			T = t + Doc.DTDTDif1(S32E.RECTARIH1(i),S32E.RECTIME1(i),S32E.RECTARIH1(i),"09:00")
		End If
		if sorgu = 0 Then
			fark1 = Doc.DTDTDif1(S32E.RECTARIH1(i),S32E.RECTIME1(i),t1,"09:00")
			t = t + fark1
			IF t >= S32E.SURE(i) Then
				satirNo = S32E_REP.AddRow
				S32E_REP.EVRAKNO(satirNo) = S32E.EVRAKNO(i)
				S32E_REP.MPSNO(satirNo) = S32E.MPSNO(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTYPE(satirNo) = S32E.RECTYPE(i)
				S32E_REP.X_T_ISMERKEZI(satirNo) = S32E.X_T_ISMERKEZI(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTARIH1(satirNo) = S32E.RECTARIH1(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTIME1(satirNo) = S32E.RECTIME1(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTARIH2(satirNo) = S32E.RECTARIH2(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTIME2(satirNo) = S32E.RECTIME2(i)
				S32E_REP.SURE(satirNo) = S32E.SURE(i)
				sorgu = sorgu + 1
			Else
				satirNo = S32E_REP.AddRow
				S32E_REP.EVRAKNO(satirNo) = S32E.EVRAKNO(i)
				S32E_REP.MPSNO(satirNo) = S32E.MPSNO(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTYPE(satirNo) = S32E.RECTYPE(i)
				S32E_REP.X_T_ISMERKEZI(satirNo) = S32E.X_T_ISMERKEZI(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTARIH1(satirNo) = S32E.RECTARIH1(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTIME1(satirNo) = S32E.RECTIME1(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTARIH2(satirNo) = t1
				S32E_REP.RECTIME2(satirNo) = "09:00"
				S32E_REP.SURE(satirNo) = S32E.SURE(i)
				S32E.RECTARIH1(i) = t1
				S32E.RECTIME1(i) = "09:00"
			End If
		End If
		
		fark1 = Doc.DTDTDif1(S32E.RECTARIH1(i),S32E.RECTIME1(i),t2,"09:00")
		t = t + fark1
		if sorgu = 0 Then
			IF t >= S32E.SURE(i) Then
				satirNo = S32E_REP.AddRow
				S32E_REP.EVRAKNO(satirNo) = S32E.EVRAKNO(i)
				S32E_REP.MPSNO(satirNo) = S32E.MPSNO(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTYPE(satirNo) = S32E.RECTYPE(i)
				S32E_REP.X_T_ISMERKEZI(satirNo) = S32E.X_T_ISMERKEZI(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTARIH1(satirNo) = S32E.RECTARIH1(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTIME1(satirNo) = S32E.RECTIME1(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTARIH2(satirNo) = S32E.RECTARIH2(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTIME2(satirNo) = S32E.RECTIME2(i)
				S32E_REP.SURE(satirNo) = S32E.SURE(i)
				sorgu = sorgu + 1
			Else
				satirNo = S32E_REP.AddRow
				S32E_REP.EVRAKNO(satirNo) = S32E.EVRAKNO(i)
				S32E_REP.MPSNO(satirNo) = S32E.MPSNO(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTYPE(satirNo) = S32E.RECTYPE(i)
				S32E_REP.X_T_ISMERKEZI(satirNo) = S32E.X_T_ISMERKEZI(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTARIH1(satirNo) = S32E.RECTARIH1(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTIME1(satirNo) = S32E.RECTIME1(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTARIH2(satirNo) = t2
				S32E_REP.RECTIME2(satirNo) = "09:00"
				S32E_REP.SURE(satirNo) = S32E.SURE(i)
				S32E.RECTARIH1(i) = t2
				S32E.RECTIME1(i) = "09:00"
			End If
		End If
		
		fark1 = Doc.DTDTDif1(S32E.RECTARIH1(i),S32E.RECTIME1(i),t3,"09:00")
		t = t + fark1
		
		if sorgu = 0 Then
			IF t >= S32E.SURE(i) Then
				satirNo = S32E_REP.AddRow
				S32E_REP.EVRAKNO(satirNo) = S32E.EVRAKNO(i)
				S32E_REP.MPSNO(satirNo) = S32E.MPSNO(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTYPE(satirNo) = S32E.RECTYPE(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTYPE(satirNo) = S32E.RECTYPE(i)
				S32E_REP.X_T_ISMERKEZI(satirNo) = S32E.X_T_ISMERKEZI(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTARIH1(satirNo) = S32E.RECTARIH1(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTIME1(satirNo) = S32E.RECTIME1(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTARIH2(satirNo) = S32E.RECTARIH2(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTIME2(satirNo) = S32E.RECTIME2(i)
				S32E_REP.SURE(satirNo) = S32E.SURE(i)
			Else
				satirNo = S32E_REP.AddRow
				S32E_REP.EVRAKNO(satirNo) = S32E.EVRAKNO(i)
				S32E_REP.MPSNO(satirNo) = S32E.MPSNO(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTYPE(satirNo) = S32E.RECTYPE(i)
				S32E_REP.X_T_ISMERKEZI(satirNo) = S32E.X_T_ISMERKEZI(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTARIH1(satirNo) = S32E.RECTARIH1(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTIME1(satirNo) = S32E.RECTIME1(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTARIH2(satirNo) = t3
				S32E_REP.RECTIME2(satirNo) = "09:00"
				S32E_REP.SURE(satirNo) = S32E.SURE(i)
				S32E.RECTARIH1(i) = t3
				S32E.RECTIME1(i) = "09:00"
			End If
		End If
	next
End Sub
Sub gecmis_hesapla()
	mpsno1 = ""
	eskit = ""
	QStr = "CODEFROM='KAAN'&EVRAKNOTARIH>='2009/10/01'&EVRAKNOTARIH>='" & MaliYilbasi & "'"
	Set EXC12 = Doc.Select1("___EXCRAXTT2","EXCRATT", QStr,"EVRAKNOTARIH",1)
	C(24) = 0
	
	SonSayac = Doc.GetKur("KAAN", "SAAT", LISTEE.RECTARIH11, "", -1)
	IlkSayac = EXC12.KURS_1
	C(24) = (SonSayac - IlkSayac) * 120
	
	QStr = "RECTARIH1>='2009/10/01'&RECTARIH1>='" & MaliYilbasi & "'&RECTARIH1<'" & LISTEE.RECTARIH11 & "'"
	Set S32E_REP1 = Doc.Select2("vvf3e4666", S32E_REP.GetAppName, QStr, "RECTARIH1", -1)
	For i = 1 to S32E_REP1.GetRowCount()
		if trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00", "GK_3", S32E_REP1.X_T_ISMERKEZI(i), "")) <> "T" Then
			if Trim(eskit) <> trim(S32E_REP1.RECTARIH1(i)) Then
				c(12) = c(12) + 96
				eskit = S32E_REP1.RECTARIH1(i)
			End If
			eskitarih = "GENERAL CUMMULATIVE OF LAST WEEK"
			input = GetUDF(S32E_REP1.EVRAKNO(i),"INPUT") - GetUDF(S32E_REP1.EVRAKNO(i),"IADE")
			farkg = Doc.DTDTDif1(S32E_REP1.RECTARIH1(i),S32E_REP1.RECTIME1(i), S32E_REP1.RECTARIH2(i),S32E_REP1.RECTIME2(i))
			sure = S32E_REP1.SURE(i)
			C(2) = C(2) + Doc.Div(input, sure) * farkg
			if trim(S32E_REP1.X_T_ISMERKEZI(i))<> "MARIS" and (trim(S32E_REP1.RECTYPE(i)) = "DS" or trim(S32E_REP1.RECTYPE(i)) = "R") Then
				C(4) = C(4) + Doc.Div(input, S32E_REP1.SURE(i)) * farkg
			End If
			mb1 = Doc.Div(GetUDF(S32E_REP1.EVRAKNO(i),"MB"), sure) * farkg
			me1 = Doc.Div(GetUDF(S32E_REP1.EVRAKNO(i),"ME"), sure) * farkg
			
			tezgah1 = S32E_REP1.X_T_ISMERKEZI(i)
			temizlik = GetUDF(S32E_REP1.EVRAKNO(i),"PP") + GetUDF(S32E_REP1.EVRAKNO(i),"PE") _
								+ GetUDF(S32E_REP1.EVRAKNO(i),"HDPE") + GetUDF(S32E_REP1.EVRAKNO(i),"KIRMA")
			C(18) = C(18) + (Doc.Div(temizlik, sure) * farkg)
			Select Case trim(tezgah1)
				Case "LEISTRITZ"
					C(7) = C(7) + mE1 - mB1
				Case "ZSK32K"
					C(8) = C(8) + mE1 - mB1
				Case "ZSK32S"
					C(9) = C(9) + mE1 - mB1
				Case "MARIS"
					C(10) = C(10) + mE1 - mB1
				Case "LAB-EX"
					C(11) = C(11) + mE1 - mB1
			End Select
			mpsno2 = S32E_REP1.MPSNO(i)
			if trim(mpsno2) <> trim(mpsno1) Then
				mpsno1 = mpsno2
				C(19) = C(19) + 1
				C(21) = C(21) + 1
				if trim(S32E_REP1.RECTYPE(i)) = "P" Then
					C(21) = C(21) + 1
				End If
			End If
		End If
	Next
	c(23) = 0
	excelkolon = excelkolon + 1
	call exceleyazdir(eskitarih, excelkolon)
End Sub
Sub hafta_hesapla()
	mpsno1 = ""
	for x = 1 to 35
		c(x) = 0
		d(x) = 0
	Next
	MaliYilbasi = GetMaliYilBasi()
	QStr = "RECTARIH1>= '" & LISTEE.RECTARIH11 & "'&RECTARIH1<= '" & LISTEE.RECTARIH12 & _
	"'&RECTARIH1>= '2009/10/01'&RECTARIH1>= '" & MaliYilbasi & "'"
	Set S32E_REP1 = Doc.Select2("sf4te666", S32E_REP.GetAppName, QStr, "", -1)
	
	For v = 1 to S32E_REP1.getreccount
		if trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00", "GK_3", S32E_REP1.X_T_ISMERKEZI(v), "")) <> "T" Then
			if S32E_REP1.RECTARIH1(v) <= LISTEE.RECTARIH12 and S32E_REP1.RECTARIH1(v) >= "2009/10/01" and S32E_REP1.RECTARIH1(v) >= LISTEE.RECTARIH11 Then
				if trim(yenitarih)<>trim(S32E_REP1.RECTARIH1(v)) Then
					mpsno1 = ""
					excelkolon = excelkolon + 1
					eskitarih = yenitarih
					YENitarih = S32E_REP1.RECTARIH1(v)
					call exceleyazdir(eskitarih, excelkolon)
					for x = 1 to 35
						c(x) = 0
						d(x) = 0
					Next
				End If
				input = GetUDF(S32E_REP1.EVRAKNO(v),"INPUT") - GetUDF(S32E_REP1.EVRAKNO(v),"IADE")
				farkg = Doc.DTDTDif1(S32E_REP1.RECTARIH1(v),S32E_REP1.RECTIME1(v), S32E_REP1.RECTARIH2(v),S32E_REP1.RECTIME2(v))
				sure = S32E_REP1.SURE(v)
				C(2) = C(2) + Doc.div(input, sure)*farkg
				if trim(S32E_REP1.X_T_ISMERKEZI(v))<>"MARIS" and ( trim(S32E_REP1.RECTYPE(v)) = "DS" or trim(S32E_REP1.RECTYPE(v)) = "R" ) Then
					C(4) = C(4) + Doc.div(input, sure)*farkg
				End If
				
				mb1 = Doc.div(GetUDF(S32E_REP1.EVRAKNO(v),"MB"), sure) * farkg
				me1 = Doc.div(GetUDF(S32E_REP1.EVRAKNO(v),"ME"), sure) * farkg
				
				tezgah1 = S32E_REP1.X_T_ISMERKEZI(v)
				temizlik = GetUDF(S32E_REP1.EVRAKNO(v),"PP") + GetUDF(S32E_REP1.EVRAKNO(v),"PE") _
				+ GetUDF(S32E_REP1.EVRAKNO(v),"HDPE") + GetUDF(S32E_REP1.EVRAKNO(v),"KIRMA")
				C(18) = C(18) + Doc.div(temizlik, sure)* farkg
				Select Case trim(tezgah1)
					Case "LEISTRITZ"
						C(7) = C(7) + mE1 - mB1
					Case "ZSK32K"
						C(8) = C(8) + mE1 - mB1
					Case "ZSK32S"
						C(9) = C(9) + mE1 - mB1
					Case "MARIS"
						C(10) = C(10) + mE1 - mB1
					Case "LAB-EX"
						C(11) = C(11) + mE1 - mB1
				End Select
				mpsno2 = S32E_REP1.MPSNO(v)
				C(12) = 96
				
				if trim(mpsno2)<>trim(mpsno1) Then
					mpsno1 = mpsno2
					C(19) = C(19) + 1
					C(21) = C(21) + 1
					if trim(S32E_REP1.RECTYPE(v)) = "P" Then
						C(21) = C(21) + 1
					End If
				End If
				
				C(23) = Doc.GetKur("KAAN", "SAAT", S32E_REP1.rectarih1(v), "", -1)
				
				ErtesiGun = Doc.StrDate_Increment(S32E_REP1.RECTARIH1(v), 1)
				D(23) = Doc.GetKur("KAAN", "SAAT", ErtesiGun, "", -1)
				if d(23)> 0 and c(23) >0 Then
					C(24) = (D(23) - C(23)) * 120
				End If
			End If
		End If
	Next
	
	excelkolon = excelkolon + 1
	call exceleyazdir(yenitarih, excelkolon)
End Sub
Function GetUDFValue2(U02E, AlanAdi)
	GetUDFValue2 = ""
	if U02E.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function
	Set U2 = Doc.Select2("de44dddr", U02E.GetAppName, "QS_VARCODE = '" & AlanAdi & "'", "", -1)
	if U2.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function
	
	if U2.QS_VARTYPE = "N" Then
		GetUDFValue2 = U2.QS_DVALUE
	Else
		GetUDFValue2 = trim(U2.QS_VALUE)
	End If
End Function
Function GetMaliYilBasi()
	MaliYilbasi = trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("ML_YILB"))
	MaliYilbasi = Right(MaliYilbasi, 4) & "/" & Mid(MaliYilbasi, 4, 2) & "/" & Left(MaliYilbasi, 2)
	GetMaliYilBasi = MaliYilbasi
End Function
Function GetUDF(EvrakNo, Alan)
	GetUDF = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E", EvrakNo, Alan, 0)
End Function
Sub SendTableToExcel(Tablo)
	Set pe2 = Doc.CreateSObject("ExcelOutput")
	PE2.SetOrientation "P"
	PE2.PageB_ReportHead_std1 Tablo.GetAppName

	Alanlar = Tablo.GetFields()
	ncol = 0
	For i=0 To uBound(Alanlar,1)
		AlanAdi = Alanlar(i,0)
		if Cikacak(AlanAdi) Then
			ncol = ncol+1
			if ncol > 256 Then Exit For
			AlanTuru = Alanlar(i,2)
				If AlanTuru = "INT16" or AlanTuru = "INT32" or AlanTuru = "DOUBLE" Then
				Align = "RIGHT"
			Else
				Align = "LEFT"
			End If
			pe2.AddColumnDef Alanlar(i,0), Alanlar(i,3), Alanlar(i,4), Align
			pe2.SetRowValue_String -1, AlanAdi
		End If
	Next
	PE2.LineOut

	For satirNo = 1 to Tablo.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Excel 'e gÃ¶nderiliyor : " & Tablo.GetAppName & " - " & i & " / " & Tablo.GetRowCount
		PE2.InitRowValues

		ExcelSatir = -1
		ncol = 0
		For i=0 To uBound(Alanlar,1)
			AlanAdi = Alanlar(i,0)
			if Cikacak(AlanAdi) Then
				ncol = ncol+1
				if ncol > 256 Then Exit For
				AlanTuru = Alanlar(i,2)
				If AlanTuru = "INT16" or AlanTuru = "INT32" or AlanTuru = "DOUBLE" Then
					Deger = Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo)
					pe2.SetRowValue_Double ExcelSatir, Deger
				ElseIf AlanTuru = "DATE" Then
					OutputStr=Doc.StrToNorDate(Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo))
					pe2.SetRowValue_String ExcelSatir,OutputStr
				Else
					pe2.SetRowValue_String ExcelSatir,Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo)
				End If
			End If
		Next
		PE2.LineOut
	Next
	pe2.Show
End Sub
Function Cikacak(AlanAdi)
	Cikacak = True
	if InStr(1, AlanAdi, "BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE_") > 0 Then
		Cikacak = False
	End If
	if InStr(1, AlanAdi, "BOMREC_CODE_") > 0 Then
		Cikacak = False
	End If
	if InStr(1, AlanAdi, "JOBNO_") > 0 Then
		Cikacak = False
	End If
	if InStr(1, AlanAdi, "MPSNO_") > 0 Then
		Cikacak = False
	End If
	if InStr(1, AlanAdi, "BOMREC_CODE_") > 0 Then
		Cikacak = False
	End If
End Function

Sub Makro1()
	toplamnm = 0
	Ay = Doc.StrDate_Month(Doc.BuGun) - 1
	Yil = Doc.StrDate_Year(Doc.BuGun)
	if Ay < 1 Then
		Ay = 12 - Ay
		Yil = Yil - 1
	End if
	DonemBasi = Yil & "/" & Right("00" & Ay, 2) & "/01" 	
	Set d7Date = Doc.CreateSObject("d7Date")
	d7Date.SetDate DonemBasi
	d7Date.AyinSonGununeGit()
	DonemSonu = d7Date.GetRevDate()
	Donem = Inputbox("Tarih Aralýðý Giriniz","Dinamo",DonemBasi & "-" & DonemSonu)
	if trim(Donem) = "" Then Exit Sub
	
	Tarih1 = Mid(trim(Donem), 1, 10)
	Tarih2 = Mid(trim(Donem), 12, 10)
	
	XXC8=0
	XC8=0
	tem = 0
	tem1 = 0
	s=0
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb = Excel.Workbooks.Add
	
	Set Sh1 = Wb.Sheets.Add
	Sh1.Name = "PRODUCTION SPLIT BY MACHINE"
	
	Sh1.Cells(1,3).Select
	Sh1.Cells(1,3).Font.Name="Arial"
	Sh1.Cells(1,3).Font.Size=12
	
	Sh1.Cells(1,3)= "PRODUCTION SPLIT BY MACHINE"
	Excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	ReDim Total(5, 13)
	
	Doc.CreateTableObject "ABCXYZ=SFDC32E","TMPCOPY"
	Set SF = Doc.GetTableObject("ABCXYZ")	
	
	For i=1 TO LISTET.GetRowCount()
		Call Sifirla()
		mpsno1=""

		SF.sort("mpsno+notes")
		For jj=1 to SF.GetRowCount()
			Doc.StatusBarMessage "Atölye kayýtlarý okunuyor : " & LISTET.KOD(i) & " - " & jj & " / " & SF.GetRowCount()
			ia = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"IADE",0)
			tip = SF.RECTYPE(jj)

'18/05/2010
			ab = GetGrade(SF.EVRAKNO(jj), SF.MPSNO(jj), SF.RECTARIH1(jj)) 
			neden = GetGradeNeden(SF.EVRAKNO(jj), SF.MPSNO(jj), SF.RECTARIH1(jj))

			input = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"INPUT",0)
			netinput = input - Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"IADE",0)
			output = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"OUTPUT",0)
			elekalti = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"EAA",0)
			NM = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"NM",0)

			kmb = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"MB",0)
'18/05/2010
'			netoutput=output+elekalti
			netoutput=output+elekalti+nm
			tem = tem + Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"PP",0) + Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"PE",0)+Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"HDPE",0) + Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"KIRMA",0)
			tem1 = tem1 + Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"PP",0) + Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"PE",0)+Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",SF.EVRAKNO(jj),"HDPE",0)
			farkg = Doc.DTDTDif1(SF.RECTARIH1(jj),SF.RECTIME1(jj), SF.RECTARIH2(jj),SF.RECTIME2(jj))
			sure = sf.SURE(jj)
			toplamnm = toplamnm + nm
			L5 = l5 + kme - kmb
			
			Kriter = "MPSNO='" & mpsno1 & "'&RECTARIH1<='" & Tarih2 & "'"
			sab1 = 0

			Set s20t=Doc.Select1("___tABLE10zzzzz","STOK20T", QStr,"",-1)

				if Left(ab1, 1)="A" Then
					sab1=1
				End If
			next
			if trim(SF.RECTYPE(jj))<>"D" and trim(SF.RECTYPE(jj))<>"P" Then

					mpsno1 = SF.MPSNO(jj)					
					select Case trim(ab)

						Case "AB"

						Case "B"

					End Select

			Total(4, 9) = Doc.Div(Total(4,8), Total(4,4))
			Total(5, 9) = Doc.Div(Total(5,8), Total(5,4))
			
			Total(0, 10) = "HOURS"
			Total(1, 10) = Total(1,10) + l5
			Total(3, 10) = Total(3,10) + l7
			Total(4, 10) = Total(4,10) + l8
			Total(5, 10) = Total(5,10) + l9
			
'			Total(2, 10) = Total(3,10) + Total(4,10)+ Total(5,10)
			Total(2, 10) = Total(3,10) + Total(5,10)
			
			Total(0, 11) = "KG / HR"
			Total(1, 11) = Doc.Div(Total(1,6), Total(1,10))
			Total(2, 11) = Doc.Div(Total(2,6), Total(2,10))
			Total(3, 11) = Doc.Div(Total(3,6), Total(3,10))
			Total(4, 11) = Doc.Div(Total(4,6), Total(4,10))
			Total(5, 11) = Doc.Div(Total(5,6), Total(5,10))
		End If
		'----------------------
	next
	s=s+5
	Sh1.Cells(s,2).Select
	Excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	
	
	For xx=0 to 5
		s=s+1
		For z=0 to 11
			Sh1.Cells(s,2).Select
			Excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
			if xx=0 Then
				Sh1.Cells(s,z+2).Select
				Excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
			End If
			
			if z=0 or xx=0 Then
				Sh1.Cells(s,z+2)= Total(xx, z)
			elseif z = 9 Then
				Sh1.Cells(s,z+2)= Doc.NumFormat(Total(xx, z)*100, 10,3)
				
			Else
				Sh1.Cells(s,z+2)= Doc.NumFormat(Total(xx, z), 10,2)
			End If
		Next
	Next
	Call Excele_yaz2()
	Excel.Visible = True
	
End Sub

Sub SureHesapla(satirn)
	BaslangicTarihi = Doc.StrDate_Increment(Tarih1, -20)
	BitisTarihi = Doc.StrDate_Increment(Tarih2, 20)
	QStr = "X_T_ISMERKEZI='" & LISTET.KOD(satirn) & "'"
	QStr = QStr & "&RECTARIH1>='" & BaslangicTarihi & "'&RECTARIH1<='" & BitisTarihi & "'"
	Set LISTEG = Doc.Select1("___tABLE10XXCCX","SFDC32E",QStr,"",-1)
	
	For tt=1 to LISTEG.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Süreler Hesaplanýyor : " & trim(LISTET.KOD(satirn)) & " - " & tt & " / " & LISTEG.GetRowCount()
		mpsno = LISTEG.MPSNO(tt)
		'lbc = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",LISTEG.EVRAKNO(tt),"LBC",0)
		lbc = GetGrade(LISTEG.EVRAKNO(tt), LISTEG.MPSNO(tt), LISTEG.RECTARIH1(tt))
		if Left(lbc, 1) = "A" Then
			Kriter = "MPSNO='" & mpsno & "'&TARIH >='" & Tarih1 &"'&TARIH <='" & Tarih2 & "'"
			Set s70t=Doc.Select1("___tABLE10","STOK20T", kriter,"",-1)
			if s70t.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
				SF.ADDROW
				s1=sf.GetRowCount()
				sf.evrakno(s1) = LISTEG.evrakno(tt)
				sf.MPSNO(s1) = LISTEG.MPSNO(tt)
				sf.RECTYPE(s1) = LISTEG.RECTYPE(tt)
				sf.X_T_ISMERKEZI(s1) = LISTEG.X_T_ISMERKEZI(tt)
				sf.rectarih1(s1) = LISTEG.RECTARIH1(tt)
				sf.rectime1(s1) = LISTEG.RECTIME1(tt)
				sf.RECTARIH2(s1) = LISTEG.RECTARIH2(tt)
				sf.rectime2(s1) = LISTEG.RECTIME2(tt)
				sf.NOTES(s1) = trim(lbc)
			End If
		Else
			if LISTEG.RECTARIH1(tt) >= Tarih1 and LISTEG.RECTARIH1(tt)<= Tarih2 Then
				SF.ADDROW
				s1=sf.GetRowCount()
				sf.evrakno(s1) = LISTEG.evrakno(tt)
				sf.MPSNO(s1) = LISTEG.MPSNO(tt)
				sf.RECTYPE(s1) = LISTEG.RECTYPE(tt)
				sf.X_T_ISMERKEZI(s1) = LISTEG.X_T_ISMERKEZI(tt)
				sf.rectarih1(s1) = LISTEG.RECTARIH1(tt)
				sf.rectime1(s1) = LISTEG.RECTIME1(tt)
				sf.RECTARIH2(s1) = LISTEG.RECTARIH2(tt)
				sf.rectime2(s1) = LISTEG.RECTIME2(tt)
				sf.NOTES(s1) = trim(lbc)
			End If
		End If
	Next
End Sub
Sub KutuCiz(aralik)
	Sh2.Range(aralik).Select
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlDiagonalDown).LineStyle = xlNone
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlDiagonalUp).LineStyle = xlNone
	
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeLeft).LineStyle = xlContinuous
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeLeft).Weight = xlThin
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeLeft).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
	
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeTop).LineStyle = xlContinuous
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeTop).Weight = xlThin
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeTop).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
	
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeBottom).LineStyle = xlContinuous
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeBottom).Weight = xlThin
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeBottom).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
	
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeRight).LineStyle = xlContinuous
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeRight).Weight = xlThin
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeRight).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
	
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlInsideVertical).LineStyle = xlNone
End Sub
Sub BaslikAyarla(aralik)
	Sh2.Range(aralik).Select
	Excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	Excel.Selection.Font.Name = "Arial Tur"
	Excel.Selection.Font.Size = 12
	Excel.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = xlCenterAcrossSelection
	Excel.Selection.VerticalAlignment = xlBottom
	Excel.Selection.WrapText = False
	Excel.Selection.Orientation = 0
	Excel.Selection.AddIndent = False
	Excel.Selection.IndentLevel = 0
	Excel.Selection.ShrinkToFit = False
	Excel.Selection.ReadingOrder = xlContext
	Excel.Selection.MergeCells = False
End Sub

	Excel.Selection.Orientation = 0
	Excel.Selection.AddIndent = False

	Excel.Selection.ReadingOrder = xlContext
	Excel.Selection.MergeCells = True
End Sub

End Sub

	Excel.Selection.Interior.Pattern = xlSolid
End Sub
Sub Satiryuksekligi(aralik, deger)
	Sh2.Rows(aralik).Select
	Excel.Selection.RowHeight = deger
End Sub

Function GetGrade(CalismaBildirimNo, MPSNo, Tarih)
	if Tarih <= "2010/05/19" or trim(CalismaBildirimNo) = "" Then
		GetGrade = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",CalismaBildirimNo,"LBC",0)
	Else
		GetGrade = Doc.GetUDFValue("MMPS10E",MPSNo,"LBC",0)
		if trim(GetGrade) = "" Then GetGrade = "A"
	End if
End Function
Function GetGradeNeden(CalismaBildirimNo, MPSNo, Tarih)
	if Tarih <= "2010/05/19" or trim(CalismaBildirimNo) = "" Then
		GetGradeNeden = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",CalismaBildirimNo,"BGR",0)
	Else
		GetGradeNeden = Doc.GetUDFValue("MMPS10E",MPSNo,"BGR",0)
	End if
End Function

Yeni Alt Ekra Form Kodu:

Hangi Tablo için : STOK60

Sub Makro1()
	Set S60T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60T")
	ret = S60T.ExportRowToXML(S60T.GetCurrentRow())
	satirNo = S60T.AddRow
	S60T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
	msgbox satirNo & " , " & ret
	S60T.ImportRowFromXML satirNo, ret
End Sub                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            

Ekran Formu

Baþlangýçta Bu Makro Çalýþacak :

Alt Ekran Form Kodu : 
Yeni Alt Ekra Form Kodu: 
Alt Ekran Açýlmadan Önce Çalýþtýrýlacak Makro :

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : SNK10 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : ETIKET
