Function GetEtiketTable(EtiketFormu)
	if Doc.GetDocType = "STOK20" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK20_EtiketYarat()
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK68" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK68_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK83" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK83_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK60" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK60_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK25" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK25_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "TRACK25" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = TRACK25_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK29" or Doc.GetDocType = "STOK29I" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK29_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK36" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK36_EtiketYarat()
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK19" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK19_EtiketYarat()
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK26" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = Transfer_EtiketYarat(STOK26T)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK63" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = Transfer_EtiketYarat(STOK63T)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK61" Then
 		Set GetEtiketTable = Transfer_EtiketYarat(STOK61T)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK24" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK24_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "MMPS10" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = MMPS10_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK46" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK46_EtiketYarat()
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK32" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK32_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu, Miktar)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "D7KIDSPL" Then
'		Set GetEtiketTable = D7KIDSPL_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
		Set GetEtiketTable = D7KIDSLB_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "D7KIDSLB" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = D7KIDSLB_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK00" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK00_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	End if
End Function
Function D7KIDSLB_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Set D7KIDSLB_EtiketYarat = Doc.GetTableObject("D7KIDSLB")
End Function
Function MMPS10_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")
	Set d7LB = Doc1.GetTableObject("D7KIDSLB")

	Doc1.CreateTableObject "xxx11dw=D7KIDSLB", "TMPCOPY"
	Set d7LB1 = Doc1.GetTableObject("xxx11dw")
	
	Set M10TM = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10TM")
	Set M10E = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")
	M10TM.SafePack()
	For j = 1 to M10TM.GetRowCount()
		Miktar = M10TM.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(j)
		'DefKutuIciMiktar = GetStdKoliAdet(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(j), M10TM.MUSTERIKODU(j), "")
		KutuIciMiktar = Doc.GetUDFValue("MMPS10E", M10E.EVRAKNO, "KOLI_MIK",0)
		KutuIciMiktar = doc.Round(KutuIciMiktar,0)

		'EtkTarih = Doc.GetUDFValue("MMPS10E", M10E.EVRAKNO, "ETK_TRH",0)

		'if KutuIciMiktar = 0 then KutuIciMiktar = DefKutuIciMiktar
		if KutuIciMiktar = 0 then KutuIciMiktar = Miktar
		EtiketAdedi = Doc.GetUDFValue("MMPS10E", M10E.EVRAKNO, "ETIK_ADT",0)	
		if EtiketAdedi = 0 Then EtiketAdedi = 1

		for i = 1 to EtiketAdedi
			Redim IDArray(1,0)
			KutuMiktari = KutuIciMiktar

			SeriNo = trim(M10E.EVRAKNO)
			BarkodNo = Doc.Sayac0danAl("SERINO")
			Doc1.Load_Voucher BarkodNo

			d7LB.SKOD = M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(j)
'			d7LB.LOTNUMBER = Replace(Doc.BuGun, "/", "")
			d7LB.LOTNUMBER = "0" & trim(M10E.EVRAKNO)
			d7LB.SERINO = SeriNo
			d7LB.SF_MIKTAR = KutuMiktari
			d7LB.SF_UNIT = M10TM.SF_SF_UNIT(j)
			d7LB.MPSNO = M10E.EVRAKNO
			d7LB.FOKOD = M10TM.FOKOD_MAMULKODU(j)
			d7LB.MPSTM_TRNUM = M10TM.TRNUM(j)
			d7LB.AMBCODE1 = "QC"
			d7LB.AMBCODE2 = "IML"
			d7LB.AMBCODE3 = "IML"

			d7LB.REFTARIH1 = Doc.BuGun()

			CHKodu = M10TM.MUSTERIKODU(j)
			d7LB.CH_MUSTERI = CHKodu
			d7LB.FORMKODU = EtiketFormu
			d7LB.STATU = ""
			d7LB.APPKODU = "MMPS10"
			d7LB.EVRAKTYPE = "UP"
			d7LB.EVRAKNO = M10E.EVRAKNO

			Doc1.Save_Voucher
			Set d7LB2 = Doc1.Select2("___D7KIDSLB1", "D7KIDSLB", "", "", -1)
			d7LB1.AppendTable(d7LB2)
		Next
	Next

	Set MMPS10_EtiketYarat = d7LB1
End Function
Function UretimBarkod_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")
	Set d7LB = Doc1.GetTableObject("D7KIDSLB")

	Doc1.CreateTableObject "xxx11dw=D7KIDSLB", "TMPCOPY"
	Set d7LB1 = Doc1.GetTableObject("xxx11dw")
	Set UretimBarkod_EtiketYarat = d7LB1

	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")

	Miktar = MTable.SF_MIKTAR
	KutuIciMiktar = Miktar 
		if trim(MTable.ELEKALTI) = "E" Then
		EtiketAdedi = 1
	Else
		EtiketAdedi = InputBox("Kaç adet etiket basýlacak?", "", "1")
		EtiketAdedi = Doc.CDbl(EtiketAdedi)
		if EtiketAdedi = 0 Then Exit Function
	End if
	For i = 1 to EtiketAdedi
		Redim IDArray(1,0)
		KutuMiktari = KutuIciMiktar

		'Grade = MTable.GRADE
		'SeriNo = Grade
		BarkodNo = Doc.Sayac0danAl("SERINO")
		Doc1.Load_Voucher BarkodNo

		d7LB.SKOD = MTable.MAMULSTOKKODU
'		d7LB.LOTNUMBER = Doc.GetUDFValue("MMPS10E", MTable.EVRAKNO, "PARTI", 0)
		d7LB.LOTNUMBER = MTable.LOTNUMBER
		'd7LB.SERINO = MTable.Grade
		d7LB.SF_MIKTAR = KutuMiktari
		d7LB.SF_UNIT = MTable.SF_SF_UNIT
		d7LB.MPSNO = MTable.EVRAKNO
		d7LB.FOKOD = MTable.FOKOD_MAMULKODU
		d7LB.MPSTM_TRNUM = MTable.TRNUM
		if trim(MTable.ELEKALTI) = "E" Then
			d7LB.REFTEXT01 = "ELEKALTI"
			d7LB.AMBCODE1 = "EAA"		
		Else
			d7LB.AMBCODE1 = "QC"		
		End if
		d7LB.AMBCODE2 = "IML"
		d7LB.AMBCODE3 = "IML"

		d7LB.REFTARIH1 = Doc.BuGun()

		CHKodu = MTable.MUSTERIKODU
		d7LB.CH_MUSTERI = CHKodu
		d7LB.FORMKODU = EtiketFormu
		d7LB.STATU = "OK"
		d7LB.APPKODU = "MMPS10"
		d7LB.EVRAKTYPE = "UP"
		d7LB.EVRAKNO = MTable.EVRAKNO

		Doc1.Save_Voucher
		Set d7LB2 = Doc1.Select2("___D7KIDSLB1", "D7KIDSLB", "", "", -1)
		d7LB1.AppendTable(d7LB2)
	Next

	Set UretimBarkod_EtiketYarat = d7LB1
End Function

Function STOK29_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")
	Set d7LB = Doc1.GetTableObject("D7KIDSLB")

	Doc1.CreateTableObject "xxx11dw=D7KIDSLB", "TMPCOPY"
	Set d7LB1 = Doc1.GetTableObject("xxx11dw")

	Set STOK29_EtiketYarat = d7LB1

	Set MT2 = Doc.Select2G("fw34ffrfr", "MAKROTABLET2", "", "", "KOD", -1)
	Set g = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	For i = 1 to MT2.GetRowCOunt()
		Set S29T2 = Doc.Select2G("wf434f4f4", "STOK29T", "KOD='" & MT2.KOD(i) & "'", "", "KOD", -1)
		if S29T2.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			if S29T2.SF_MIKTAR <> MT2.SF_MIKTAR(i) Then
				Doc.d7MsgBox "Stok Kodu : " & trim(MT2.KOD(i)) & ", Ýrsaliye Miktarý : " & S29T2.SF_MIKTAR & _
							", Etiket Basýlan Miktar : " & MT2.SF_MIKTAR(i), "", MB_OK
				Hata = True
			End if
		End if
	Next
	Set g = Nothing
	if Hata Then
		k = Doc.d7MsgBox("Ýþleme devam etmek istiyor musunuz", "", MB_YESNO)
		if k <> 6 Then Exit Function
	End if

	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET2")
	Set S29E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK29E")

	For i = 1 to MTableT2.GetRowCount()
		Barkod = Doc.LeftJustify(trim(MTableT2.EDEFF__ETIKET_NO(i)), 30)
		Set D7LB_111 = Doc.Select1("dee3ffghg7y66", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Barkod & "'", "", -1)
		if trim(Barkod) <> "" and D7LB_111.GetRowCount = 0 Then
			Doc1.Load_Voucher Barkod
	
			d7LB.SKOD = MTableT2.KOD(i)
			d7LB.APPKODU = "STOK29"
			d7LB.EVRAKTYPE = "SI"
			d7LB.EVRAKNO = S29E.EVRAKNO
			d7LB.LOTNUMBER = MTableT2.LOTNUMBER(i)
			d7LB.SERINO = MTableT2.SERINO(i)
			d7LB.SF_MIKTAR = MTableT2.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			d7LB.SF_UNIT = MTableT2.SF_SF_UNIT(i)
			d7LB.VRI_NUM1 = MTableT2.SF_VRI_NUM1(i)
			d7LB.VRI_NUM2 = MTableT2.SF_VRI_NUM2(i)
			d7LB.FOKOD = MTableT2.FOKOD(i)
			d7LB.VRI_VR_C = MTableT2.SF_VRI_VR_C(i)
			d7LB.VRI_VR_R1 = MTableT2.SF_VRI_VR_R1(i)
			d7LB.VRI_VR_R2 = MTableT2.SF_VRI_VR_R2(i)
			d7LB.VRI_VR_R3 = MTableT2.SF_VRI_VR_R3(i)
			d7LB.AMBCODE1 = S29E.AMBCODE
			d7LB.CH_TEDARIKCI = S29E.CARIHESAPCODE
			d7LB.SIPARISNO_46 = MTableT2.SIPNO(i)
			d7LB.SIPARTNO_46 = MTableT2.SIPARTNO(i)
			d7LB.FORMKODU = EtiketFormu
			d7LB.REFTARIH1 = S29E.TARIH
			Doc1.Save_Voucher

			Set d7LB2 = Doc1.Select2("___D7KIDSLB1", "D7KIDSLB", "", "", -1)
			d7LB1.AppendTable(d7LB2)
		End if
	Next
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	Set STOK29_EtiketYarat = d7LB1
End Function
Function Palet_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")
	Set d7LB = Doc1.GetTableObject("D7KIDSLB")

	Set M10TM = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10TM")
	Set M10E = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")
	M10TM.SafePack()
	For j = 1 to M10TM.GetRowCount()
		Miktar = M10TM.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(j)
		KutuIciMiktar = Doc.GetUDFValue("MMPS10E", M10E.EVRAKNO, "PALET_MIK",0)
		KutuIciMiktar = doc.Round(KutuIciMiktar,0)

		EtiketAdedi = 1
		for i = 1 to EtiketAdedi
			KutuMiktari = KutuIciMiktar

			SeriNo = trim(M10E.EVRAKNO)
			BarkodNo = Doc.Sayac0danAl("SERINO")
			Doc1.Load_Voucher BarkodNo

			d7LB.SKOD = M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(j)
'			d7LB.LOTNUMBER = Replace(Doc.BuGun, "/", "")
			d7LB.LOTNUMBER = "0" & trim(M10E.EVRAKNO)
			d7LB.SERINO = SeriNo
			d7LB.SF_MIKTAR = KutuMiktari
			d7LB.SF_UNIT = M10TM.SF_SF_UNIT(j)
			d7LB.MPSNO = M10E.EVRAKNO
			d7LB.FOKOD = M10TM.FOKOD_MAMULKODU(j)
			d7LB.MPSTM_TRNUM = M10TM.TRNUM(j)
			d7LB.AMBCODE1 = "MA"
			d7LB.AMBCODE2 = "IM"
			d7LB.AMBCODE3 = "IM"

			d7LB.REFTARIH1 = Doc.BuGun()

			CHKodu = M10TM.MUSTERIKODU(j)
			if trim(CHKodu) = "" Then
				CHKodu = GetMusteriCHKodu(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(j))
			End if
			d7LB.CH_MUSTERI = CHKodu
			d7LB.FORMKODU = EtiketFormu
			d7LB.STATU = ""
			d7LB.APPKODU = "MMPS10"
			d7LB.EVRAKTYPE = "UP"
			d7LB.EVRAKNO = M10E.EVRAKNO

			Doc1.Save_Voucher
			Set d7LB2 = Doc1.Select2("___D7KIDSLB1", "D7KIDSLB", "", "", -1)
		Next
	Next

Function STOK83_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")
	Set d7LB = Doc1.GetTableObject("D7KIDSLB")

	Doc1.CreateTableObject "xxx11dw=D7KIDSLB", "TMPCOPY"

	Set STOK83_EtiketYarat = d7LB1

	Set S83T = Doc.Select2("wdf234fwf", "STOK83T", "", "", -1)
	S83T.SafePack
	If not IsNull(S83T.GetFieldDef("YENI_LOTNUMBER")) Then
		For i = 1 to S83T.GetRowCount()
			if trim(S83T.YENI_LOTNUMBER(i)) <> "" Then
				S83T.LOTNUMBER(i) = S83T.YENI_LOTNUMBER(i)
			End if
		Next
	End if
	Set S83E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83E")
	
	S83T.GroupBy2("KOD+LOTNUMBER")
	For i = 1 to S83T.GetRowCount()
		BarkodNo = Doc.Sayac0danAl("SERINO")
		Doc1.Load_Voucher BarkodNo

		d7LB.SKOD = S83T.KOD(i)
		d7LB.LOTNUMBER = S83T.LOTNUMBER(i)
		SeriNo = trim(S83T.SERINO(i))
		d7LB.SERINO = SeriNo
		d7LB.SF_MIKTAR = S83T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		d7LB.SF_UNIT = S83T.SF_SF_UNIT(i)
		d7LB.FOKOD = S83T.FOKOD(i)

		d7LB.FORMKODU = EtiketFormu
		d7LB.STATU = "OK"
		d7LB.APPKODU = "STOK83"
		d7LB.EVRAKTYPE = "UT"
		d7LB.EVRAKNO = S83E.EVRAKNO
		d7LB.AMBCODE1 = S83E.TARGETAMBCODE

		Doc1.Save_Voucher
		Set d7LB2 = Doc1.Select2("___D7KIDSLB1", "D7KIDSLB", "", "", -1)

Function STOK60_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")
	Set d7LB = Doc1.GetTableObject("D7KIDSLB")

	Doc1.CreateTableObject "xxx11dw=D7KIDSLB", "TMPCOPY"
	Set d7LB1 = Doc1.GetTableObject("xxx11dw")
	
	Set S60T1 = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60T")
	S60T1.SafePack()
	Set S60T = Doc.Select2("dddeesw", "STOK60T", "FATURASIZMI<>'E'", "", -1)
	Set S60E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60E")
	S60T.GroupBy2("KOD")
	For i = 1 to S60T.GetRowCount()
		Miktar = S60T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		KutuIciMiktar = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "B_B_TECH_1", S60T.KOD(i), "")
		KutuIciMiktar = doc.Round(KutuIciMiktar,0)
		if KutuIciMiktar = 0 then KutuIciMiktar = Miktar

		EtiketAdedi = Doc.Ceil(Doc.Div(Miktar, KutuIciMiktar))
		if EtiketAdedi = 0 Then EtiketAdedi = 1

		for j = 1 to EtiketAdedi
			KutuMiktari = KutuIciMiktar

			BarkodNo = Doc.Sayac0danAl("SERINO")
			Doc1.Load_Voucher BarkodNo

			d7LB.SKOD = S60T.KOD(i)
			d7LB.LOTNUMBER = S60T.LOTNUMBER(i)
			SeriNo = trim(S60T.SERINO(i))
			d7LB.SERINO = SeriNo
			d7LB.SF_MIKTAR = KutuMiktari
			d7LB.SF_UNIT = S60T.SF_SF_UNIT(i)
			d7LB.FOKOD = S60T.FOKOD(i)
			d7LB.REFTARIH1 = S60E.TARIH
			CHKodu = S60E.CH_KODU
			d7LB.CH_MUSTERI = CHKodu
			d7LB.FORMKODU = EtiketFormu
			d7LB.STATU = "OK"
			d7LB.APPKODU = "STOK60"
			d7LB.EVRAKTYPE = "RI"
			d7LB.EVRAKNO = S60E.EVRAKNO
			d7LB.REFNUM01 = S60T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			d7LB.AMBCODE1 = S60E.AMBCODE

			Doc1.Save_Voucher
			Set d7LB2 = Doc1.Select2("___D7KIDSLB1", "D7KIDSLB", "", "", -1)
			d7LB1.AppendTable(d7LB2)
		Next
	Next

	Set STOK60_EtiketYarat = d7LB1
End Function

		Set D7LB_111 = Doc.Select1("dee3ffghg7y66", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & d7PL.D_ID(i) & "'", "", -1)

Function GetFasonInfo(Kod, LotNo, SeriNo)
	Dim FasonArr(2)
	QStr = "KOD='" & Kod & "'&LOTNUMBER='" & LotNo & "'&SERINO='" & SeriNo & "'"
	Set S68T = Doc.Select1("deefft66", "STOK68T", QStr, "", -1)
	if S68T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		FasonArr(0) = S68T.CHCODE
		FasonArr(1) = S68T.SIPNO
		FasonArr(2) = S68T.SIPARTNO
	End if
	GetFasonInfo = FasonArr
End Function
Function GetSonrakiOp(MPSNo, IsNo)
	SrNum = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "SRNUM", IsNo, "JOBNO")
	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("ded44hh7j", "MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO='" & MPSNo & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'&SRNUM>'" & SrNum & "'", "SRNUM", -1)
	if M10T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		GetSonrakiOp = M10T.JOBNO
	Else
		GetSonrakiOp = "DEPO"
	End if
End Function

Sub Makro1()
'	Dim MTable
'	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
'	MTable.Addfield2 "ARANACAKDEYIM","CHAR",30,0,"L","",""
'	MTable.ARANACAKDEYIM=""

	AnaTabloStr = "STOK29T"
	Set AnaTablo = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK29T")
	AnaTablo.SafePack()
	Dim MTableT, MTableT2
	Set MTableT=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET=STACK","IO")

	MTableT.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	Call AddAllFields(MTableT)
	Call AddUDEFFields(MTableT, "S29TETK")
	Call AlanlariDoldur(AnaTablo, MTableT)
	MTableT.SetRowAID 0,0,0

	Set MTableT2=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET2=STACK","IO")

	MTableT2.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	Call AddAllFields(MTableT2)
	Call AddUDEFFields(MTableT2, "S29TETK")
	MTableT2.SetRowAID 0,0,0

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	EkranFormu = GetEkranFormu(AnaTabloStr, AnaTablo, "S29TETK")
'	Call MsgBoxToTxt("", EkranFormu)
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU1",EkranFormu, Par
End Sub
Function GetEkranFormu(AnaTabloStr, AnaTablo, KTA_Adi)
'	EnBoy = GetScreenWidthAndHeight()
'	En = EnBoy(0)
'	Boy = EnBoy(1)
	En = 0
	Boy = 0

	if En = 0 or Boy = 0 Then
		En = 1000
		Boy = 600
	Else
		En = En - 24
		Boy = Boy - 60
	End if 
	TabEn = En - 25
	TabBoy = Boy - 25

	GridEn = TabEn - 25
	GridBoy = TabBoy - 90

	FormStr = "FORM MAKROFORMU1 HEIGHT=" & Boy & "P WIDTH=" & En & "P SCROLLBARS=NO CAPTIONTYPE=SYS" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "SET CAPTION= " & Chr(34) & "Barkod Etiket Basýmý" & Chr(34) & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "MENUDEF" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "   POPUP TEXT=" & Chr(34) & "Dosya" & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "   MENUITEM TEXT=" & Chr(34) & "Ýþlem Yapmadan Çý&kýþ" & Chr(34) & " ID=ID_APP_CANCEL KEY=ESCAPE" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "   ENDPOPUP" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDMENUDEF" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "TABDEF X=0p Y=+0p WIDTH=" & TabEn & "p H=" & TabBoy & "p AUTOSIZE=N" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & 	"TAB Name=""Etiket Adetleri Giriþ"" CAPTION="" Etiket Adetleri Giriþ """ & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & 	"TAB Name=""Etiketler"" CAPTION="" Etiketler""" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDTABDEF" & vbCrLf

'**
	FormStr = FormStr & "TAB ""Etiket Adetleri Giriþ""" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "BUTTON LEFT=3 TOP=25p WIDTH=20 HEIGHT=2 CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & "Etiketleri Hesapla" & Chr(34) & " ID=" & Chr(34) & "MT_RUNMACRO_AFTERCALC('MAKR7S.@@@BUMAKRO@@@','EtiketleriHesapla')" & chr(34) & vbcrlf
'	FormStr = FormStr & "BUTTON LEFT=E+2 TOP=+0 WIDTH=20 HEIGHT=2 CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & "Çýkýþ" & Chr(34) & " ID=ID_APP_CANCEL" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "GRID LEFT=1 TOP=E+10p HEIGHT=" & GridBoy & "p WIDTH=" & GridEn & "p TABLE=MAKROTABLET HEADERROWHEIGHT=35P" & vbCrLf

	MainTableStr = GetMainTableStr()
	FormStr = FormStr & MainTableStr & vbCrLf

	UDFStr = GetUDFStr(KTA_Adi)
	FormStr = FormStr & UDFStr & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDGRID" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDTAB" & vbCrLf

'**
	FormStr = FormStr & "TAB ""Etiketler""" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "BUTTON LEFT=3 TOP=25p WIDTH=20 HEIGHT=2 CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & "Etiketleri Yazdýr" & Chr(34) & " ID=" & Chr(34) & "MT_RUNMACRO_AFTERCALC('PFRM7S.ETK-HMD','PrintLabel')" & chr(34) & vbcrlf
	FormStr = FormStr & "GRID LEFT=1 TOP=E+10p HEIGHT=" & GridBoy & "p WIDTH=" & GridEn & "p TABLE=MAKROTABLET2 HEADERROWHEIGHT=35P" & vbCrLf

	MainTableStr = GetMainTableStr()
	FormStr = FormStr & MainTableStr & vbCrLf

	UDFStr = GetUDFStr(KTA_Adi)
	FormStr = FormStr & UDFStr & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDGRID" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDTAB" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDFORM" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = Replace(FormStr, "@@@BUMAKRO@@@", SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME)

	GetEkranFormu = FormStr
End Function
Function GetUDFStr(KTA_Adi)
	UDFStr = ""
	Set U10T = Doc.Select1("dd333", "UFLD10T", "EVRAKNO='" & KTA_Adi & "'", "", -1)
	For i = 1 to U10T.GetRowCount()
		AlanAdi = "EDEFF__" & trim(U10T.VARCODE(i))
		AlanIndex = U10T.VARINDEX(i)
		if trim(AlanIndex) <> "" Then
			AlanAdi = AlanAdi & "_" & trim(AlanIndex)
		End if
		Aciklama = trim(U10T.VARASPNAME(i))
		AlanTuru = trim(U10T.VARTYPE(i))
		Grupkodu = trim(U10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT3(i))
		if AlanTuru = "D" Then
			UDFStr = UDFStr & "COMBOBOX D=" & AlanAdi & " CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & Aciklama & Chr(34) 
		Elseif AlanTuru = "T" and trim(Grupkodu) <> "" Then
			UDFStr = UDFStr & "COMBOBOX D=" & AlanAdi & " CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & Aciklama & Chr(34) 
		Else
			UDFStr = UDFStr & "EDITTEXT D=" & AlanAdi & " CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & Aciklama & Chr(34) 
		End if
		if Len(trim(U10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT1(i))) = 15 Then
			UDFStr = UDFStr & " BCKCOL=" & trim(U10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT1(i))
		End if
		UDFStr = UDFStr & vbCrLf
	Next
	GetUDFStr = UDFStr
End Function
Sub AddUDEFFields(MTableT, KTA_Adi)
	Set U10T = Doc.Select1("dd333", "UFLD10T", "EVRAKNO='"  & KTA_Adi & "'", "", -1)
	For i = 1 to U10T.GetRowCount()
		AlanAdi = "EDEFF__" & trim(U10T.VARCODE(i))
		AlanIndex = U10T.VARINDEX(i)
		if trim(AlanIndex) <> "" Then
			AlanAdi = AlanAdi & "_" & trim(AlanIndex)
		End if
		Aciklama = trim(U10T.VARASPNAME(i))
		AlanTuru = trim(U10T.VARTYPE(i))
		Grupkodu = trim(U10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT3(i))
		Tablo = trim(U10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT4(i))
		Uzunluk = U10T.VARLEN(i)
		Ondalik = U10T.VARSIG(i)
		if AlanTuru = "D" Then
			MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "DATE",10,0,"L","",""
		Elseif AlanTuru = "T" and trim(Grupkodu) <> "" Then
			MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "CHAR",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"L","",Grupkodu
		Elseif AlanTuru = "T" and trim(Tablo) <> "" Then
			MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "CHAR",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"L","F2=DO_BRW('" & Tablo & "')",""
		Elseif AlanTuru = "T" Then
			MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "CHAR",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"L","",""
		Elseif AlanTuru = "N" Then
			MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "DOUBLE",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"R","",""
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Sub AddAllFields(MTableT)
	Grid = Doc.GetGridColInfo("", "STOK29T")
	For i = 0 to uBound(Grid, 1)
		AlanAdi = Grid(i,0)
		Aciklama = Grid(i,2)
		AlanTuru = Grid(i,4)
		Grupkodu = ""
		Tablo = ""
		Uzunluk = Grid(i,5)
		Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
		if AlanTuru = "D" Then
			MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "DATE",10,0,"L","",""
		Elseif AlanTuru = "C" and trim(Grupkodu) <> "" Then
			MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "CHAR",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"L","",Grupkodu
		Elseif AlanTuru = "C" and trim(Tablo) <> "" Then
			MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "CHAR",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"L","F2=DO_BRW('" & Tablo & "')",""
		Elseif AlanTuru = "C" Then
			MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "CHAR",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"L","",""
		Elseif AlanTuru = "N" Then
			MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "DOUBLE",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"R","",""
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Function GetMainTableStr()
	MainTableStr = ""
	Grid = Doc.GetGridColInfo("", "STOK29T")
	For i = 0 to uBound(Grid, 1)
		AlanAdi = Grid(i,0)
		Aciklama = Replace(Grid(i,2), "\n", " ")
		Aciklama = Replace(Aciklama, chr(13), " ")
		Aciklama = Replace(Aciklama, chr(10), " ")
		AlanTuru = Grid(i,4)
		Grupkodu = ""
		Tablo = ""
		if AlanTuru = "D" Then
			MainTableStr = MainTableStr & "COMBOBOX D=" & AlanAdi & " CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & Aciklama & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
		Elseif AlanTuru = "C" and trim(Grupkodu) <> "" Then
			MainTableStr = MainTableStr & "COMBOBOX D=" & AlanAdi & " CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & Aciklama & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
		Else
			MainTableStr = MainTableStr & "EDITTEXT D=" & AlanAdi & " CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & Aciklama & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
		End if
	Next
	GetMainTableStr = MainTableStr
End Function
Sub MsgBoxToTxt(MesajBaslik, Mesaj)
	Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
	tmpFolder = fso.GetSpecialFolder(2)
	dosyaAdi = tmpFolder & "\Hata.txt"
	Set HataFile = Fso.OpenTextFile(dosyaAdi, 2, True)
	HataFile.WriteLine(MesajBaslik & " : " & vbcrlf & Mesaj)
	HataFile.Close
	Set wshShell = CreateObject("WSCript.shell")
	wshshell.run "notepad " & dosyaAdi, 1, True
	set wshshell = nothing
End Sub
Sub AlanlariDoldur(AnaTablo, MainTableT)
	Set S29T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK29T")
	MainTableT.Empty
	Alanlar = MainTableT.GetFieldNames("")
	For i = 1 to AnaTablo.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MainTableT.AddRow()
		For each Alan in Alanlar
			if InStr(1,Alan, "UDEFF__") = 0 Then
				AlanDegeri = S29T.GetFieldValue(Alan, i)
			Else
				AlanAdiIndex = GetAlanAdiIndex(Mid(Alan, 8), "STOK29T")
				KTA_Alan = AlanAdiIndex(0)
				KTA_Index = AlanAdiIndex(1)

				AlanDegeri = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK29T", S29T.ARTNO(i), KTA_Alan, KTA_Index)
			End if
			MainTableT.SetFieldValue Alan, satirNo, AlanDegeri
		Next
	Next
End Sub
Sub EtiketleriHesapla()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET2")
	MTableT2.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	MTableT2.Empty
	Alanlar = MTableT.GetFieldNames("")
	For i = 1 to MTableT.GetRowCount()
		EtiketIciMiktar = MTableT.EDEFF__ETK_ICI_MK(i)
		if EtiketIciMiktar > 0 Then
			EtiketSayisi = Doc.Ceil(Doc.Div(MTableT.SF_MIKTAR(i), EtiketIciMiktar))
		Else
			EtiketSayisi = MTableT.EDEFF__ETK_MIK(i)
			EtiketIciMiktar = Round(Doc.Div(MTableT.SF_MIKTAR(i), EtiketSayisi), 3)
		End if
		Kalan = MTableT.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		if EtiketSayisi > 0 Then
			For j = 1 to EtiketSayisi
				satirNo = MTableT2.AddRow
				For each AlanAdi in Alanlar
					AlanDegeri = MTableT.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi, i)
					MTableT2.SetFieldValue AlanAdi, satirNo, AlanDegeri
				Next
				if Kalan < EtiketIciMiktar Then EtiketIciMiktar = Kalan

				MTableT2.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = EtiketIciMiktar
				MTableT2.EDEFF__ETIKET_NO(satirNo) = Doc.Sayac0danAl("SERINO")
				Kalan = Kalan - EtiketIciMiktar
			Next
		End if
	Next
	MTableT2.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub
Function GetAlanAdiIndex(AlanAdi, KTA_Tablo)
	AlanAdi = trim(AlanAdi)
	Dim AlanArr(1)
	AlanArr(0) = AlanAdi
	AlanArr(1) = 0

	GetAlanAdiIndex = AlanArr
	tire = Instr(1,AlanAdi, "_")
	If tire = 0 Then Exit Function

	If Right(AlanAdi,1) =  "_" Then Exit Function
	If Right(AlanAdi,1) < "0" or Right(AlanAdi,1) > "9" Then Exit Function

	Set U10T = Doc.Select1("dd3333", "UFLD10T", "EVRAKNO='STOK29T'&VARCODE='" & AlanAdi & "'", "", -1)
	if U10T.GetRowCount() > 0 and Len(AlanAdi) <= Len(U10T.VARCODE) Then Exit Function

	Uzunluk = Len(AlanAdi)
	if Uzunluk < 3 Then Exit Function
	For i = Uzunluk-1 to Uzunluk-3 step -1
		chr1 = Mid(AlanAdi, i, 1)
		if chr1 = "_" Then
			AlanArr(0) = Left(AlanAdi, i-1)
			AlanArr(1) = Doc.CDbl(Mid(AlanAdi, i+1))
		End if
	Next
	GetAlanAdiIndex = AlanArr
End Function
Function GetScreenWidthAndHeight()
	Dim ScrArr(1)
	ScrArr(0) = 0
	ScrArr(1) = 0

	GetScreenWidthAndHeight = ScrArr
	Exit Function

	Set IE = CreateObject("InternetExplorer.Application")
	IE.Visible = False
	IE.Navigate ("about:blank")
	Set Scr = IE.Document.parentWindow.screen
	Width = Scr.Width
	Height = Scr.Height
	AvailableWidth = Scr.availwidth
	AvailableHeight = Scr.availheight
	BitsPerPixel = Scr.colordepth

	ScrArr(0) = AvailableWidth
	ScrArr(1) = AvailableHeight

	Set Scr = Nothing
	IE.Quit
	Set IE = Nothing
	GetScreenWidthAndHeight = ScrArr
End Function

Sub Makro1()
	Dim MTable
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "EQ_KOD","CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "AMBCODE","CHAR",6,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('GDEF00')",""

	Dim MTableT
	Set MTableT=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET=STOK01(NOFILE)","IO")
	MTableT.Addfield2 "SELECTED", "CHAR", 1, 0, "L", "", ""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "STOK_MIKTAR1","DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "KOD_STOK00_IUNIT", "CHAR", 6, 0, "L", "", "STUNIT"
	MTableT.Addfield2 "AMBALAJ_ICI_MIKTAR","DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "EVRAKTYPE", "CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "IRSALIYENO", "CHAR",10,0,"R","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "TARIH","DATE",10,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "XIRSALIYENO", "CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "CARIHESAPCODE", "CHAR",16,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "CARIHESAPCODE_CARI00_AD", "CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "MPSNO", "CHAR",10,0,"R","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "MUSTERI", "CHAR",16,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "MUSTERI_CARI00_AD", "CHAR",50,0,"L","",""

	MTable.AMBCODE = "HM"

	Dim MTableT2
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.Select2("MAKROTABLET2", MTableT.GetAppName, "", "", -1)
	MTableT2.Addfield2 "BARKOD", "CHAR",30,0,"L","",""

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU1",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par

	MTableT.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub
Sub StokSuz()
	Set MTable = Doc.GettableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GettableObject("MAKROTABLET")

	QStr = "AMBCODE='" & MTable.AMBCODE & "'"
	if trim(MTable.EQ_KOD) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & "&KOD='" & MTable.EQ_KOD & "'"
	End if

	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("w3dede", "STOK01", QStr, "KOD+LOTNUMBER", -1)
	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	MTableT.Empty()

	Alanlar = S01.GetFieldNames("")

	MTableT.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	For i = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()
		IrsaliyeNo = ""
		TedarikciIrsNo = ""
		TedarikciCHKodu = ""
		IrsaliyeTarihi = ""
		EvrakTuru = ""

		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Onaylanmamýþlar getiriliyor : " & i & " / " & S01.GetRowCount()
		if trim(S01.LOTNUMBER(i)) <> "" Then
			QStr = "KOD='" & S01.KOD(i) & "'&LOTNUMBER='" & S01.LOTNUMBER(i) & "'"
			Set S29T = Doc.Select1("dde33", "STOK29T", QStr, "", -1)
			if S29T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
				IrsaliyeNo = S29T.EVRAKNO
				TedarikciIrsNo = S29T.XIRSALIYENO
				TedarikciCHKodu = S29T.CARIHESAPCODE
				IrsaliyeTarihi = S29T.TARIH
				EvrakTuru = "SI"
			Else
				QStr = "KOD='" & S01.KOD(i) & "'&LOTNUMBER='" & S01.LOTNUMBER(i) & "'"
				Set S68T = Doc.Select1("dde33", "STOK68T", QStr, "", -1)
				if S68T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
					IrsaliyeNo = S68T.EVRAKNO
					TedarikciIrsNo = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK68E", "XIRSALIYENO", S68T.EVRAKNO, "")
					TedarikciCHKodu = S68T.CHCODE
					IrsaliyeTarihi = S68T.TARIH
					EvrakTuru = "FG"
				End if
			End if
		End if
			satirNo = MTableT.AddRow
			MTableT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			For each alan in Alanlar
				alanDegeri = S01.GetFieldValue(Alan, i)
				if Alan <> "STOK_MIKTAR" Then
					MTableT.SetFieldValue Alan, satirNo, alanDegeri
				End if
			Next
			MTableT.STOK_MIKTAR1 = S01.STOK_MIKTAR(i)
			MTableT.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", MTableT.KOD, "")
			MTableT.EVRAKTYPE = EvrakTuru
			MTableT.IRSALIYENO = IrsaliyeNo
			MTableT.TARIH = Doc.StrToNorDate(IrsaliyeTarihi)
			MTableT.XIRSALIYENO = TedarikciIrsNo
			MTableT.CARIHESAPCODE = TedarikciCHKodu
			MTableT.CARIHESAPCODE_CARI00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", TedarikciCHKodu, "")
	Next
	MTableT.Sort("TARIH+EVRAKTYPE+IRSALIYENO")
	MTableT.SetCurrentRow(1)
	MTableT.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub
Sub EtiketHesapla()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET2")
	MTableT2.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	MTableT2.Empty
	Alanlar = MTableT.GetFieldNames("")
	For i = 1 to MTableT.GetRowCount()
		EtiketIciMiktar = MTableT.AMBALAJ_ICI_MIKTAR(i)
		if EtiketIciMiktar > 0 And Doc.Is_EY1(MTableT.SELECTED(i)) Then
			EtiketSayisi = Doc.Ceil(Doc.Div(MTableT.SF_MIKTAR(i), EtiketIciMiktar))

			Kalan = MTableT.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			if EtiketSayisi > 0 Then
				For j = 1 to EtiketSayisi
					satirNo = MTableT2.AddRow
					For each AlanAdi in Alanlar
						AlanDegeri = MTableT.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi, i)
						MTableT2.SetFieldValue AlanAdi, satirNo, AlanDegeri
					Next
					if Kalan < EtiketIciMiktar Then EtiketIciMiktar = Kalan
	
					MTableT2.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = EtiketIciMiktar
					MTableT2.BARKOD(satirNo) = Doc.Sayac0danAl("SERINO")
					Kalan = Kalan - EtiketIciMiktar
				Next
			End if
		End if
	Next
	MTableT2.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub
Sub EtiketleriBas()
	Doc.RunMacro0 "PFRM7S.ETK-HMD", "PrintLabel"
End Sub

----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : SNK10 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : ETIKET-3
Hangi Tablo için : STOK00
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Depo Mevcutlarýnýn Etiketlenmesi (Mamul)
Kod : 
Public 	sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, UstMargin, SolMargin, AltMargin, sagMargin
Sub Makro1()
	Dim MTable
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "EQ_KOD","CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "AMBCODE","CHAR",6,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('GDEF00')",""

	Dim MTableT
	Set MTableT=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET=STOK01(NOFILE)","IO")
	MTableT.Addfield2 "SELECTED", "CHAR", 1, 0, "L", "", ""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "STOK_MIKTAR1","DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "KOD_STOK00_IUNIT", "CHAR", 6, 0, "L", "", "STUNIT"
	MTableT.Addfield2 "AMBALAJ_ICI_MIKTAR","DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "EVRAKTYPE", "CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "IRSALIYENO", "CHAR",10,0,"R","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "TARIH","DATE",10,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "XIRSALIYENO", "CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "CARIHESAPCODE", "CHAR",16,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "CARIHESAPCODE_CARI00_AD", "CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "MPSNO", "CHAR",10,0,"R","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "MPSTM_TRNUM", "CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "URETIMFISNO", "CHAR",10,0,"R","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "MUSTERI", "CHAR",16,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "MUSTERI_CARI00_AD", "CHAR",50,0,"L","",""


	MTable.AMBCODE = "SVK"

	Dim MTableT2
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.Select2("MAKROTABLET2", MTableT.GetAppName, "", "", -1)
	MTableT2.Addfield2 "BARKOD", "CHAR",30,0,"L","",""

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU1",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par

	MTableT.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub
Sub StokSuz()
	Set MTable = Doc.GettableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GettableObject("MAKROTABLET")

	QStr = "AMBCODE='" & MTable.AMBCODE & "'"
	if trim(MTable.EQ_KOD) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & "&KOD='" & MTable.EQ_KOD & "'"
	End if

	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("w3dede", "STOK01", QStr, "KOD+LOTNUMBER", -1)
	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	MTableT.Empty()

	Alanlar = S01.GetFieldNames("")

	MTableT.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	For i = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()
		MPSTrNum = ""
		MPSNo = ""
		Musteri = ""
		Tarih = ""
		UretimFisNo = ""
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Liste getiriliyor : " & i & " / " & S01.GetRowCount()
		if trim(S01.LOTNUMBER(i)) <> "" Then
			QStr = "KOD='" & S01.KOD(i) & "'&LOTNUMBER='" & S01.LOTNUMBER(i) & "'"
			Set S20T = Doc.Select1("dde33", "STOK20T", QStr, "", -1)
			if S20T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
				MPSNo = S20T.MPSNO(1)
				MPSTRNum = S20T.MPSTM_TRNUM(1)
				Musteri = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10TM", "MUSTERIKODU", S20T.MPSNO & S20T.MPSTM_TRNUM, "EVRAKNO+TRNUM")
				Tarih = Doc.StrToNorDate(S20T.TARIH)
				UretimFisNo = S20T.EVRAKNO
			End if
		End if
		satirNo = MTableT.AddRow
		MTableT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		For each alan in Alanlar
			alanDegeri = S01.GetFieldValue(Alan, i)
			if Alan <> "STOK_MIKTAR" Then
				MTableT.SetFieldValue Alan, satirNo, alanDegeri
			End if
		Next
		MTableT.STOK_MIKTAR1 = S01.STOK_MIKTAR(i)
		MTableT.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", MTableT.KOD, "")
		MTableT.URETIMFISNO = UretimFisNo
		MTableT.MPSNO = MPSNo
		MTableT.MPSTM_TRNUM = MPSTrNum
		MTableT.TARIH = Tarih
		MTableT.MUSTERI = Musteri
		MTableT.MUSTERI_CARI00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", Musteri, "")
	Next
'	MTableT.Sort("TARIH+EVRAKTYPE+IRSALIYENO")
	MTableT.SetCurrentRow(1)
	MTableT.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub
Sub EtiketHesapla()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET2")
	MTableT2.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	MTableT2.Empty
	Alanlar = MTableT.GetFieldNames("")
	For i = 1 to MTableT.GetRowCount()
		EtiketIciMiktar = MTableT.AMBALAJ_ICI_MIKTAR(i)
		if EtiketIciMiktar > 0 And Doc.Is_EY1(MTableT.SELECTED(i)) Then
			EtiketSayisi = Doc.Ceil(Doc.Div(MTableT.SF_MIKTAR(i), EtiketIciMiktar))

			Kalan = MTableT.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			if EtiketSayisi > 0 Then
				For j = 1 to EtiketSayisi
					satirNo = MTableT2.AddRow
					For each AlanAdi in Alanlar
						AlanDegeri = MTableT.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi, i)
						MTableT2.SetFieldValue AlanAdi, satirNo, AlanDegeri
					Next
					if Kalan < EtiketIciMiktar Then EtiketIciMiktar = Kalan
	
					MTableT2.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = EtiketIciMiktar
					MTableT2.BARKOD(satirNo) = Doc.Sayac0danAl("SERINO")
					Kalan = Kalan - EtiketIciMiktar
				Next
			End if
		End if
	Next
	MTableT2.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub
Sub EtiketleriBas()
	Doc.RunMacro0 "PFRM7S.ETK-MAMUL", "PrintLabel"
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : SNK10 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : FL-STML
Hangi Tablo için : STOK48
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Fiyat listesini standart maliyet tablosuna aktarma
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
'	for i = 1 to LISTET.GetRowCount()
'		Doc.Load_Voucher LISTET.EVRAKNO(i)
		Call Makro2()
'	Next
End Sub
Sub Makro2()
	Endeks = "OT"
	if trim(Doc.GetGecoValue("STMTYP","KOD",Endeks,d7c_ErrMsgNo)) = "" then
		ret = GrupKoduYarat("STMTYP",Endeks,"Teklif Maliyet Endeksi")
	end if
	if ret <> 0 then
		k = Doc.d7MsgBox(Endeks & " Maliyet endeksi yaratýlamadý. Ýþlem kesilmiþtir.","dinamo",MB_OK)
		exit sub
	end if
	if trim(Doc.GetGecoValue("STMMUS","KOD","HM",d7c_ErrMsgNo)) = "" then
		ret = GrupKoduYarat("STMMUS","HM","Hammadde Maliyeti")
	end if
	if ret <> 0 then
		k = Doc.d7MsgBox("HM masraf unsuru yaratýlamadý. Ýþlem kesilmiþtir.","dinamo",MB_OK)
		exit sub
	end if

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STDMH0","")
	Set STDMH0E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STDMH0E")
	Set STDMH0T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STDMH0T")
	For i = 1 to STOK48T.GetRecCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Aktarýlýyor : " & i & " / " & STOK48T.GetRecCount()
		Tarih = STOK48T.KRI_TARIH_1(i)
		if trim(Tarih) = "" Then Tarih = STOK48E.REF_TARIH_1
		if trim(STOK48T.KOD(i))	<> "" then
			StokKodu = STOK48T.KOD(i)
			rows = Doc.SelectEQ("STDMH0E","KOD",StokKodu)
			if rows = 0 then
				ret = Doc1.New_Voucher()				
				STDMH0E.STDMALTYPE = Endeks
				STDMH0E.KOD = StokKodu
				STDMH0T.AddRow
				satirNo = STDMH0T.GetRecCount
			else
				set ST = Doc.GetTableObject("AU_STDMH0E_SELECT")
				For j = 1 to ST.GetRecCount()
					if trim(ST.STDMALTYPE(j)) = Endeks then
						EvrakNo = ST.EVRAKNO(j)
					end if
				Next
				if trim(EvrakNo) <> "" then
					ret = Doc1.Load_Voucher(EvrakNo)
					if ret <> 0 then
						k = Doc.d7MsgBox(trim(StokKodu) & ": Standart Maliyet tablosu yüklenemedi. Ýþlem kesilmiþtir.","dinamo",MB_OK)
						exit sub
					end if
					if STDMH0T.GetRowCount = 0 and Tarih > "2006/06/01" Then Tarih = "2006/06/01"
					if trim(Tarih) = "" Then Tarih = Doc.BuGun
					satirNo = satirBul(STDMH0T, Tarih)
					if satirNo = 0 then
						STDMH0T.AddRow
						satirNo = STDMH0T.GetRecCount
					End if
				else
					ret = Doc1.New_Voucher()				
					STDMH0E.STDMALTYPE = Endeks
					STDMH0E.KOD = StokKodu
					STDMH0T.AddRow
					if Tarih > "2006/06/01" Then Tarih = "2006/06/01"
					if trim(Tarih) = "" Then Tarih = Doc.BuGun
					satirNo = STDMH0T.GetRecCount
				end if
			end if
			Fiyat = STOK48T.PRICE(i)
			IslemBirimi = STOK48T.SF_UNIT(i)
			TemelBirim = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", STOK48T.KOD(i), "")
			TemelFiyat = Doc.Stok00_toUnit(STOK48T.KOD(i), TemelBirim, IslemBirimi, d7c_ErrMsgNo) * Fiyat
			STDMH0T.VALIDAFTERTARIH(satirNo) = Doc.StrToNorDate(Tarih)
			STDMH0T.UNSUR(satirNo) = "HM"
			STDMH0T.TUTAR(satirNo) = TemelFiyat
			STDMH0T.PARABIRIMI(satirNo) = STOK48T.PRICEUNIT(i)
			ret = Doc1.Save_Voucher()
			if ret <> 0 then
				k = Doc.d7MsgBox(trim(StokKodu) & ": Standart Maliyet tablosu kaydedilemedi. Ýþlem kesilmiþtir.","dinamo",MB_OK)
				ret = Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
				exit sub
			end if
			ret = Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
		end if
	Next
End Sub
Function GrupKoduYarat(GrupKoduTipi,GrupKodu,GrupAciklamasi)
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("GECO10","")
	Set GT = Doc1.GetTableObject("GECOUST")
	ret = Doc1.Load_Voucher(GrupKoduTipi)
	if ret <> 0 then
		GrupKoduYarat = 1
		ret = Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
		exit Function
	end if
	GT.AddRow
	satirNo = GT.GetRecCount
	GT.KOD(satirNo) = GrupKodu
	GT.AD(satirNo) = GrupAciklamasi
	ret = Doc1.Save_Voucher()
	if ret <> 0 then
		GrupKoduYarat = 1
	else
		GrupKoduYarat = 0
	end if
	ret = Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
End Function
Function satirBul(STDMH0T, Tarih)
	satirBul = 0
	For i = 1 to STDMH0T.GetRowCount()
		if STDMH0T.VALIDAFTERTARIH(i) = Tarih then
			satirBul = i
			exit function
		end if
	Next
End Function

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : SNK10 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : HARUN-0002
Hangi Tablo için : SFDC32
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : ENERJÝ RAPOR MAKRO **YENÝ
Kod : 
DIM S32E_REP, d(), SH2, WB, EXCEL, yenitarih, eskitarih, MaliYilbasi
dim cc, excelkolon
dim htotal(), gtotal(), c()
Const xlAutomatic = -4105
Const xlNone = -4142
Const xlContinuous = 1
Const xlThin = 2
Const xlDiagonalDown = 5
Const xlDiagonalUp = 6
Const xlEdgeLeft = 7
Const xlEdgeTop = 8
Const xlEdgeBottom = 9
Const xlEdgeRight = 10
Const xlInsideVertical = 11
Const xlInsideHorizontal = 12
Const xlGeneral = 1
Const xlBottom = -4107 
Const xlContext = -5002

Sub makro1()
	Set s = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	excelkolon = 1
	cc = 0
	c2 = 0
	redim c(35)
	redim d(35)
	redim htotal(100)
	redim gtotal(100)
	Set Excel = CreateObject("excel.Application")
	Set Wb = Excel.Workbooks.Add	
	Set Sh2 = Wb.Sheets.Add
	Sh2.Name = REPLACE(LISTEE.RECTARIH11, "/", "")
	for x=1 to 35
		c(x) = 0
		d(x) = 0
	Next
	MaliYilbasi = GetMaliYilbasi()

'	Call kur_yazdir1(2)
'	Call kur_yazdir(4, "2009/12/08")

'	Excel.Visible = True
'	Exit Sub
	Basla = Timer
	Call data_yarat()
	Bitis = Timer
'	Doc.d7MsgBox "data_yarat : " & Bitis - Basla & " s", "", MB_OK
	
	S32E_REP.sort("RECTARIH1+MPSNO")
	yenitarih=LISTEE.RECTARIH11
	
	Basla = Timer
	call gecmis_hesapla()
	Bitis = Timer
'	Doc.d7MsgBox "gecmis_hesapla : " & Bitis - Basla & " s", "", MB_OK

	Basla = Timer
	call hafta_hesapla()
	Bitis = Timer
'	Doc.d7MsgBox "hafta_hesapla : " & Bitis - Basla & " s", "", MB_OK
	For g=1 to 33
		Sh2.Cells(g,1).Select
		excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	Next
	For g1=1 to 13
		Sh2.Cells(1,g1).Select
		excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	Next
	Call CizgiCiz("A1:L84")
	Sh2.Range("A1:L75").Select
	Sh2.Range("A1:L75").columnwidth=12.45
	Sh2.Range("A:A").Select
	Sh2.Range("A:A").columnwidth=28.43

	Call belirle("A1:L84")
	call Font_belirle()
	Call exceleyazdir_toplam(excelkolon)
	Excel.Visible = True

End Sub
Sub exceleyazdir(eskitarih, excelkolon)
	ff=excelkolon
	Sh2.Cells(1,ff)= eskitarih

	Sh2.Cells(2,1)= "TOTAL FINISHED PRODUCTIONS (KGS)"
	Sh2.Cells(2,ff)= Doc.numformat(c(2),12,2)

	Sh2.Cells(4,1)= "REWORK (KGS)"
	Sh2.Cells(4,ff)= Doc.numformat(c(4),12,2)

	Sh2.Cells(7,1)= "OPERATING HOURS (HRS)-LEISTRITZ"
	Sh2.Cells(7,ff)= Doc.numformat(c(7),12,2)

	Sh2.Cells(8,1)= "OPERATING HOURS (HRS)-ZSK32-K"
	Sh2.Cells(8,ff)= Doc.numformat(c(8),12,2)

	Sh2.Cells(9,1)= "OPERATING HOURS (HRS)-ZSK32-S"
	Sh2.Cells(9,ff)= Doc.numformat(c(9),12,2)

	Sh2.Cells(10,1)= "OPERATING HOURS (HRS)-MARIS"
	Sh2.Cells(10,ff)= Doc.numformat(c(10),12,2)

	Sh2.Cells(12,1)= "WORKING HOURS (HRS)-TOTAL"
	Sh2.Cells(12,ff)= Doc.numformat(c(12),12,2)

	Sh2.Cells(14,1)= "TOTAL NUMBER OF LAB BATCHES"
	Sh2.Cells(15,1)= "TOTAL QUANTITY OF LAB BATCHES (kg)"
	Sh2.Cells(16,1)= "TOTAL EXTRUSION QUANTITY (2,5 TIME PASSING)-(kg)"
	Sh2.Cells(17,1)= "TOTAL CLEANING MATERIAL QUANTITY FOR LAB BATCHES (kg)"

	Sh2.Cells(18,1)= "CLEANING MATERIALS (KGS)"
	Sh2.Cells(18,ff)= Doc.numformat(c(18),12,2)

	Sh2.Cells(19,1)= "NO OF LOTS (FINAL PRODUCTIONS ONLY) "
	Sh2.Cells(19,ff)= Doc.numformat(c(19),12,2)

	Sh2.Cells(21,1)= "NO OF LOTS (INCLUDING PRE-MIX)"
	Sh2.Cells(21,ff)= Doc.numformat(c(21),12,2)

	Sh2.Cells(23,1)= "POWER COMPANY ENERGY COUNTER IND.(KWH)(MAIN PANEL)"
	Sh2.Cells(23,ff)= Doc.numformat(c(23),12,2)

	Sh2.Cells(24,1)= "TOTAL ENERGY CONSUMPTION FROM POWER COMPANY(KWh)"
	Sh2.Cells(24,ff)= Doc.numformat(c(24),12,2)

	Sh2.Cells(3,1)= "TOTAL FINISHED PRODUCTIONS AND CLEANING (KGS)"
	Sh2.Cells(3,ff)= doc.numformat(Sh2.Cells(2,ff) + Sh2.Cells(18,ff) + Sh2.Cells(16,ff) + Sh2.Cells(17,ff),12,2)

	Sh2.Cells(5,1)= "REWORK (%)"
	Sh2.Cells(5,ff)= doc.numformat(Doc.Div(Sh2.Cells(4,ff), Sh2.Cells(2,ff))*100,12,2)

	
	Sh2.Cells(6,1)= "MONO MATERIALS USED IN  PRODUCTIONS (KGS)"
	Sh2.Cells(6,ff)= doc.numformat(Sh2.Cells(2,ff) - Sh2.Cells(4,ff),12,2)


	Sh2.Cells(11,1)= "OPERATING HOURS (HRS)-TOTAL"
	Sh2.Cells(11,ff)= doc.numformat(Sh2.Cells(7,ff) + Sh2.Cells(8,ff) + Sh2.Cells(9,ff) + Sh2.Cells(10,ff),12,2)

	Sh2.Cells(13,1)= "USING CAPACITY OF LINES  (%)"
	Sh2.Cells(13,ff)= doc.numformat(Doc.Div(Sh2.Cells(11,ff), Sh2.Cells(12,ff))*100,12,2)

	Sh2.Cells(20,1)= "CLEANING MATERIALS (FOR FINAL PRODUCTS) (KGS/LOT)"
	Sh2.Cells(20,ff)= doc.numformat(Doc.Div(Sh2.Cells(18,ff), Sh2.Cells(19,ff)),12,2)

	Sh2.Cells(22,1)= "CLEANING MATERIALS (INCLUDING PRE-MIX) (KGS/LOT)"
	Sh2.Cells(22,ff)= doc.numformat(Doc.Div(Sh2.Cells(18,ff), Sh2.Cells(21,ff)),12,2)

	Sh2.Cells(25,1)= "ENERGY CONSUMPTION (KWh/kg)"
	Sh2.Cells(25,ff)= doc.numformat(Doc.Div(Sh2.Cells(24,ff), Sh2.Cells(3,ff)),12,2)

	if ff=2 then
		Call kur_yazdir1(ff)
		cc=0
	else
		Call kur_yazdir(ff, eskitarih)
		ss=ss+1
	end if
End Sub
Sub Exceleyazdir_toplam(excelkolon)
	excelkolon = excelkolon + 1
	Sh2.Cells(1, excelkolon) = "CUMMULATIVE OF THIS WEEK"
	Sh2.Cells(1, excelkolon+1) = "GENERAL CUMMULATIVE"

	for c1=2 to 90
		for c2=2 to excelkolon
			if trim(Sh2.cells(c1, c2)) <> "" then
				GTotal(c1) = GTotal(c1) + Sh2.cells(c1, c2) 
			end if
		Next
		for c2=3 to excelkolon
			if trim(Sh2.cells(c1, c2)) <> "" then
				hTotal(c1) = hTotal(c1) + Sh2.cells(c1, c2) 
			end if
		Next
		
		Sh2.Cells(c1,excelkolon)= doc.numformat(hTotal(c1),12,2)
		Sh2.Cells(c1,excelkolon+1)= doc.numformat(gTotal(c1),12,2)
	Next

	Sh2.Cells(5,excelkolon)= "%"&doc.numformat(Doc.Div(Sh2.Cells(4,excelkolon),Sh2.Cells(2,excelkolon) )*100,12,2)
	Sh2.Cells(5,excelkolon+1)= "%"&doc.numformat(Doc.Div(Sh2.Cells(4,excelkolon+1),Sh2.Cells(2,excelkolon+1) )*100,12,2)
	Sh2.Cells(13,excelkolon)= "%"&doc.numformat(Doc.Div(Sh2.Cells(11,excelkolon),Sh2.Cells(12,excelkolon) )*100,12,2)
	Sh2.Cells(13,excelkolon+1)= "%"&doc.numformat(Doc.Div(Sh2.Cells(11,excelkolon+1),Sh2.Cells(12,excelkolon+1))*100,12,2)

	Sh2.Cells(20,excelkolon)= doc.numformat(Doc.Div(Sh2.Cells(18,excelkolon),Sh2.Cells(19,excelkolon) ),12,2)
	Sh2.Cells(20,excelkolon+1)= doc.numformat(Doc.Div(Sh2.Cells(18,excelkolon+1),Sh2.Cells(19,excelkolon+1) ),12,2)

	Sh2.Cells(22,excelkolon)= doc.numformat(Doc.Div(Sh2.Cells(18,excelkolon),Sh2.Cells(21,excelkolon) ),12,2)
	Sh2.Cells(22,excelkolon+1)= doc.numformat(Doc.Div(Sh2.Cells(18,excelkolon+1),Sh2.Cells(21,excelkolon+1) ),12,2)

	Sh2.Cells(25,excelkolon)= doc.numformat(Doc.Div(Sh2.Cells(24,excelkolon),Sh2.Cells(3,excelkolon) ),12,2)
	Sh2.Cells(25,excelkolon+1)= doc.numformat(Doc.Div(Sh2.Cells(24,excelkolon+1),Sh2.Cells(3,excelkolon+1) ),12,2)
End Sub
Sub CizgiCiz(aralik)
	Sh2.Range(aralik).Select
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlDiagonalDown).LineStyle = xlNone
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlDiagonalUp).LineStyle = xlNone
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeLeft).LineStyle = xlContinuous
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeLeft).Weight = xlThin
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeLeft).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic

	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeTop).LineStyle = xlContinuous
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeTop).Weight = xlThin
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeTop).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic

	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeBottom).LineStyle = xlContinuous
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeBottom).Weight = xlThin
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeBottom).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic

	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeRight).LineStyle = xlContinuous
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeRight).Weight = xlThin
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlEdgeRight).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic

	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlInsideVertical).LineStyle = xlContinuous
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlInsideVertical).Weight = xlThin
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlInsideVertical).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic

	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlInsideHorizontal).LineStyle = xlContinuous
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlInsideHorizontal).Weight = xlThin
	Excel.Selection.Borders(xlInsideHorizontal).ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
End Sub
Sub Font_belirle()
	excel.Selection.Font.Name = "Arial"
  	excel.Selection.Font.Size = 9	
End Sub
Sub Belirle(aralik)
	Sh2.Range(aralik).Select

	Excel.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = xlGeneral
	Excel.Selection.VerticalAlignment = xlBottom
	Excel.Selection.WrapText = True
	Excel.Selection.Orientation = 0
	Excel.Selection.AddIndent = False
	Excel.Selection.IndentLevel = 0
	Excel.Selection.ShrinkToFit = False
	Excel.Selection.ReadingOrder = xlContext
	Excel.Selection.MergeCells = False
End Sub
Sub Kur_yazdir(xCol,Eskitarih)
	Set grup1 = Doc.Select1("_gecust","GECOUST","EVRAKNO='*PUNIT'&AD='X'","",-1)
	For i=1 to grup1.GetRowCount()
		kur = grup1.KOD(i)

		sh2.cells(26+(i-1)*2, 1)= kur &"- COUNTER INDICATION "
		Kriter = "CODEFROM='"& kur & "'&EVRAKNOTARIH='" & eskitarih & "'"
		Set EXC1=Doc.Select1("___EXCRATTccc","EXCRATT",Kriter,"",-1)
		For h2=1 to exc1.GetRecCount()
			Sh2.Cells(26+(i-1)*2, xCol)=exc1.kurs_1(h2)
			tt = Doc.StrDate_Increment(eskitarih, 1)
			Kriter1= "CODEFROM='" & kur & "'&EVRAKNOTARIH='" & tt &"'"
			Set EXC11=Doc.Select1("___EXCRATTnnnn1","EXCRATT",Kriter1,"",-1)

			For h21=1 to exc11.getreccount
				sh2.cells(27+(i-1)*2, xCol)=exc11.kurs_1(h21)-exc1.kurs_1(h2)
			Next
		Next
	Next
End Sub
Sub Kur_yazdir_(ff,Eskitarih)
	Set grup1 = Doc.Select1("_gecust","GECOUST","EVRAKNO='*PUNIT'&AD='X'","",-1)
	For i=1 to grup1.GetRowCount()
		kur = grup1.KOD(i)
		n=n+1
		sh2.cells(26+(n-1)*2, 1) =  kur &"- COUNTER INDICATION "
		Kriter = Kriter & "CODEFROM='"& kur &"'&EVRAKNOTARIH='" & eskitarih & "'"
		Set EXC1=Doc.Select1("___EXCRATTccc","EXCRATT",Kriter,"",-1)

		For j=1 to exc1.GetRowCount()
			sh2.cells(26+(n-1)*2, ff) = exc1.KURS_1(j)

			tt = Doc.StrDate_Increment(eskitarih, 1)
			Kriter1=Kriter1&"CODEFROM='"&kur &"'&EVRAKNOTARIH='"& tt &"'"
			Set EXC11=Doc.Select1("___EXCRATTnnnn1","EXCRATT",Kriter1,"",-1)
			For k=1 to exc11.GetRowCount()
				sh2.cells(27+(n-1)*2, ff) = exc11.KURS_1(k) - exc1.KURS_1(j)
			Next
		Next
	Next
End Sub
Sub Kur_yazdir1(ff)
	BaslangicTarihi = "2009/10/01"
	if MaliYilBasi > BaslangicTarihi Then BaslangicTarihi = MaliYilBasi
	BitisTarihi = Doc.StrDate_Increment(LISTEE.RECTARIH11, -1)

	Set Grup1 = Doc.Select1("_gecust","GECOUST","EVRAKNO='*PUNIT'&AD='X'","",-1)
	For h1=1 to Grup1.getreccount
		kur = Grup1.KOD(h1)
		cc = cc+1
		if BitisTarihi <= BaslangicTarihi Then
			ToplamSarfiyat = 0
		Else
			BaslangicSayac = Doc.GetKur(Kur, "SAAT", BaslangicTarihi, "", -1)
			BitisSayac = Doc.GetKur(Kur, "SAAT", BitisTarihi, "", -1)
			ToplamSarfiyat = BitisSayac - BaslangicSayac
		End if
		Sh2.cells(27 + (cc-1) * 2, 1) = kur & "- ENERGY (kWh) "
		Sh2.cells(27 + (cc-1) * 2, ff) = ToplamSarfiyat
	Next
End Sub
Sub Kur_yazdir11(ff)
	Set grup1 = Doc.Select1("_gecust","GECOUST","EVRAKNO='"+"*PUNIT"+"'","",-1)
	for h1=1 to grup1.getreccount
		kur = grup1.KOD(h1)
		ad = grup1.AD(h1)
		if trim(ad)="X" then
			cc=cc+1
			Dim Kriter
				Kriter=""
				Kriter=Kriter&"CODEFROM='"&kur &"'"
				Kriter=Kriter&" & "
				Kriter=Kriter&"EVRAKNOTARIH>='"&"2009/10/01" &"'"
				Kriter=Kriter&" & "
				Kriter=Kriter&"EVRAKNOTARIH<'"&LISTEE.RECTARIH11 &"'"

				Set EXC12=Doc.Select1("___EXCRATT2","EXCRATT",Kriter,"",-1)
				For h2=1 to exc12.getreccount
					
					t22=Doc.Date_NumToStr(doc.CDateToInt(exc12.EVRAKNOTARIH(h2)) + 1)
					t22=mid(t22, 7, 4)&"/"&mid(t22,4,2)&"/"&mid(t22,1,2)

					Dim Kriter1
					Kriter1=""
					Kriter1=Kriter1&"CODEFROM='"&kur &"'"
					Kriter1=Kriter1&" & "
					Kriter1=Kriter1&"EVRAKNOTARIH='"&t22 &"'"

					Set EXC123=Doc.Select1("___EXCRATT23","EXCRATT",Kriter1,"",-1)
					for h3 = 1 to exc123.getreccount
						topla = topla + (exc123.KURS_1(h3) - exc12.KURS_1(h2))
					next
				Next
				sh2.cells(27+(cc-1)*2, 1)=kur&"- ENERGY (KWh) "
				sh2.cells(27+(cc-1)*2, ff)=topla
			topla = 0
		End If			
	Next
End Sub
Sub Kur_yazdir2(ff)
	Set grup1 = Doc.Select1("_gecust","GECOUST","EVRAKNO='*PUNIT'&AD='X'","",-1)
	for i = 1 to grup1.GetRowCount()
		kur = grup1.KOD(i)

		cc=cc+1
		QStr = "CODEFROM='"& kur &"'&EVRAKNOTARIH>='2009/10/01'&EVRAKNOTARIH>='" & MaliYilbasi & "'&EVRAKNOTARIH<'"&LISTEE.RECTARIH11 & "'"
		Set EXC12=Doc.Select1("___EXCRATT2","EXCRATT",QStr,"",-1)
		For j = 1 to exc12.GetRowCount()
			ErtesiGun = Doc.StrDate_Increment(exc12.EVRAKNOTARIH(j), 1)
			ErtesiGunKur = Doc.GetKur(Kur, "SAAT", ErtesiGun, "", -1)
			Topla = Topla + ErtesiGunKur - exc12.KURS_1(j)

'			Kriter1="CODEFROM='" & kur & "'&EVRAKNOTARIH='"& t22 &"'"
'			Set EXC123=Doc.Select1("___EXCRATT23","EXCRATT",Kriter1,"",-1)
'			for k = 1 to exc123.GetRowCount()
'				topla = topla + (exc123.KURS_1(k) - exc12.KURS_1(j))
'			next
		Next
		sh2.cells(27+(cc-1)*2, 1) = kur&"- ENERGY (KWh) "
		sh2.cells(27+(cc-1)*2, ff) = topla
		topla = 0
	Next
End Sub
Sub Data_yarat()
	Doc.CreateTableObject "ABCXYZ=SFDC32E","TMPCOPY"
	Set S32E_REP = Doc.GetTableObject("ABCXYZ")
	yenitarih = LISTEE.RECTARIH11

	Set S32E = Doc.Select1("___tABLE10XXX","SFDC32E","RECTARIH1>='" & MaliYilbasi & "'","",-1)

	for i = 1 to S32E.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Data_yarat : " & i & " / " & S32E.GetRowCount()
		t = 0
		sorgu = 0
	
		t0 = Doc.StrDate_Increment(S32E.RECTARIH1(i), -1)
		t1 = Doc.StrDate_Increment(S32E.RECTARIH1(i), 1)
		t2 = Doc.StrDate_Increment(S32E.RECTARIH1(i), 2)
		t3 = Doc.StrDate_Increment(S32E.RECTARIH1(i),3)
		if S32E.RECTIME1(i) < "09:00" then
			satirNo = S32E_REP.AddRow()
			S32E_REP.EVRAKNO(satirNo) = S32E.EVRAKNO(i)
			S32E_REP.MPSNO(satirNo) = S32E.MPSNO(i)
			S32E_REP.RECTYPE(satirNo) = S32E.RECTYPE(i)
			S32E_REP.X_T_ISMERKEZI(satirNo) = S32E.X_T_ISMERKEZI(i)
			S32E_REP.RECTARIH1(satirNo) = t0
			S32E_REP.RECTIME1(satirNo) = S32E.RECTIME1(i)
			if S32E.RECTIME2(i) < "09:00" and S32E.RECTARIH2(i) = S32E.RECTARIH1(i) then
				sorgu = sorgu+1
				S32E_REP.RECTARIH2(satirNo) = t0
				S32E_REP.RECTIME2(satirNo) = S32E.RECTIME2(i)
			Else
				S32E_REP.RECTARIH2(satirNo) = t0
				S32E_REP.RECTIME2(satirNo) = "09:00"
				S32E.RECTIME1(i) = "09:00"
			End if
			S32E_REP.SURE(satirNo) = S32E.SURE(i)
			T = t+doc.DTDTDif1(S32E.RECTARIH1(i),S32E.RECTIME1(i),S32E.RECTARIH1(i),"09:00")
		End if
		if sorgu = 0 then
			fark1 = doc.DTDTDif1(S32E.RECTARIH1(i),S32E.RECTIME1(i),t1,"09:00")
			t = t+fark1
			IF t >= S32E.SURE(i) THEN
				satirNo = S32E_REP.AddRow
				S32E_REP.EVRAKNO(satirNo) = S32E.EVRAKNO(i)
				S32E_REP.MPSNO(satirNo) = S32E.MPSNO(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTYPE(satirNo) = S32E.RECTYPE(i)
				S32E_REP.X_T_ISMERKEZI(satirNo) = S32E.X_T_ISMERKEZI(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTARIH1(satirNo) = S32E.RECTARIH1(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTIME1(satirNo) = S32E.RECTIME1(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTARIH2(satirNo) = S32E.RECTARIH2(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTIME2(satirNo) = S32E.RECTIME2(i)
				S32E_REP.SURE(satirNo) = S32E.SURE(i)
				sorgu = sorgu+1
			Else
				satirNo = S32E_REP.AddRow
				S32E_REP.EVRAKNO(satirNo) = S32E.EVRAKNO(i)
				S32E_REP.MPSNO(satirNo) = S32E.MPSNO(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTYPE(satirNo) = S32E.RECTYPE(i)
				S32E_REP.X_T_ISMERKEZI(satirNo) = S32E.X_T_ISMERKEZI(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTARIH1(satirNo) = S32E.RECTARIH1(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTIME1(satirNo) = S32E.RECTIME1(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTARIH2(satirNo) = t1
				S32E_REP.RECTIME2(satirNo) = "09:00"
				S32E_REP.SURE(satirNo) = S32E.SURE(i)
				S32E.RECTARIH1(i) = t1
				S32E.RECTIME1(i) = "09:00"
			End if
		End if

		fark1 = doc.DTDTDif1(S32E.RECTARIH1(i),S32E.RECTIME1(i),t2,"09:00")
		t = t+fark1
		if sorgu = 0 then
			IF t >= S32E.SURE(i) THEN
				satirNo = S32E_REP.AddRow
				S32E_REP.EVRAKNO(satirNo) = S32E.EVRAKNO(i)
				S32E_REP.MPSNO(satirNo) = S32E.MPSNO(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTYPE(satirNo) = S32E.RECTYPE(i)
				S32E_REP.X_T_ISMERKEZI(satirNo) = S32E.X_T_ISMERKEZI(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTARIH1(satirNo) = S32E.RECTARIH1(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTIME1(satirNo) = S32E.RECTIME1(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTARIH2(satirNo) = S32E.RECTARIH2(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTIME2(satirNo) = S32E.RECTIME2(i)
				S32E_REP.SURE(satirNo) = S32E.SURE(i)
				sorgu = sorgu+1
			Else
				satirNo = S32E_REP.AddRow
				S32E_REP.EVRAKNO(satirNo) = S32E.EVRAKNO(i)
				S32E_REP.MPSNO(satirNo) = S32E.MPSNO(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTYPE(satirNo) = S32E.RECTYPE(i)
				S32E_REP.X_T_ISMERKEZI(satirNo) = S32E.X_T_ISMERKEZI(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTARIH1(satirNo) = S32E.RECTARIH1(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTIME1(satirNo) = S32E.RECTIME1(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTARIH2(satirNo) = t2
				S32E_REP.RECTIME2(satirNo) = "09:00"
				S32E_REP.SURE(satirNo) = S32E.SURE(i)
				S32E.RECTARIH1(i) = t2
				S32E.RECTIME1(i) = "09:00"
			End if
		End if

		fark1 = doc.DTDTDif1(S32E.RECTARIH1(i),S32E.RECTIME1(i),t3,"09:00")
		t = t+fark1
	
		if sorgu = 0 then
			IF t >= S32E.SURE(i) THEN
				satirNo = S32E_REP.AddRow
				S32E_REP.EVRAKNO(satirNo) = S32E.EVRAKNO(i)
				S32E_REP.MPSNO(satirNo) = S32E.MPSNO(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTYPE(satirNo) = S32E.RECTYPE(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTYPE(satirNo) = S32E.RECTYPE(i)
				S32E_REP.X_T_ISMERKEZI(satirNo) = S32E.X_T_ISMERKEZI(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTARIH1(satirNo) = S32E.RECTARIH1(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTIME1(satirNo) = S32E.RECTIME1(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTARIH2(satirNo) = S32E.RECTARIH2(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTIME2(satirNo) = S32E.RECTIME2(i)
				S32E_REP.SURE(satirNo) = S32E.SURE(i)
			Else
				satirNo = S32E_REP.AddRow
				S32E_REP.EVRAKNO(satirNo) = S32E.EVRAKNO(i)
				S32E_REP.MPSNO(satirNo) = S32E.MPSNO(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTYPE(satirNo) = S32E.RECTYPE(i)
				S32E_REP.X_T_ISMERKEZI(satirNo) = S32E.X_T_ISMERKEZI(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTARIH1(satirNo) = S32E.RECTARIH1(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTIME1(satirNo) = S32E.RECTIME1(i)
				S32E_REP.RECTARIH2(satirNo) = t3
				S32E_REP.RECTIME2(satirNo) = "09:00"
				S32E_REP.SURE(satirNo) = S32E.SURE(i)
				S32E.RECTARIH1(i) = t3
				S32E.RECTIME1(i) = "09:00"
			End if
		End if
	next
End Sub
Sub gecmis_hesapla()
	mpsno1=""
	eskit = ""
	QStr = "CODEFROM='KAAN'&EVRAKNOTARIH>='2009/10/01'&EVRAKNOTARIH>='" & MaliYilbasi & "'"
	Set EXC12=Doc.Select1("___EXCRAXTT2","EXCRATT", QStr,"EVRAKNOTARIH",1)
	C(24) = 0

	SonSayac = Doc.GetKur("KAAN", "SAAT", LISTEE.RECTARIH11, "", -1)
	IlkSayac = EXC12.KURS_1
	C(24) = (SonSayac - IlkSayac) * 120

	QStr = "RECTARIH1>='2009/10/01'&RECTARIH1>='" & MaliYilbasi & "'&RECTARIH1<'" & LISTEE.RECTARIH11 & "'"
	Set S32E_REP1 = Doc.Select2("vvf3e4666", S32E_REP.GetAppName, QStr, "", -1)
	For i=1 to S32E_REP1.GetRowCount()
		if Trim(eskit) <> trim(S32E_REP1.RECTARIH1(i)) then
			c(12) = c(12) + 96
			eskit= S32E_REP1.RECTARIH1(i)
		end if
		eskitarih="GENERAL CUMMULATIVE OF LAST WEEK"
		input=Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",S32E_REP1.EVRAKNO(i),"INPUT",0)-Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",S32E_REP1.EVRAKNO(i),"IADE",0)
		farkg=doc.DTDTDif1(S32E_REP1.RECTARIH1(i),S32E_REP1.RECTIME1(i), S32E_REP1.RECTARIH2(i),S32E_REP1.RECTIME2(i))
		sure = S32E_REP1.SURE(i)
		C(2)=C(2)+Doc.Div(input, sure)*farkg
		if trim(S32E_REP1.X_T_ISMERKEZI(i))<>"MARIS" and (trim(S32E_REP1.RECTYPE(i))="DS" or trim(S32E_REP1.RECTYPE(i))="R") then
			C(4)=C(4)+Doc.Div(input, S32E_REP1.SURE(i))*farkg
		end if
		mb1=Doc.Div(Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",S32E_REP1.EVRAKNO(i),"MB",0), sure) * farkg
		me1=Doc.Div(Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",S32E_REP1.EVRAKNO(i),"ME",0), sure) * farkg

		tezgah1=S32E_REP1.X_T_ISMERKEZI(i)
		temizlik = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",S32E_REP1.EVRAKNO(i),"PP",0)+Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",S32E_REP1.EVRAKNO(i),"PE",0)_
						+Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",S32E_REP1.EVRAKNO(i),"HDPE",0)+Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",S32E_REP1.EVRAKNO(i),"KIRMA",0)
		C(18)=C(18)+( Doc.Div(temizlik, sure)* farkg )
		select case trim(tezgah1)
			case "LEISTRITZ"
				C(7) = C(7) + mE1 - mB1
			case "ZSK32K"
				C(8) = C(8) + mE1 - mB1
			case "ZSK32S"
				C(9) = C(9) + mE1 - mB1
			case "MARIS"
				C(10) = C(10) + mE1 - mB1
		end select
		mpsno2=S32E_REP1.MPSNO(i)
		if trim(mpsno2)<>trim(mpsno1) then
			mpsno1=mpsno2
			C(19)=C(19)+1			
			C(21)=C(21) + 1
			if trim(S32E_REP1.RECTYPE(i))="P" Then
				C(21)=C(21) + 1
			end if
		end if
	next
	c(23) = 0
	excelkolon = excelkolon + 1
	call exceleyazdir(eskitarih, excelkolon)
End Sub
Sub hafta_hesapla()
	mpsno1=""
	for x=1 to 35
		c(x) = 0
		d(x) = 0
	Next
	MaliYilbasi = GetMaliYilBasi()
	QStr = "RECTARIH1>='" & LISTEE.RECTARIH11 & "'&RECTARIH1<='" & LISTEE.RECTARIH12 & _
				"'&RECTARIH1>='2009/10/01'&RECTARIH1>='" & MaliYilbasi & "'"
	Set S32E_REP1 = Doc.Select2("sf4te666", S32E_REP.GetAppName, QStr, "", -1)

	For v=1 to S32E_REP1.getreccount
		if S32E_REP1.RECTARIH1(v) <= LISTEE.RECTARIH12 and S32E_REP1.RECTARIH1(v) >= "2009/10/01" and S32E_REP1.RECTARIH1(v) >= LISTEE.RECTARIH11 then
			if trim(yenitarih)<>trim(S32E_REP1.RECTARIH1(v)) then
				mpsno1=""
				excelkolon=excelkolon +1
				eskitarih=yenitarih
				YENitarih=S32E_REP1.RECTARIH1(v)
				call exceleyazdir(eskitarih, excelkolon)
				for x=1 to 35
					c(x) = 0
					d(x) = 0
				Next
			end if
			input=Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",S32E_REP1.EVRAKNO(v),"INPUT",0)-Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",S32E_REP1.EVRAKNO(v),"IADE",0)
			farkg=doc.DTDTDif1(S32E_REP1.RECTARIH1(v),S32E_REP1.RECTIME1(v), S32E_REP1.RECTARIH2(v),S32E_REP1.RECTIME2(v))
			sure = S32E_REP1.SURE(v)
			C(2)=C(2)+doc.div(input, sure)*farkg
			if trim(S32E_REP1.X_T_ISMERKEZI(v))<>"MARIS" and ( trim(S32E_REP1.RECTYPE(v))="DS" or trim(S32E_REP1.RECTYPE(v))="R" ) then
				C(4)=C(4)+doc.div(input, sure)*farkg
			end if

			mb1=doc.div(Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",S32E_REP1.EVRAKNO(v),"MB",0), sure) * farkg
			me1=doc.div(Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",S32E_REP1.EVRAKNO(v),"ME",0), sure) * farkg

			tezgah1=S32E_REP1.X_T_ISMERKEZI(v)
			temizlik = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",S32E_REP1.EVRAKNO(v),"PP",0)+Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",S32E_REP1.EVRAKNO(v),"PE",0)_
						+Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",S32E_REP1.EVRAKNO(v),"HDPE",0)+Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",S32E_REP1.EVRAKNO(v),"KIRMA",0)
			C(18)=C(18)+ doc.div(temizlik, sure)* farkg
			select case trim(tezgah1)
						case "LEISTRITZ"
								C(7) = C(7) + mE1 - mB1
						case "ZSK32K"
								C(8) = C(8) + mE1 - mB1
						case "ZSK32S"
								C(9) = C(9) + mE1 - mB1
						case "MARIS"
								C(10) = C(10) + mE1 - mB1
			end select
			mpsno2=S32E_REP1.MPSNO(v)
				C(12) = 96

			if trim(mpsno2)<>trim(mpsno1) then
				mpsno1=mpsno2
				C(19)=C(19)+1			
				C(21)=C(21) + 1
				if trim(S32E_REP1.RECTYPE(v))="P" Then
					C(21)=C(21) + 1
				end if
			end if
		
			C(23) = Doc.GetKur("KAAN", "SAAT", S32E_REP1.rectarih1(v), "", -1)

			ErtesiGun = Doc.StrDate_Increment(S32E_REP1.RECTARIH1(v), 1)
			D(23) = Doc.GetKur("KAAN", "SAAT", ErtesiGun, "", -1)
			if d(23)> 0  and c(23) >0  then
				C(24) = (D(23) - C(23)) * 120
			end if
		end if
	next

	excelkolon = excelkolon + 1
	call exceleyazdir(yenitarih, excelkolon)
End Sub
Function GetUDFValue2(U02E, AlanAdi)
	GetUDFValue2 = ""
	if U02E.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function
	Set U2 = Doc.Select2("de44dddr", U02E.GetAppName, "QS_VARCODE='" & AlanAdi & "'", "", -1)
	if U2.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function

	if U2.QS_VARTYPE = "N" Then
		GetUDFValue2 = U2.QS_DVALUE
	Else
		GetUDFValue2 = trim(U2.QS_VALUE)
	End if
End Function
Function GetMaliYilBasi()
	MaliYilbasi = trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("ML_YILB"))
	MaliYilbasi = Right(MaliYilbasi, 4) & "/" & Mid(MaliYilbasi, 4, 2) & "/" & Left(MaliYilbasi, 2)
	GetMaliYilBasi = MaliYilbasi
End Function

Sub Makro1()
	For i = 1 to MMPS10T.GetRowCount()
		MMPS10T.R_HM_BAKIYE(i) = MMPS10T.R_MIKTART(i) - Abs(MMPS10T.R_HM_TRANSFER_EDILEN(i))
		MMPS10T.R_HM_TRANSFER_EDILEN(i) = 0
	Next

Bu Uygulama için Baþlangýç Ekran Formu :

Yeni Alt Ekra Form Kodu: 
Alt Ekran Açýlmadan Önce Çalýþtýrýlacak Makro : 


----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : SNK10 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : KODDUZELT1
Hangi Tablo için : PFRM7S
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Kod Düzelt
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Kod = PFRM7SE_B.SCRIPTTEXT
	KodArray = Split(Kod, vbcrlf)
	PFRM7SE_B.SCRIPTTEXT = ""
	Girinti = 0
	For i = 0 to uBound(KodArray)
		Doc.StatusBarMessage i & " / " & uBound(KodArray)
		satir = KodArray(i)
		satir = Replace(satir, Chr(9), " ")
		satir = trim(Satir)
				
		if uCase(Left(satir, 7)) = "ELSEIF " Then
			Girinti = Girinti - 1
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "ELSE" Then
			Girinti = Girinti - 1
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "END SUB" Then
			Girinti = 0
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "END FUNCTION" Then
			Girinti = 0
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "END IF" Then
			Girinti = Girinti - 1
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "NEXT" Then
			Girinti = Girinti - 1
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "WEND" Then
			Girinti = Girinti - 1
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "LOOP" Then
			Girinti = Girinti - 1
		Elseif uCase(Left(satir, 5)) = "CASE " Then
			Girinti = Girinti - 1
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "END SELECT" Then
			Girinti = Girinti - 2
		End if
		YeniSatir = Girintilisatir(satir, Girinti)
		
		PFRM7SE_B.SCRIPTTEXT = trim(PFRM7SE_B.SCRIPTTEXT) & YeniSatir & vbcrlf
		'  PFRM7SE_B.SCRIPTTEXT = YeniSatir
		
		if Ucase(Left(satir, 4)) = "SUB " Then
			Girinti = 1
		Elseif Ucase(Left(satir, 9)) = "FUNCTION " Then
			Girinti = 1
		Elseif uCase(Left(satir, 3)) = "IF " and Not TekSatirIf(satir) Then
			Girinti = Girinti  + 1
		Elseif uCase(Left(satir, 5)) = "CASE " Then
			Girinti = Girinti  + 1
		Elseif uCase(Left(satir, 12)) = "SELECT CASE " Then
			Girinti = Girinti  + 2
		Elseif uCase(Left(satir, 7)) = "ELSEIF " Then
			Girinti = Girinti  + 1
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "ELSE" Then
			Girinti = Girinti  + 1
		Elseif uCase(Left(satir, 4)) = "FOR " Then
			Girinti = Girinti  + 1
		Elseif uCase(Left(satir, 6)) = "WHILE " Then
			Girinti = Girinti  + 1
		Elseif uCase(Left(satir, 3)) = "DO " or trim(uCase(satir)) = "DO" Then
			Girinti = Girinti  + 1
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Function Girintilisatir(satir, Girinti)
	Girintilisatir = satir
	For i = 1 to Girinti
		Girintilisatir = Chr(9) & Girintilisatir
	Next
End Function
Function TekSatirIf(satir)
	TekSatirIf = False
	k = InStr(1, uCase(satir), "THEN ")
	if k = 0 Then Exit Function
	if trim(Mid(satir, k + 5)) <> "" Then
		TekSatirIf = True
	End if
End Function
Sub TabTrim(satir)
	While Left(satir, 1) = Chr(9)
		satir = Mid(satir, 2)
	Wend
	While Right(satir, 1) = Chr(9)
		satir = Left(satir, Len(satir) - 1)
	Wend
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : SNK10 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : MALIYET-01
Hangi Tablo için : 0STOK2
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : STOK AÇILIÞ FÝÞÝNE FÝYAT GETÝRME
Kod : 
Sub makro1()
	
	hedeffirma = "OZDEN5"
	firmalarindizini=Doc.Getd7Directory_DataRoot()
	hedeffirmadizini=firmalarindizini+"@"+hedeffirma

		Dim Tbl_STOK2AT
		Set Tbl_STOK2AT = Doc.GetTableObject("0stok2T")
		FOR i=1 TO Tbl_STOK2AT.GETRECCOUNT()
			kod = Tbl_STOK2AT.KOD(i)
			Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI2("DOCUMENT", "", HedefFirma)
			Set STOK10 = Doc1.Select1("ABCxx","STOK10A","KOD='"+kod+"'","",-1)
			for z=1 to  STOK10.getreccount
				tutar=tutar + STOK10.TL_TUTAR(z) + STOK10.TL_NONMIKTARTUTAR(z)
				tutardvz = tutardvz + STOK10.DVZ_TUTAR_ALT(z) + STOK10.DVZ_NONMIKTUTAR_ALT(z)
				miktar = miktar + STOK10.SF_MIKTAR(z)
			next
			Fiyat=doc.div(tutar, miktar)
			fiyatdvz=doc.div(tutardvz, miktar)
			tutar1=fiyat * Tbl_STOK2AT.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			tutar1dvz=fiyatdvz * Tbl_STOK2AT.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			kur = doc.div(fiyat, fiyatdvz)

			Tbl_STOK2AT.setcurrentrow(i)

			Tbl_STOK2AT.PUNIT_ORG(i)= "ECU"

			Tbl_STOK2AT.OR_FIYAT(i)=fiyatdvz
			'Tbl_STOK2AT.OR_TUTAR(i)=tutar1dvz
			'Tbl_STOK2AT.KUR(i)=kur

			Tbl_STOK2AT.TL_FIYAT(i)=fiyat
			'Tbl_STOK2AT.TL_TUTAR(i)=tutar1

			tutardvz=0
			tutar=0
			miktar=0
		NEXT


end sub

Sub Makro1()
	Dim MTable
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "ARANACAKDEYIM","CHAR",30,0,"L","",""
	MTable.ARANACAKDEYIM=""

	AnaTabloStr = "MMPS10T"
	Set AnaTablo = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10T")
	AnaTablo.SafePack()
	Dim MTableT
	Set MTableT=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET=STACK","IO")

	MTableT.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	Call AddAllFields(MTableT)
	Call AddUDEFFields(MTableT)

	Call AlanlariDoldur(AnaTablo, MTableT)
	MTableT.SetRowAID 0,0,0

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	EkranFormu = GetEkranFormu(AnaTabloStr, AnaTablo)
'	Call MsgBoxToTxt("", EkranFormu)
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU1",EkranFormu, Par
End Sub
Function GetEkranFormu(AnaTabloStr, AnaTablo)
'	EnBoy = GetScreenWidthAndHeight()
'	En = EnBoy(0)
'	Boy = EnBoy(1)
	En = 1000
	Boy = 400
	if En = 0 or Boy = 0 Then
		En = 1000
		Boy = 690
	Else
		En = En - 24
		Boy = Boy - 60
	End if 
	GridEn = En - 25
	GridBoy = Boy - 90

	FormStr = "FORM MAKROFORMU1 HEIGHT=" & Boy & "P WIDTH=" & En & "P SCROLLBARS=NO CAPTIONTYPE=SYS" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "SET CAPTION= " & Chr(34) & "Sipariþ Seviyeli MPS Yaratma" & Chr(34) & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "MENUDEF" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "   POPUP TEXT=" & Chr(34) & "Dosya" & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "   MENUITEM TEXT=" & Chr(34) & "Ýþlem Yapmadan Çý&kýþ" & Chr(34) & " ID=ID_APP_CANCEL KEY=ESCAPE" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "   ENDPOPUP" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDMENUDEF" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "LOCATE TOP=10p" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "//LTEXT LEFT=1 TOP=+0 T='Ünvanda Geçen Deyim : '" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "//EDITTEXT LEFT=E+10 TOP=+0 D=MAKROTABLE.ARANACAKDEYIM" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "GRID LEFT=1 TOP=+0 HEIGHT=" & GridBoy & "p WIDTH=" & GridEn & "p TABLE=MAKROTABLET HEADERROWHEIGHT=35P" & vbCrLf

	MainTableStr = GetMainTableStr()
	FormStr = FormStr & MainTableStr & vbCrLf

	UDFStr = GetUDFStr()
	FormStr = FormStr & UDFStr & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDGRID" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "BUTTON LEFT=3 TOP=E+7p WIDTH=20 HEIGHT=2 CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & "KTA 'larý Kaydet" & Chr(34) & " ID=" & Chr(34) & "MT_RUNMACRO_AFTERCALC('MAKR7S.@@@BUMAKRO@@@','KTA_Yaz')" & chr(34) & vbcrlf
	FormStr = FormStr & "BUTTON LEFT=E+2 TOP=+0 WIDTH=20 HEIGHT=2 CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & "Çýkýþ" & Chr(34) & " ID=ID_APP_CANCEL" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDFORM" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = Replace(FormStr, "@@@BUMAKRO@@@", SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME)

	GetEkranFormu = FormStr
End Function
Function GetUDFStr()
	UDFStr = ""
	Set U10T = Doc.Select1("dd333", "UFLD10T", "EVRAKNO='MMPS10T'", "", -1)
	For i = 1 to U10T.GetRowCount()
		AlanAdi = "UDEFF__" & trim(U10T.VARCODE(i))
		AlanIndex = U10T.VARINDEX(i)
		if trim(AlanIndex) <> "" Then
			AlanAdi = AlanAdi & "_" & trim(AlanIndex)
		End if
		Aciklama = trim(U10T.VARASPNAME(i))
		AlanTuru = trim(U10T.VARTYPE(i))
		Grupkodu = trim(U10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT3(i))
		if AlanTuru = "D" Then
			UDFStr = UDFStr & "COMBOBOX D=" & AlanAdi & " CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & Aciklama & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
		Elseif AlanTuru = "T" and trim(Grupkodu) <> "" Then
			UDFStr = UDFStr & "COMBOBOX D=" & AlanAdi & " CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & Aciklama & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
		Else
			UDFStr = UDFStr & "EDITTEXT D=" & AlanAdi & " CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & Aciklama & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
		End if
	Next
	GetUDFStr = UDFStr
End Function
Sub AddUDEFFields(MTableT)
	Set U10T = Doc.Select1("dd333", "UFLD10T", "EVRAKNO='MMPS10T'", "", -1)
	For i = 1 to U10T.GetRowCount()
		AlanAdi = "UDEFF__" & trim(U10T.VARCODE(i))
		AlanIndex = U10T.VARINDEX(i)
		if trim(AlanIndex) <> "" Then
			AlanAdi = AlanAdi & "_" & trim(AlanIndex)
		End if
		Aciklama = trim(U10T.VARASPNAME(i))
		AlanTuru = trim(U10T.VARTYPE(i))
		Grupkodu = trim(U10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT3(i))
		Tablo = trim(U10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT4(i))
		Uzunluk = U10T.VARLEN(i)
		Ondalik = U10T.VARSIG(i)

		if AlanTuru = "D" Then
			MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "DATE",10,0,"L","",""
		Elseif AlanTuru = "T" and trim(Grupkodu) <> "" Then
			MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "CHAR",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"L","",Grupkodu
		Elseif AlanTuru = "T" and trim(Tablo) <> "" Then
			MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "CHAR",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"L","F2=DO_BRW('" & Tablo & "')",""
		Elseif AlanTuru = "T" Then
			MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "CHAR",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"L","",""
		Elseif AlanTuru = "N" Then
			MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "DOUBLE",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"R","",""
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Sub AddAllFields(MTableT)
	Grid = Doc.GetGridColInfo("", "MMPS10T")
	For i = 0 to uBound(Grid, 1)
		AlanAdi = Grid(i,0)
		if DahilMi(AlanAdi) Then
			Aciklama = Grid(i,2)
			AlanTuru = Grid(i,4)
			Grupkodu = ""
			Tablo = ""
			Uzunluk = Grid(i,5)
			Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
			if AlanTuru = "D" Then
				MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "DATE",10,0,"L","",""
			Elseif AlanTuru = "C" and trim(Grupkodu) <> "" Then
				MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "CHAR",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"L","",Grupkodu
			Elseif AlanTuru = "C" and trim(Tablo) <> "" Then
				MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "CHAR",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"L","F2=DO_BRW('" & Tablo & "')",""
			Elseif AlanTuru = "C" Then
				MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "CHAR",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"L","",""
			Elseif AlanTuru = "N" Then
				MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "DOUBLE",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"R","",""
			End if
		End if
	Next
	MTableT.Addfield2 "EVRAKNO", "CHAR", 10,0,"R","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "TRNUM", "CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
End Sub
Function GetMainTableStr()
	MainTableStr = ""
	Grid = Doc.GetGridColInfo("", "MMPS10T")
	For i = 0 to uBound(Grid, 1)
		AlanAdi = Grid(i,0)
		if DahilMi(AlanAdi) Then
			Aciklama = Replace(Grid(i,2), "\n", " ")
			Aciklama = Replace(Aciklama, chr(13), " ")
			Aciklama = Replace(Aciklama, chr(10), " ")
			AlanTuru = Grid(i,4)
			Grupkodu = ""
			Tablo = ""
			if AlanTuru = "D" Then
				MainTableStr = MainTableStr & "COMBOBOX D=" & AlanAdi & " CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & Aciklama & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
			Elseif AlanTuru = "C" and trim(Grupkodu) <> "" Then
				MainTableStr = MainTableStr & "COMBOBOX D=" & AlanAdi & " CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & Aciklama & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
			Else
				MainTableStr = MainTableStr & "EDITTEXT D=" & AlanAdi & " CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & Aciklama & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
			End if
		End if
	Next
'	AlanAdi = "EVRAKNO"
'	Aciklama = "Evrak No"
'	MainTableStr = MainTableStr & "EDITTEXT D=" & AlanAdi & " CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & Aciklama & Chr(34) & vbCrLf

'	AlanAdi = "TRNUM"
'	Aciklama = "TRNUM"
'	MainTableStr = MainTableStr & "EDITTEXT D=" & AlanAdi & " CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & Aciklama & Chr(34) & vbCrLf

	GetMainTableStr = MainTableStr
End Function
Sub MsgBoxToTxt(MesajBaslik, Mesaj)
	Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
	tmpFolder = fso.GetSpecialFolder(2)
	dosyaAdi = tmpFolder & "\Hata.txt"
	Set HataFile = Fso.OpenTextFile(dosyaAdi, 2, True)
	HataFile.WriteLine(MesajBaslik & " : " & vbcrlf & Mesaj)
	HataFile.Close
	Set wshShell = CreateObject("WSCript.shell")
	wshshell.run "notepad " & dosyaAdi, 1, True
	set wshshell = nothing
End Sub
Sub AlanlariDoldur(AnaTablo, MainTableT)
	MainTableT.Empty
	Alanlar1 = MainTableT.GetFieldNames("")
	For i = 1 to AnaTablo.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MainTableT.AddRow()
		For each Alan in Alanlar1
			if InStr(1,Alan, "UDEFF__") > 0 or DahilMi(Alan) Then
				if InStr(1,Alan, "UDEFF__") = 0 Then
					AlanDegeri = MMPS10T.GetFieldValue(Alan, i)
				Else
					AlanAdiIndex = GetAlanAdiIndex(Mid(Alan, 8), "MMPS10T")
					KTA_Alan = AlanAdiIndex(0)
					KTA_Index = AlanAdiIndex(1)

					AlanDegeri = Doc.GetUDFValue("MMPS10T", MMPS10T.EVRAKNO(i) & MMPS10T.TRNUM(i), KTA_Alan, KTA_Index)
				End if
				MainTableT.SetFieldValue Alan, satirNo, AlanDegeri
			End if
		Next
	Next
End Sub
Sub KTA_Yaz()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	Set AnaTablo = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10T")
	Alanlar = MTableT.GetFieldNames("")
	For i = 1 to MTableT.GetRowCount()
		nret = Doc.UFLD00_CREATEEDITTABLE("T_U0001", "MMPS10T", MTableT.EVRAKNO(i) & MTableT.TRNUM(i))
		Set T_U1 = Doc.GetTableObject("T_U0001")
		For each Alan in Alanlar
			if InStr(1,Alan, "UDEFF__") > 0 Then
				KTA_Alan = Mid(Alan, 8)
				AlanDegeri = MTableT.GetFieldValue(Alan, i)
				K = T_U1.SetFieldValue(KTA_Alan,-1,AlanDegeri)
				T_U1.Exec "SAVE"
			End if
		Next 
	Next
End Sub
Function GetAlanAdiIndex(AlanAdi, KTA_Tablo)
	AlanAdi = trim(AlanAdi)
	Dim AlanArr(1)
	AlanArr(0) = AlanAdi
	AlanArr(1) = 0

	GetAlanAdiIndex = AlanArr
	tire = Instr(1,AlanAdi, "_")
	If tire = 0 Then Exit Function

	If Right(AlanAdi,1) =  "_" Then Exit Function
	If Right(AlanAdi,1) < "0" or Right(AlanAdi,1) > "9" Then Exit Function

	Set U10T = Doc.Select1("dd3333", "UFLD10T", "EVRAKNO='MMPS10T'&VARCODE='" & AlanAdi & "'", "", -1)
	if U10T.GetRowCount() > 0 and Len(AlanAdi) <= Len(U10T.VARCODE) Then Exit Function

	Uzunluk = Len(AlanAdi)
	if Uzunluk < 3 Then Exit Function
	For i = Uzunluk-1 to Uzunluk-3 step -1
		chr1 = Mid(AlanAdi, i, 1)
		if chr1 = "_" Then
			AlanArr(0) = Left(AlanAdi, i-1)
			AlanArr(1) = Doc.CDbl(Mid(AlanAdi, i+1))
		End if
	Next
	GetAlanAdiIndex = AlanArr
End Function
Function GetScreenWidthAndHeight()
	Dim ScrArr(1)
	ScrArr(0) = 0
	ScrArr(1) = 0

	Set IE = CreateObject("InternetExplorer.Application")
	IE.Visible = False
	IE.Navigate ("about:blank")
	Set Scr = IE.Document.parentWindow.screen
	Width = Scr.Width
	Height = Scr.Height
	AvailableWidth = Scr.availwidth
	AvailableHeight = Scr.availheight
	BitsPerPixel = Scr.colordepth

	ScrArr(0) = AvailableWidth
	ScrArr(1) = AvailableHeight

	Set Scr = Nothing
	IE.Quit
	Set IE = Nothing
	GetScreenWidthAndHeight = ScrArr
End Function
Function DahilMi(AlanAdi)
	Alanlar = Array("EVRAKNO", "TRNUM", "R_KAYNAKTYPE", "R_KAYNAKKODU", "R_MIKTAR0")
	sss = Filter(Alanlar, AlanAdi, True)
	if uBound(sss) <> -1 Then
		DahilMi = True
	Else
		DahilMi = False
	End if
End Function

Sub Makro1()
	for i=1 to LISTET.GetReccount()
		evrakno=LISTET.EVRAKNO(i)
		if Trim(evrakno)<>"" then
			retcode=Doc.Load_Voucher(evrakno)
				for t=1 to STOK40T.getreccount()
					STOK40T.ACIKKAPALI(t)="K"
				next
				nretcode = Doc.Save_Voucher()
		end if
	next
	Doc.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
End Sub
