Sub Makro1()
	Set MTableT=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("SIP_TABLE_XXX=STOK40E(NOFILE)","IO")
	MTableT.Setd7FieldEvent "EVRAKNO","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "EVRAKNO_AV"

	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "BARKOD","CHAR",30,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Setd7FieldEvent "BARKOD","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "BARKOD_AV"
End Sub
Sub SiparisSec()

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadMainForm_Dyn "SIPARIS_SEC",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
End Sub
Sub EVRAKNO_AV()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("SIP_TABLE_XXX")
	MTableT.MUSTERIKODU = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40E", "MUSTERIKODU", MTableT.EVRAKNO, "")
	MTableT.MUSTERIKODU_CARI00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", MTableT.MUSTERIKODU, "")
	MTableT.TARIH = Doc.BuGun
End Sub
Sub BARKOD_AV()
	Set STACK = Doc.GetTableObject("STACK")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")

	STOK01_PDA.D_ID2 = STOK01_PDA.BARKOD
	Doc.CallFieldAfterValidate_Row STOK01_PDA.GetAppName(),"D_ID2",STOK01_PDA.GetCurrentRow()	

	if STACK.PROPT = 1 Then
		Call SipBagla()
	End if
End Sub
Sub SipBagla()
	Set STOK60T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60T")
	Set SIP_LIST = Doc.GetTableObject("SIP_LIST")
	if Doc.IsBlank(SIP_LIST) Then Exit Sub

	Set SL = Doc.Select2("asdf3fsd", "SIP_LIST", "KOD='" & STOK60T.KOD & "'", "REV_RTESTARIH", -1)
	Set S60T = Doc.Select2G("saef32fsf", "STOK60T", "KOD='" & STOK60T.KOD & "'&SIPARTNO<>'" & Space(16) & "'", "", "KOD+SIPARTNO", -1)
	For i = 1 to S60T.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = SL.AddRow()
		SL.KOD(satirNo) = S60T.KOD(i)
		SL.ARTNO(satirNo) = S60T.SIPARTNO(i)
		SL.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = S60T.SF_MIKTAR(i) * -1
	Next
	SL.GroupBy2("ARTNO")
	For i = 1 to SL.GetRowCount()
		SL.SF_MIKTAR(i) = SL.SF_MIKTAR(i) - SL.SF_NETKAPANANMIK(i)
	Next

	Set SL1 = Doc.Select2("adf3wfdsdfd", SL.GetAppName, "SF_MIKTAR>0", "", -1)
	if SL1.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	Kalan = STOK60T.SF_MIKTAR
	For i = 1 to SL1.GetRowCount()
		if Kalan <= 0 Then Exit For
		if i > 1 Then
			ret = STOK60T.ExportRowToXML(STOK60T.GetCurrentRow())
			satirNo = STOK60T.AddRow
			STOK60T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			STOK60T.ImportRowFromXML satirNo, ret
		End if
		if SL1.SF_MIKTAR(i) >= Kalan or i = SL1.GetRowCount() Then 
			Eklenen = Kalan
		Else
			Eklenen = SL1.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		End if

		STOK60T.SIPARTNO = SL1.ARTNO(i)
		Doc.CallFieldAfterValidate_Row "STOK60T","SIPARTNO",STOK60T.GetCurrentRow()	

		STOK60T.SF_MIKTAR = Eklenen
		Doc.CallFieldAfterValidate_Row "STOK60T","SF_MIKTAR",STOK60T.GetCurrentRow()	

		Kalan = Kalan - Eklenen
	Next
End Sub
Sub SevkeBasla()
	Set SP = Doc.Select2("SIP_TABLE_XX", "SIP_TABLE_XXX", "EVRAKNO<>'" & Space(10) & "'", "", -1)
	if SP.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		k = Doc.d7MsgBox("Sipariþ Seçmediniz. Devam etmek ister misiniz", "", MB_YESNO)
		if k <> 6 Then Exit Sub
	End if

	Set SIP_LIST = Doc.Select1("SIP_LIST", "STOK40T", "EVRAKNO='xxsde34frt'", "", -1)
	For i = 1 to SP.GetRowCount()
		Set S40T = Doc.Select1("saff4f4fsdfsf", "STOK40T", "EVRAKNO='" & SP.EVRAKNO(i) & "'&ACIKKAPALI=' '", "", -1)
		if S40T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then SIP_LIST.AppendTable(S40T)
	Next
	Set STOK60E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60E")
	Set S60T1 = Doc.Select1G("sdf34fdfsd", "STOK60T", "EVRAKNO='" & STOK60E.EVRAKNO & "'", "", "KOD+SIPARTNO", -1)

	For i = 1 to S60T1.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = SIP_LIST.AddRow()
		SIP_LIST.EVRAKNO(satirNo) = S60T1.SIPNO(i)
		SIP_LIST.KOD(satirNo) = S60T1.KOD(i)
		SIP_LIST.ARTNO(satirNo) = S60T1.SIPARTNO(i)
		SIP_LIST.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = S60T1.SF_MIKTAR(i)
	Next
	Doc.RunCommand "CMD_LOAD_FORM"
End Sub
Sub GeriDon()
	Doc.RUN_APP_OK

	Set SIP_TABLE = Doc.GetTableObject("SIP_TABLE_XXX")
	Set ST = Doc.GetTableObject("SIP_TABLE_XX")
	SIP_TABLE.Empty
	For i = 1 to ST.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = SIP_TABLE.AddRow()
		SIP_TABLE.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		SIP_TABLE.EVRAKNO = ST.EVRAKNO(i)		
	Next
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : SNK10 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : TRACK601_2
Hangi Tablo için : 
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : 
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
End Sub
Sub SiparisSec()

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadMainForm_Dyn "SIPARIS_SEC",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
End Sub
Sub EVRAKNO_AV()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("SIP_TABLE_XXX")
	MTableT.MUSTERIKODU = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40E", "MUSTERIKODU", MTableT.EVRAKNO, "")
	MTableT.MUSTERIKODU_CARI00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", MTableT.MUSTERIKODU, "")
	MTableT.TARIH = Doc.BuGun
End Sub
Sub BARKOD_AV()
	Set STACK = Doc.GetTableObject("STACK")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")

	STOK01_PDA.D_ID2 = STOK01_PDA.BARKOD
	Doc.CallFieldAfterValidate_Row STOK01_PDA.GetAppName(),"D_ID2",STOK01_PDA.GetCurrentRow()	

	if STACK.PROPT = 1 Then
		Call SipBagla()
	End if
End Sub
Sub SipBagla()
	Set STOK60T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60T")
	Set SIP_LIST = Doc.GetTableObject("SIP_LIST")
	if Doc.IsBlank(SIP_LIST) Then Exit Sub

	Set SL = Doc.Select2("asdf3fsd", "SIP_LIST", "KOD='" & STOK60T.KOD & "'", "REV_RTESTARIH", -1)
	Set S60T = Doc.Select2G("saef32fsf", "STOK60T", "KOD='" & STOK60T.KOD & "'&SIPARTNO<>'" & Space(16) & "'", "", "KOD+SIPARTNO", -1)
	For i = 1 to S60T.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = SL.AddRow()
		SL.KOD(satirNo) = S60T.KOD(i)
		SL.ARTNO(satirNo) = S60T.SIPARTNO(i)
		SL.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = S60T.SF_MIKTAR(i) * -1
	Next
	SL.GroupBy2("ARTNO")
	For i = 1 to SL.GetRowCount()
		SL.SF_MIKTAR(i) = SL.SF_MIKTAR(i) - SL.SF_NETKAPANANMIK(i)
	Next

	Set SL1 = Doc.Select2("adf3wfdsdfd", SL.GetAppName, "SF_MIKTAR>0", "", -1)
	if SL1.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	Kalan = STOK60T.SF_MIKTAR
	For i = 1 to SL1.GetRowCount()
		if Kalan <= 0 Then Exit For
		if i > 1 Then
			ret = STOK60T.ExportRowToXML(STOK60T.GetCurrentRow())
			satirNo = STOK60T.AddRow
			STOK60T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			STOK60T.ImportRowFromXML satirNo, ret
		End if
		if SL1.SF_MIKTAR(i) >= Kalan or i = SL1.GetRowCount() Then 
			Eklenen = Kalan
		Else
			Eklenen = SL1.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		End if

		STOK60T.SIPARTNO = SL1.ARTNO(i)
		Doc.CallFieldAfterValidate_Row "STOK60T","SIPARTNO",STOK60T.GetCurrentRow()	

		STOK60T.SF_MIKTAR = Eklenen
		Doc.CallFieldAfterValidate_Row "STOK60T","SF_MIKTAR",STOK60T.GetCurrentRow()	

		Kalan = Kalan - Eklenen
	Next
End Sub
Sub SevkeBasla()
	Set SP = Doc.Select2("SIP_TABLE_XX", "SIP_TABLE_XXX", "EVRAKNO<>'" & Space(10) & "'", "", -1)
	if SP.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		k = Doc.d7MsgBox("Sipariþ Seçmediniz. Devam etmek ister misiniz", "", MB_YESNO)
		if k <> 6 Then Exit Sub
	End if

	Set SIP_LIST = Doc.Select1("SIP_LIST", "STOK40T", "EVRAKNO='xxsde34frt'", "", -1)
	For i = 1 to SP.GetRowCount()
		Set S40T = Doc.Select1("saff4f4fsdfsf", "STOK40T", "EVRAKNO='" & SP.EVRAKNO(i) & "'&ACIKKAPALI=' '", "", -1)
		if S40T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then SIP_LIST.AppendTable(S40T)
	Next
	Set STOK60E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60E")
	Set S60T1 = Doc.Select1G("sdf34fdfsd", "STOK60T", "EVRAKNO='" & STOK60E.EVRAKNO & "'", "", "KOD+SIPARTNO", -1)

	For i = 1 to S60T1.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = SIP_LIST.AddRow()
		SIP_LIST.EVRAKNO(satirNo) = S60T1.SIPNO(i)
		SIP_LIST.KOD(satirNo) = S60T1.KOD(i)
		SIP_LIST.ARTNO(satirNo) = S60T1.SIPARTNO(i)
		SIP_LIST.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = S60T1.SF_MIKTAR(i)
	Next
	Doc.RunCommand "CMD_LOAD_FORM"
End Sub
Sub GeriDon()
	Doc.RUN_APP_OK

	Set SIP_TABLE = Doc.GetTableObject("SIP_TABLE_XXX")
	Set ST = Doc.GetTableObject("SIP_TABLE_XX")
	SIP_TABLE.Empty
	For i = 1 to ST.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = SIP_TABLE.AddRow()
		SIP_TABLE.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		SIP_TABLE.EVRAKNO = ST.EVRAKNO(i)		
	Next
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : SNK10 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : TRACK711
Hangi Tablo için : 
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : 
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Set MTableT=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("SIP_TABLE_XXX=STOK40E(NOFILE)","IO")
	MTableT.Setd7FieldEvent "EVRAKNO","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "EVRAKNO_AV"

	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "BARKOD","CHAR",30,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Setd7FieldEvent "BARKOD","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "BARKOD_AV"
End Sub
Sub SiparisSec()

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadMainForm_Dyn "SIPARIS_SEC",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
End Sub
Sub EVRAKNO_AV()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("SIP_TABLE_XXX")
	MTableT.MUSTERIKODU = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40E", "MUSTERIKODU", MTableT.EVRAKNO, "")
	MTableT.MUSTERIKODU_CARI00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", MTableT.MUSTERIKODU, "")
	MTableT.TARIH = Doc.BuGun
End Sub
Sub BARKOD_AV()
	Set STACK = Doc.GetTableObject("STACK")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")

	STOK01_PDA.D_ID2 = STOK01_PDA.BARKOD
	Doc.CallFieldAfterValidate_Row STOK01_PDA.GetAppName(),"D_ID2",STOK01_PDA.GetCurrentRow()	

	if STACK.PROPT = 1 Then
		Call SipBagla()
	End if
End Sub
Sub SipBagla()
	Set STOK70T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK70T")
	Set SIP_LIST = Doc.GetTableObject("SIP_LIST")
	if Doc.IsBlank(SIP_LIST) Then Exit Sub

	Set SL = Doc.Select2("asdf3fsd", "SIP_LIST", "KOD='" & STOK70T.KOD & "'", "REV_RTESTARIH", -1)
	Set S70T = Doc.Select2G("saef32fsf", "STOK70T", "KOD='" & STOK70T.KOD & "'&SIPARTNO<>'" & Space(16) & "'", "", "KOD+S60_SIPARTNO", -1)
	For i = 1 to S70T.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = SL.AddRow()
		SL.KOD(satirNo) = S70T.KOD(i)
		SL.ARTNO(satirNo) = S70T.S60_SIPARTNO(i)
		SL.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = S70T.SF_MIKTAR(i) * -1
	Next
	SL.GroupBy2("ARTNO")
	For i = 1 to SL.GetRowCount()
		SL.SF_MIKTAR(i) = SL.SF_MIKTAR(i) - SL.SF_NETKAPANANMIK(i)
	Next

	Set SL1 = Doc.Select2("adf3wfdsdfd", SL.GetAppName, "SF_MIKTAR>0", "", -1)
	if SL1.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	Kalan = STOK70T.SF_MIKTAR
	For i = 1 to SL1.GetRowCount()
		if Kalan <= 0 Then Exit For
		if i > 1 Then
			ret = STOK70T.ExportRowToXML(STOK70T.GetCurrentRow())
			satirNo = STOK70T.AddRow
			STOK70T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			STOK70T.ImportRowFromXML satirNo, ret
		End if
		if SL1.SF_MIKTAR(i) >= Kalan or i = SL1.GetRowCount() Then 
			Eklenen = Kalan
		Else
			Eklenen = SL1.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		End if

		STOK70T.S60_SIPARTNO = SL1.ARTNO(i)
		Doc.CallFieldAfterValidate_Row "STOK70T","S60_SIPARTNO",STOK70T.GetCurrentRow()	

		STOK70T.SF_MIKTAR = Eklenen
		Doc.CallFieldAfterValidate_Row "STOK70T","SF_MIKTAR",STOK70T.GetCurrentRow()	

		Kalan = Kalan - Eklenen
	Next
End Sub
Sub SevkeBasla()
	Set SP = Doc.Select2("SIP_TABLE_XX", "SIP_TABLE_XXX", "EVRAKNO<>'" & Space(10) & "'", "", -1)
	if SP.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		k = Doc.d7MsgBox("Sipariþ Seçmediniz. Devam etmek ister misiniz", "", MB_YESNO)
		if k <> 6 Then Exit Sub
	End if

	Set SIP_LIST = Doc.Select1("SIP_LIST", "STOK40T", "EVRAKNO='xxsde34frt'", "", -1)
	For i = 1 to SP.GetRowCount()
		Set S40T = Doc.Select1("saff4f4fsdfsf", "STOK40T", "EVRAKNO='" & SP.EVRAKNO(i) & "'&ACIKKAPALI=' '", "", -1)
		if S40T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then SIP_LIST.AppendTable(S40T)
	Next
	Set STOK70E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK70E")
	Set S70T1 = Doc.Select1G("sdf34fdfsd", "STOK70T", "EVRAKNO='" & STOK70E.EVRAKNO & "'", "", "KOD+S60_SIPARTNO", -1)

	For i = 1 to S70T1.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = SIP_LIST.AddRow()
		SIP_LIST.EVRAKNO(satirNo) = S70T1.S60_SIPNO(i)
		SIP_LIST.KOD(satirNo) = S70T1.KOD(i)
		SIP_LIST.ARTNO(satirNo) = S70T1.SIPARTNO(i)
		SIP_LIST.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = S70T1.SF_MIKTAR(i)
	Next
	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadMainForm_Dyn "TRACK711X",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
End Sub
Sub GeriDon()
	Doc.RUN_APP_OK

	Set SIP_TABLE = Doc.GetTableObject("SIP_TABLE_XXX")
	Set ST = Doc.GetTableObject("SIP_TABLE_XX")
	SIP_TABLE.Empty
	For i = 1 to ST.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = SIP_TABLE.AddRow()
		SIP_TABLE.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		SIP_TABLE.EVRAKNO = ST.EVRAKNO(i)		
	Next
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
	Set Tfile = fso.OpenTextFile("c:\1.xml",2, True)
	Set S60T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60T")
	ret = S60T.ExportRowToXML(S60T.GetCurrentRow())
	satirNo = S60T.AddRow
	S60T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
	'msgbox satirNo & " , " & ret
	Tfile.WriteLine ret
	Tfile.Close
	S60T.ImportRowFromXML satirNo, ret
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	for i=1 to LISTET.GetReccount()
		evrakno=LISTET.KOD(i)
		if Trim(evrakno)<>"" then
			retcode=Doc.Load_Voucher(evrakno)
			if retcode=0 then
				retcode=Doc.Save_Voucher()			
			end if
		end if
	next
	Doc.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : SNK10 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : e
Hangi Tablo için : MMPS10
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : 
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Call PaletYarat()
End Sub
Sub PaletYarat()
	Barkod1 = InputBox("Paletteki Ýlk Barkodu Okutunuz")
	Barkod2 = InputBox("Paletteki Son Barkodu Okutunuz")

	if trim(Barkod1) = "" or trim(Barkod2) = "" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Baþlangýç ve Bitiþ barkodlarýný okutmalýsýnýz", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Set d7LB = Doc.Select1("we23fsdfsdf", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD>='" & Barkod1 & "'&KOD<='" & Barkod2 & "'", "", -1)

	if d7LB.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub

	if d7LB.GetRowCount() > 50 Then
		k = Doc.d7MsgBox("Verilen aralýkta " & d7LB.GetRowCount() & " adet torba var. Palet etiketi basmayý onaylýyor musunuz", "Uyarý", MB_YESNO)
		if k <> 6 Then Exit Sub
	End if

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSPL", "")
	Set d7LB1 = Doc1.GetTableObject("D7KIDSLB")
	Set d7PL1 = Doc1.GetTableObject("D7KIDSPL")

	Doc1.New_Voucher

	d7LB1.NOSUBLEVEL = "1"

	For i = 1 to d7LB.GetRowCount()
		if trim(d7LB.REFTEXT01(i)) <> "ELEKALTI" Then
			satirNo = d7PL1.AddRow()
			d7PL1.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			d7PL1.D_ID = D7LB.KOD(i)
		End if
	Next

	nret = Doc1.Save_Voucher()
	if nret = 0 Then
		Doc1.RunMacro0 "PFRM7S.ETK-MAMUL", "PrintLabel"
	End if
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu

Sub Makro1()
	BOMU01T.SafePack
	a = ToplamMiktarHatali
End Sub
Function ToplamMiktarHatali()
	ToplamMiktarHatali = False
	if BOMU01T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function

	Set B01 = Doc.Select2("de333", "BOMU01T", "BOMREC_INPUTTYPE='H'", "", -1)
	if B01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function

	MamulBirim = Doc.GetDBFieldvalue("STOK00", "IUNIT", BOMU01E.MAMULCODE, "")
	if UCase(trim(MamulBirim)) <> "KG" Then Exit Function

	ToplamHmd = 0
	For i = 1 to BOMU01T.GetRowCount()
		if BOMU01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE="H" Then
			HmdBirim = Doc.GetDBFieldvalue("STOK00", "IUNIT", BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i), "")
			if UCase(trim(HmdBirim)) <> "KG" Then Exit Function
			ToplamHmd = ToplamHmd + BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(i)

		End if
	Next

	if Doc.Round(ToplamHmd,6) <> Doc.Round(BOMU01E.MAMULMIKTAR,6) Then
		ToplamMiktarHatali = True	
		msgbox ToplamHmd & "," & BOMU01E.MAMULMIKTAR
	End if
End Function
